===== workbench_v2/app/service.py =====

"""Orchestration: refresh evidence -> detect change -> (maybe) call the model -> store a Review.

Cost control: no thesis-relevant change => zero LLM calls. Minor/material => ONE small call that
sees the prior structured thesis plus the delta only. The static profile is generated once.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import logging

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .ai import prompts
from .ai.provider import AIProviderError, BaseProvider
from .ai.schemas import BaselineOutput, UpdateOutput
from . import portfolio as _pf
from .core import signals
from .models import Company, Note, Review, Snapshot

VIEW_ORDER = {"cautious": 0, "neutral": 1, "constructive": 2}
BASELINE_MAX_TOKENS, UPDATE_MAX_TOKENS = 12000, 5000


log = logging.getLogger("uvicorn.error")


class ServiceError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, http: int = 400):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code, self.message, self.http = code, message, http


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def get_or_create_company(session: Session, ticker: str, name: str | None = None) -> Company:
    t = ticker.strip().upper()
    c = session.scalar(select(Company).where(Company.ticker == t))
    if c is None:
        c = Company(ticker=t, name=name or t)
        session.add(c)
        session.flush()
    elif name and c.name == c.ticker:
        c.name = name
    return c


def latest_review(session: Session, company_id: int, *, analysed_only: bool = False) -> Review | None:
    q = select(Review).where(Review.company_id == company_id)
    if analysed_only:
        q = q.where(Review.kind != "check")
    return session.scalar(q.order_by(Review.created_at.desc(), Review.id.desc()))


def _view_change(prev: str | None, new: str) -> str:
    if prev is None:
        return "new"
    a, b = VIEW_ORDER[prev], VIEW_ORDER[new]
    return "up" if b > a else "down" if b < a else "unchanged"


def apply_update(state: dict, out: dict) -> dict:
    s = copy.deepcopy(state)
    by_id = {a["id"]: a for a in s["assumptions"]}
    for u in out["assumption_updates"]:
        a = by_id.get(u["id"])
        if a:
            a["status"], a["last_reason"] = u["new_status"], u["reason"]
            if u["evidence"]:
                a["evidence"] = u["evidence"]
    nxt = max([int(a["id"][1:]) for a in s["assumptions"]] + [0])
    for n in out["new_assumptions"]:
        nxt += 1
        n = dict(n, id=f"a{nxt}")
        s["assumptions"].append(n)
    s["monitor"] = (s["monitor"] + out["monitor_adds"])[:6]
    if out.get("valuation_note"):
        s.setdefault("valuation", {})["implication"] = out["valuation_note"]
    return s


def _hash(parts: dict) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(parts, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()


def refresh(session: Session, ticker: str, *, adapter, provider: BaseProvider,
            language: str | None = None, force_rebaseline: bool = False,
            baseline_provider: BaseProvider | None = None) -> Review:
    ticker = _pf.norm_symbol(ticker)
    try:
        evidence, meta = adapter.get_equity_research_snapshot(ticker.strip().upper())
    except Exception as exc:   # OpenBBAdapterError and anything the provider throws
        raise ServiceError(getattr(exc, "code", "evidence_error"), str(getattr(exc, "message", exc))[:300], 502)

    evidence.setdefault("data_quality", {})["warnings"] = [str(w)[:200] for w in (meta.get("warnings") or [])][:6]
    has_numbers = any(isinstance(v, (int, float)) for sec in ("valuation", "financial_health") for v in (evidence.get(sec) or {}).values())
    if signals.price_of(evidence) is None or not has_numbers:
        raise ServiceError("ticker_not_found", f"No market data found for '{ticker.strip().upper()}'. Check the ticker symbol. Nothing was saved and no model call was made.", 404)

    if not evidence.get("recent_news"):                         # the OpenBB news route often returns nothing
        from .adapters.news_fallback import fetch_news
        evidence["recent_news"], note = fetch_news(ticker.strip().upper())
        evidence["data_quality"]["warnings"].append(note[:400])

    name = (evidence.get("company") or {}).get("name")
    company = get_or_create_company(session, ticker, name)
    if language:
        company.language = language
    lang = company.language

    snap = Snapshot(company_id=company.id, price=signals.price_of(evidence),
                    fingerprint=signals.fingerprint(evidence), payload=evidence)
    session.add(snap)
    session.flush()

    anchor = latest_review(session, company.id, analysed_only=True)
    last = latest_review(session, company.id)
    anchor_ev = session.get(Snapshot, anchor.snapshot_id).payload if anchor else None
    delta = signals.compute_delta(evidence, anchor_ev)
    level, reason = signals.classify(delta)
    kind = ("baseline" if anchor is None else "rebaseline" if force_rebaseline else
            "update" if level != "none" else "check")

    # ---- no thesis-relevant change: free, deterministic heartbeat ----
    if kind == "check":
        if last.kind == "check":                       # collapse repeated heartbeats
            last.snapshot_id, last.price, last.created_at = snap.id, snap.price, _now()
            last.delta, last.level_reason = delta, reason
            session.commit()
            return last
        r = Review(company_id=company.id, snapshot_id=snap.id, prev_review_id=last.id, kind="check",
                   level="none", level_reason=reason, view=last.view, view_change="unchanged",
                   one_liner=last.one_liner, state={k: v for k, v in last.state.items() if k != "what_changed"}, changes=[], delta=delta,
                   price=snap.price, language=lang, provider="none", prompt_version=prompts.PROMPT_VERSION)
        session.add(r)
        session.commit()
        return r

    # ---- cache: same thesis inputs => reuse ----
    new_titles = sorted(n["title"] for n in delta.get("new_news") or [])
    nrows = list(session.scalars(select(Note).where(Note.company_id == company.id, Note.kind.in_(("comment", "source", "question")))
                                 .order_by(Note.created_at.desc(), Note.id.desc()).limit(5)))
    active = (baseline_provider or provider) if kind in ("baseline", "rebaseline") else provider
    ihash = _hash({"pv": prompts.PROMPT_VERSION, "model": active.model, "lang": lang, "kind": kind,
                   "anchor": anchor.id if anchor else None, "fp": snap.fingerprint, "news": new_titles, "notes": [n.id for n in nrows]})
    cached = session.scalar(select(Review).where(Review.input_hash == ihash, Review.company_id == company.id))
    if cached:
        return cached

    compact = signals.compact_evidence(evidence)
    notes = [{"type": n.kind, "about": n.assumption_id, "text": n.body[:400]} for n in nrows] or None
    try:
        if kind in ("baseline", "rebaseline"):
            res = active.run(system=prompts.BASELINE_SYSTEM, schema=BaselineOutput,
                               payload=prompts.baseline_payload(compact, lang, anchor.state if anchor else None, notes),
                               max_tokens=BASELINE_MAX_TOKENS)
            o = res.data
            state = {"profile": o["profile"], "assumptions": o["assumptions"], "monitor": o["monitor"],
                     "valuation": {"implication": o["valuation_implication"], "consensus_gap": o["consensus_gap"]}}
            r = Review(kind=kind, view=o["view"], view_change=_view_change(anchor.view if anchor else None, o["view"]),
                       one_liner=o["one_liner"], state=state, narrative=o["report_markdown"], changes=[])
        else:
            prior = {"view": anchor.view, "one_liner": anchor.one_liner, "state": anchor.state}
            res = provider.run(system=prompts.UPDATE_SYSTEM, schema=UpdateOutput,
                               payload=prompts.update_payload(prior, delta, level, lang, delta.get("price_pct"), notes),
                               max_tokens=UPDATE_MAX_TOKENS)
            o = res.data
            state = apply_update(anchor.state, o)
            state["what_changed"] = o.get("what_changed") or []
            vc = _view_change(anchor.view, o["view"])           # derived, never trusted from the model
            r = Review(kind="update", view=o["view"], view_change=vc, one_liner=o["one_liner"], state=state,
                       narrative=o["narrative"], changes=o["changes"])
            if o.get("rebaseline_recommended"):
                state["rebaseline"] = o.get("rebaseline_reason") or True
    except AIProviderError as exc:
        log.error("AI call failed for %s (%s): %s usage=%s", ticker, exc.code, exc.message, exc.usage)
        session.rollback()
        raise ServiceError(exc.code, exc.message, 502)

    r.company_id, r.snapshot_id, r.prev_review_id = company.id, snap.id, anchor.id if anchor else None
    r.level, r.level_reason, r.delta, r.price, r.language = level, reason, delta, snap.price, lang
    r.provider, r.model, r.prompt_version, r.input_hash, r.usage = (
        active.name, getattr(res, "model", "") or active.model, prompts.PROMPT_VERSION, ihash, res.usage)
    session.add(r)
    session.commit()
    return r




===== workbench_v2/app/config.py =====

"""Single place for environment configuration."""
import os
try:
    from dotenv import load_dotenv
    load_dotenv(__import__("pathlib").Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / ".env")
except ImportError:
    pass

DATABASE_URL = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL", "sqlite:///" + str(__import__("pathlib").Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "workbench.db"))
OPENBB_PROVIDER = os.getenv("OPENBB_PROVIDER", "yfinance")
AI_PROVIDER = os.getenv("AI_PROVIDER", "mock").strip().lower()
AI_MODEL = os.getenv("AI_MODEL", "").strip()
AI_MODEL_BASELINE = os.getenv("AI_MODEL_BASELINE", "").strip()   # optional: stronger model for the one-off baseline
GEMINI_API_KEY = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "").strip()

def _budget(raw: str):
    """AI_THINKING_BUDGET: integer token budget for model 'thinking'; 0 disables it; 'off' = do not send the setting."""
    raw = (raw or "").strip().lower()
    if raw in ("", "off", "none"):
        return None
    try:
        return max(0, int(raw))
    except ValueError:
        return None


AI_THINKING_BUDGET = _budget(os.getenv("AI_THINKING_BUDGET", "1024"))


def _models(raw: str) -> list[str]:
    return [m.strip() for m in (raw or "").split(",") if m.strip()]


AI_MODEL_FALLBACKS = _models(os.getenv("AI_MODEL_FALLBACKS", ""))   # tried in order when a model is out of quota

APP_VERSION = "2.25.0-currency-layout"

# Keep provider caches/settings in this independent copy, not in the user's home.
from pathlib import Path
_PROVIDER_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / '.provider_cache'
try:
    import openbb_core.app.constants as _obb_constants
    _obb_constants.OPENBB_DIRECTORY = _PROVIDER_ROOT / 'openbb'
    _obb_constants.USER_SETTINGS_PATH = _obb_constants.OPENBB_DIRECTORY / 'user_settings.json'
    _obb_constants.SYSTEM_SETTINGS_PATH = _obb_constants.OPENBB_DIRECTORY / 'system_settings.json'
except ImportError:
    pass
try:
    import yfinance as _yf
    _yf.set_tz_cache_location(str(_PROVIDER_ROOT / 'yfinance'))
except ImportError:
    pass




===== workbench_v2/app/core/signals.py =====

"""Deterministic change detection. Pure Python, no I/O, no AI.

Valuation multiples move with the share price, so a P/E jump caused by a rally
is NOT a fundamental change. We strip the price effect before deciding whether
anything thesis-relevant happened.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from . import statements

FUNDAMENTAL_SECTIONS = ("financial_health", "analyst_consensus")
MULTIPLES = ("pe_ttm", "forward_pe", "peg", "price_to_sales", "price_to_book",
             "price_to_free_cash_flow", "ev_to_ebitda")   # scale WITH price
YIELDS = ("free_cash_flow_yield",)                          # scale INVERSELY

HARD_NEWS = ("raises guidance", "cuts guidance", "lowers guidance", "withdraws guidance",
             "earnings miss", "earnings beat", "acquisition", "acquires", "merger",
             "bankruptcy", "restatement", "sec investigation", "antitrust",
             "ceo resign", "ceo steps down", "chief executive",
             "上调指引", "下调指引", "撤回指引", "业绩不及预期", "业绩超预期",
             "收购", "并购", "破产", "财务重述", "反垄断", "首席执行官辞职")
SOFT_NEWS = ("guidance", "downgrade", "upgrade", "lawsuit", "recall", "layoff",
             "dividend", "buyback", "repurchase", "investigation", "cfo",
             "指引", "评级下调", "评级上调", "诉讼", "召回", "裁员", "分红", "回购")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Thresholds:
    fundamental_minor: float = 0.03
    fundamental_material: float = 0.15
    valuation_minor: float = 0.08      # measured after removing the price effect
    valuation_material: float = 0.20
    abs_floor: float = 1e-4


def _num(v: Any) -> float | None:
    return float(v) if isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) else None


def _pct(old: float, new: float) -> float:
    return (new - old) / max(abs(old), 1e-9)


def price_of(evidence: dict | None) -> float | None:
    return _num(((evidence or {}).get("market") or {}).get("current_price"))


def news_titles(evidence: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    return [str(n["title"]).strip() for n in (evidence or {}).get("recent_news") or []
            if isinstance(n, dict) and n.get("title")]


def compute_delta(cur: dict, prev: dict | None, th: Thresholds = Thresholds()) -> dict:
    """Compare current evidence with the last *analysed* anchor."""
    if not prev:
        return {"status": "initial", "price_pct": None, "fundamental_changes": [],
                "valuation_changes": [], "new_news": [], "price_driven_valuation": False}

    p_old, p_new = price_of(prev), price_of(cur)
    price_pct = _pct(p_old, p_new) if p_old and p_new else None

    fund = []
    for sec in FUNDAMENTAL_SECTIONS:
        a, b = prev.get(sec) or {}, cur.get(sec) or {}
        for k, v in b.items():
            old, new = _num(a.get(k)), _num(v)
            if old is None or new is None or abs(new - old) < th.abs_floor:
                continue
            pc = _pct(old, new)
            if abs(pc) >= th.fundamental_minor:
                fund.append({"metric": f"{sec}.{k}", "old": old, "new": new, "pct": round(pc, 4)})

    val, price_driven = [], False
    a, b = prev.get("valuation") or {}, cur.get("valuation") or {}
    for k in MULTIPLES + YIELDS:
        old, new = _num(a.get(k)), _num(b.get(k))
        if old is None or new is None or old == 0:
            continue
        raw = _pct(old, new)
        if price_pct is None:
            non_price = raw
        elif k in YIELDS:
            non_price = (1 + raw) * (1 + price_pct) - 1
        else:
            non_price = (1 + raw) / (1 + price_pct) - 1
        if abs(non_price) >= th.valuation_minor:
            val.append({"metric": f"valuation.{k}", "old": old, "new": new,
                        "raw_pct": round(raw, 4), "ex_price_pct": round(non_price, 4)})
        elif abs(raw) >= th.valuation_minor:
            price_driven = True

    q_old, q_new = statements.latest_quarter_end(prev.get("statements")), statements.latest_quarter_end(cur.get("statements"))
    if q_old and q_new and q_new != q_old:          # a new quarter was reported (only when the anchor already had quarterly data)
        fund.append({"metric": "statements.new_quarter", "old": q_old, "new": q_new, "pct": 0.0})

    old_t = set(news_titles(prev))
    fresh = [n for n in (cur.get("recent_news") or [])
             if isinstance(n, dict) and n.get("title") and str(n["title"]).strip() not in old_t]
    new_news = [{"title": n["title"], "date": n.get("date"), "source": n.get("source")} for n in fresh[:8]]

    changed = bool(fund or val or new_news)
    return {"status": "changed" if changed else "unchanged",
            "price_pct": None if price_pct is None else round(price_pct, 4),
            "fundamental_changes": fund[:20], "valuation_changes": val[:10],
            "new_news": new_news, "price_driven_valuation": price_driven}


def classify(delta: dict, th: Thresholds = Thresholds()) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """(level, reason); level is none | minor | material."""
    if delta.get("status") == "initial":
        return "material", "No prior analysis: baseline required."
    text = " ".join(n["title"].lower() for n in delta.get("new_news") or [])
    hard = next((w for w in HARD_NEWS if w in text), None)
    if hard:
        return "material", f"Material news keyword: '{hard}'."
    big_f = [c for c in delta.get("fundamental_changes") or [] if abs(c["pct"]) >= th.fundamental_material]
    if big_f:
        return "material", f"{big_f[0]['metric']} moved >= {th.fundamental_material:.0%}."
    big_v = [c for c in delta.get("valuation_changes") or [] if abs(c["ex_price_pct"]) >= th.valuation_material]
    if big_v:
        return "material", f"{big_v[0]['metric']} moved >= {th.valuation_material:.0%} beyond price."
    if delta.get("fundamental_changes"):
        return "minor", "Fundamental metric(s) moved beyond the minor threshold."
    if delta.get("valuation_changes"):
        return "minor", "Valuation changed beyond what price explains."
    soft = next((w for w in SOFT_NEWS if w in text), None)
    if soft:
        return "minor", f"News keyword: '{soft}'."
    return "none", "No thesis-relevant change (price and multiple moves are explained by price)."


def fingerprint(evidence: dict) -> str:
    """Hash of rounded, thesis-relevant evidence. Excludes price, market cap and news,
    so a price tick never invalidates the LLM cache."""
    out: dict[str, float] = {}
    for sec in FUNDAMENTAL_SECTIONS:
        for k, v in (evidence.get(sec) or {}).items():
            n = _num(v)
            if n is not None:
                out[f"{sec}.{k}"] = float(f"{n:.3g}")
    q = statements.latest_quarter_end(evidence.get("statements"))
    if q:
        out["statements.latest_quarter"] = q
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(out, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:24]


def compact_evidence(evidence: dict, max_desc: int = 500) -> dict:
    """Strip nulls and bulky text before sending to the model."""
    def clean(o: Any) -> Any:
        if isinstance(o, dict):
            return {k: clean(v) for k, v in o.items() if v not in (None, "", [], {})}
        if isinstance(o, list):
            return [clean(x) for x in o]
        return round(o, 4) if isinstance(o, float) else o
    e = dict(evidence)
    e.pop("data_quality", None)
    e.pop("schema_version", None)
    e.pop("statements", None)                     # the model gets the code-computed quarterly_trend in `derived` instead
    comp = dict(e.get("company") or {})
    for k in ("company_url", "employees", "stock_exchange"):
        comp.pop(k, None)
    if comp.get("long_description"):
        comp["long_description"] = str(comp["long_description"])[:max_desc]
    e["company"] = comp
    e["recent_news"] = [{"date": n.get("date"), "title": n.get("title")} for n in (e.get("recent_news") or [])[:5]]
    e["evidence_basis"] = evidence_basis(evidence)
    e["data_gaps"] = data_gaps(evidence)
    e["derived"] = derived_metrics(evidence)
    return clean(e)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Evidence basis, data gaps and code-computed "what the price implies" metrics.
# All arithmetic happens here; the model only interprets the results.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CYCLICAL_WORDS = ("memory", "semiconductor", "steel", "oil", "gas", "coal", "mining", "gold", "copper", "aluminum",
                  "chemical", "auto manufacturers", "airline", "shipping", "marine", "homebuild", "building materials",
                  "lumber", "paper", "agricultural", "farm", "equipment", "metals", "uranium", "solar")
CYCLICAL_SECTORS = ("energy", "basic materials")
STALE_DAYS = 380


def _first_num(*vals):
    for v in vals:
        n = _num(v)
        if n is not None:
            return n
    return None


def _age_days(iso, today: date) -> int | None:
    try:
        return (today - date.fromisoformat(str(iso)[:10])).days
    except ValueError:
        return None


def evidence_basis(ev: dict, now: datetime | None = None) -> dict:
    """What period each block of evidence covers. Ratios have no period tag in the source (TTM / latest reported);
    statement rows do, and they can lag the ratios right after earnings."""
    today = (now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)).date()
    fh = ev.get("financial_health") or {}
    inc = fh.get("income_statement_latest") or {}
    cfh = fh.get("cash_flow_history") or []
    ends = [x for x in (inc.get("period_ending"), (cfh[0] or {}).get("period_ending") if cfh else None) if x]
    age = _age_days(max(ends), today) if ends else None
    out = {"retrieved": today.isoformat(),
           "ratios": "TTM or latest reported (Yahoo Finance via OpenBB); the source gives no period label",
           "statements": {"period_end": inc.get("period_ending"), "fiscal_year": inc.get("fiscal_year"),
                          "fiscal_period": inc.get("fiscal_period"), "age_days": age,
                          "stale": bool(age is not None and age > STALE_DAYS)}}
    qe = statements.latest_quarter_end(ev.get("statements"))
    if qe:
        out["latest_reported_quarter"] = qe
    if cfh:
        out["cash_flow_history"] = "annual fiscal years " + ", ".join(str(r.get("fiscal_year") or r.get("period_ending")) for r in cfh[:4])
    return out


def data_gaps(ev: dict, now: datetime | None = None) -> list[str]:
    gaps = []
    b = evidence_basis(ev, now)
    if b["statements"]["stale"]:
        gaps.append(f"Statement rows end {b['statements']['period_end']} ({b['statements']['age_days']} days old) while ratios are newer "
                    "(TTM); do not combine figures across the two.")
    if not (ev.get("recent_news") or []):
        gaps.append("No news returned by the data source.")
    if not any(_num(v) is not None for v in (ev.get("analyst_consensus") or {}).values()):
        gaps.append("No analyst consensus data.")
    if not any(_num(v) is not None for v in (ev.get("financial_health") or {}).values()):
        gaps.append("No financial-health ratios.")
    return gaps


def is_cyclical(ev: dict) -> str | None:
    co = ev.get("company") or {}
    text = f"{co.get('industry') or ''} {co.get('sector') or ''}".lower()
    hit = next((w for w in CYCLICAL_WORDS if w in text), None) or next((w for w in CYCLICAL_SECTORS if w in text), None)
    return hit


def derived_metrics(ev: dict) -> dict:
    """Simple ratios computed from fields we already hold. No forecasts are invented."""
    m, v, fh = ev.get("market") or {}, ev.get("valuation") or {}, ev.get("financial_health") or {}
    cap = _num(m.get("market_cap"))
    cfh = fh.get("cash_flow_history") or []
    fcf = _first_num(fh.get("free_cash_flow"), (cfh[0] or {}).get("free_cash_flow") if cfh else None)
    fy = (cfh[0] or {}).get("fiscal_year") if cfh else None
    ev_val = _first_num(v.get("enterprise_value"), m.get("enterprise_value"))
    pe, fpe = _num(v.get("pe_ttm")), _num(v.get("forward_pe"))
    out: dict = {}
    if cap and fcf is not None:
        out["fcf_yield"] = {"value": round(fcf / cap, 4), "basis": f"FCF {'FY' + str(fy) if fy else 'latest'} / market cap"}
    if ev_val and fcf:
        out["ev_to_fcf"] = {"value": round(ev_val / fcf, 1), "basis": "enterprise value / latest annual FCF"}
    if pe and pe > 0:
        out["earnings_yield_ttm"] = {"value": round(1 / pe, 4), "basis": "1 / P/E (TTM)"}
    if fpe and fpe > 0:
        out["earnings_yield_forward"] = {"value": round(1 / fpe, 4), "basis": "1 / forward P/E (consensus)"}
    if pe and fpe and pe > 0 and fpe > 0:
        out["consensus_implied_eps_change"] = {"value": round(pe / fpe - 1, 3),
            "basis": "TTM P/E divided by forward P/E at one price: analysts expect forward EPS to differ from TTM EPS by this much (derived)"}
    if pe and fpe and pe > 0 and fpe > 0 and (pe / fpe - 1 >= 0.5 or pe / fpe - 1 <= -0.33):
        chg = pe / fpe - 1
        out["earnings_dislocation"] = {"value": round(chg, 3), "kind": "recovery_expected" if chg > 0 else "decline_expected",
            "caution": "Trailing and forward EPS differ a lot, so the price reflects analyst numbers, not trailing ones, and trailing multiples are distorted. "
                       "A recovery or decline is an assumption to test with evidence, not a fact. Analyst EPS is usually adjusted; reported EPS includes one-offs."}
    qt = statements.quarterly_trend(ev.get("statements"))
    if qt:
        out["quarterly_trend"] = qt
    hit = is_cyclical(ev)
    if hit:
        out["cyclical_flag"] = {"matched": hit, "caution": "Cyclical industry: a low P/E can mean peak earnings. Judge on mid-cycle earnings, not spot."}
    return out




===== workbench_v2/app/core/plan.py =====

"""Price-discipline status for a position plan. Pure Python. Long positions only."""
from __future__ import annotations

NEAR = 0.03          # within 3% counts as "near"
ORDER = ("stop_breached", "near_stop", "over_max", "target_reached", "near_target", "buy_zone")


def sizing(value, target_amount=None, max_amount=None, weight=None) -> dict | None:
    """Position size against your own plan, in money (the stock's currency). `value` is quantity x price.
    `weight` is the broker's portfolio weight as a fraction and is shown for context only."""
    if target_amount is None and max_amount is None:
        return None
    v = value or 0.0
    return {"value": round(v), "target": target_amount, "max": max_amount,
            "room_to_target": None if target_amount is None else round(target_amount - v),
            "room_to_max": None if max_amount is None else round(max_amount - v),
            "over_max": bool(max_amount is not None and v > max_amount),
            "pct_of_portfolio": None if weight is None else round(weight * 100, 1)}


def plan_status(price, buy_below=None, target=None, stop=None, near: float = NEAR,
                value=None, target_amount=None, max_amount=None, weight=None) -> dict | None:
    """Most important flag first. Distances are relative to the current price."""
    if not price or price <= 0:
        return None
    flags = []
    if stop:
        flags.append("stop_breached" if price <= stop else "near_stop" if price <= stop * (1 + near) else None)
    if target:
        flags.append("target_reached" if price >= target else "near_target" if price >= target * (1 - near) else None)
    if buy_below and price <= buy_below:
        flags.append("buy_zone")
    size = sizing(value, target_amount, max_amount, weight)
    if size and size["over_max"]:
        flags.append("over_max")
    flags = [f for f in flags if f]
    flags.sort(key=ORDER.index)
    rel = lambda x: round(x / price - 1, 4) if x else None
    return {"status": flags[0] if flags else "on_plan", "flags": flags,
            "to_target": rel(target), "to_stop": rel(stop), "to_buy": rel(buy_below), "sizing": size}




===== workbench_v2/app/core/snapshots.py =====

"""One-line descriptions of saved valuation records, so the snapshot list says what each one is.

Pure SQLite, no web framework. It only reads; it never changes a record."""
import json

KIND_LABEL = {"data": "Imported data", "analysis": "Saved analysis", "comparison": "Saved comparison"}
METHOD_LABEL = {"pe": "P/E", "p_fcf": "P/FCF", "ev_ebitda": "EV/EBITDA"}


def _summary(kind, ticker, payload):
    """Short facts for the list. `payload` is the parsed record; every field is optional (older records differ)."""
    try:
        if kind == "data":
            q = payload.get("quote") or {}
            return dict(price=q.get("value"), currency=q.get("currency"))
        if kind == "analysis":
            res = payload.get("result") or {}
            a = res.get("assumptions") or {}
            base = next((s for s in res.get("scenarios") or [] if s.get("scenario") == "Base"), None)
            return dict(method=METHOD_LABEL.get(a.get("method") or res.get("method") or "pe", "P/E"),
                        base=base and base.get("price_cagr"), years=a.get("years"), required=a.get("required_return"))
        if kind == "comparison":
            return dict(tickers=ticker)
    except (AttributeError, TypeError):
        pass
    return {}


def history_rows(conn, ticker, limit=50):
    """Newest first. Parses each record in Python so an older record with a different shape cannot break the list."""
    rows = conn.execute("SELECT id,kind,created_at,payload FROM valuations WHERE ticker=? ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT ?", (ticker.upper(), limit)).fetchall()
    out = []
    for ident, kind, created, raw in rows:
        try:
            payload = json.loads(raw)
        except ValueError:
            payload = {}
        out.append(dict(id=ident, kind=kind, created_at=created, label=KIND_LABEL.get(kind, kind), summary=_summary(kind, ticker, payload)))
    return out


def referenced_ids(conn):
    """Ids of data records that a saved analysis or comparison points to. Those must stay: they reproduce the result."""
    ids = set()
    for kind, raw in conn.execute("SELECT kind,payload FROM valuations WHERE kind IN ('analysis','comparison')"):
        try:
            p = json.loads(raw)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if kind == "analysis" and p.get("data_id"):
            ids.add(p["data_id"])
        elif kind == "comparison":
            ids.update(p.get("data_ids") or [])
    return ids


def delete_record(conn, ident):
    """The user deletes one record. An imported-data record that a saved result still uses is refused ('in_use')."""
    row = conn.execute("SELECT kind FROM valuations WHERE id=?", (ident,)).fetchone()
    if not row:
        return "missing"
    if row[0] == "data" and ident in referenced_ids(conn):
        return "in_use"
    conn.execute("DELETE FROM valuations WHERE id=?", (ident,))
    return "deleted"


def prune_data(conn, ticker, keep_latest=True):
    """Remove imported data for a ticker that nothing uses (no saved analysis or comparison points to it). The newest import is kept."""
    ids = [r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT id FROM valuations WHERE ticker=? AND kind='data' ORDER BY rowid DESC", (ticker.upper(),))]
    if keep_latest:
        ids = ids[1:]
    used = referenced_ids(conn)
    gone = [i for i in ids if i not in used]
    for i in gone:
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM valuations WHERE id=?", (i,))
    return len(gone)


===== workbench_v2/app/core/translate.py =====

"""Chinese view of a stored analysis. Only free-text fields are translated; numbers, ids, tickers and enums are never sent.
Translations are stored per review as {key: text}; a review never changes after it is written, so a stored translation never goes stale."""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
from typing import Iterator

PROFILE = ("classification", "business_model", "moat", "bear_case", "valuation_view")
ASSUMPTION = ("statement", "kill_criteria", "causal_mechanism", "missing_evidence", "warning_trigger", "last_reason", "time_horizon")
MONITOR = ("indicator", "why", "trigger", "why_this_level")


def _t(key: str, c, f) -> Iterator[tuple[str, object, object]]:
    try:
        v = c[f]
    except (KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
        return
    if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():
        yield key, c, f


def targets(r: dict) -> Iterator[tuple[str, object, object]]:
    """Every translatable (key, container, field) in a review-shaped dict: {one_liner, narrative, changes, state}."""
    yield from _t("one_liner", r, "one_liner")
    yield from _t("narrative", r, "narrative")
    for i, c in enumerate(r.get("changes") or []):
        for f in ("what", "so_what"):
            yield from _t(f"changes.{i}.{f}", c, f)
    st = r.get("state") or {}
    pr = st.get("profile") or {}
    for f in PROFILE:
        yield from _t(f"profile.{f}", pr, f)
    for f in ("key_drivers", "key_risks"):
        for i in range(len(pr.get(f) or [])):
            yield from _t(f"profile.{f}.{i}", pr[f], i)
    for a in st.get("assumptions") or []:
        for f in ASSUMPTION:
            yield from _t(f"a.{a.get('id')}.{f}", a, f)
    for i, m in enumerate(st.get("monitor") or []):
        for f in MONITOR:
            yield from _t(f"m.{i}.{f}", m, f)
    for f in ("implication", "consensus_gap"):
        yield from _t(f"valuation.{f}", st.get("valuation") or {}, f)


def flatten(r: dict) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {k: c[f] for k, c, f in targets(r)}


def localize(r: dict, items: dict[str, str] | None) -> dict:
    """A copy of r with translated text swapped in; anything without a translation stays English."""
    out = copy.deepcopy(r)
    if items:
        for k, c, f in targets(out):
            if items.get(k):
                c[f] = items[k]
    return out




===== workbench_v2/app/core/valuation_link.py =====

"""Link between the valuation lab and a company's research page. Pure functions: no database, no network, no model.

The valuation lab keeps its own append-only store. This module turns what is saved there into a short, honest summary for the
company page: what the saved assumptions imply today, what they imply at the price the user's Plan names, whether the saved
valuation is out of date, and which thesis assumptions each valuation input depends on. Nothing here changes a Plan, a thesis
or a saved valuation, and none of it is sent to a model."""
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from .compare import ticker_row

# What a thesis assumption can be tied to. The user chooses; the program never guesses a link.
DRIVERS = {
    'eps_growth': 'Earnings or cash-flow growth',
    'revenue_growth': 'Revenue growth',
    'margin': 'Margins',
    'multiple': 'What the market will pay (exit multiple)',
    'shares': 'Share count or dilution',
    'balance': 'Net debt or balance sheet',
}
# Which drivers each method's saved inputs rest on.
METHOD_DRIVERS = {'pe': ('eps_growth', 'multiple'), 'p_fcf': ('eps_growth', 'multiple', 'shares'),
                  'ev_ebitda': ('revenue_growth', 'margin', 'multiple', 'balance')}
METHOD_LABEL = {'pe': 'P/E', 'p_fcf': 'P/FCF', 'ev_ebitda': 'EV/EBITDA'}
WEAK = ('weakened', 'broken')
PRICE_MOVE = 0.15      # a saved valuation is stale when the price has moved this much since its data was fetched
DATA_DAYS = 100        # about a quarter: new results have probably been reported since


def _dt(v):
    if not v:
        return None
    try:
        d = datetime.fromisoformat(str(v).replace('Z', '+00:00'))
        return d if d.tzinfo else d.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    except ValueError:
        return None


def _sc(result, name):
    return next((s for s in result.get('scenarios') or [] if s.get('scenario') == name), None)


def at_price(result, entry):
    """What the saved Base path would return if you paid `entry`, and the most you could pay for the required return."""
    base, bear, bull = _sc(result, 'Base'), _sc(result, 'Bear'), _sc(result, 'Bull')
    a = result.get('assumptions') or {}
    years, req = a.get('years'), a.get('required_return')
    if not base or not years or req is None:
        return None
    fp = base['future_price']
    out = dict(max_entry=fp / (1 + req) ** years if fp and fp > 0 else None)
    if entry and entry > 0:
        f = lambda s: None if not s or s['future_price'] <= 0 else (s['future_price'] / entry) ** (1 / years) - 1
        out.update(entry=entry, base=f(base), bear=f(bear), bull=f(bull))
    return out


def analysis_row(rec, price_now, plan, today):
    """One saved analysis, flattened. `rec` carries the stored analysis plus 'data_price' and 'data_fetched' of its data snapshot."""
    res = (rec.get('payload') or {}).get('result') or {}
    a = res.get('assumptions') or {}
    m = a.get('method') or res.get('method') or 'pe'
    base, bear, bull = _sc(res, 'Base'), _sc(res, 'Bear'), _sc(res, 'Bull')
    if not base:
        return None
    anchor = res.get('anchor') or {}
    mult = a.get('exit_pe') if m == 'pe' else a.get('exit_multiple')
    row = dict(method=m, label=METHOD_LABEL.get(m, m), analysis_id=rec.get('id'), saved_at=rec.get('created_at'), years=a.get('years'),
               required_return=a.get('required_return'), anchor_id=a.get('anchor_id'), anchor_kind=anchor.get('category'),
               anchor_end=anchor.get('period_end'), anchor_value=anchor.get('value'), growth=a.get('growth'), fade_to=a.get('fade_to'),
               exit_multiple=mult, bear=bear and bear['price_cagr'], base=base['price_cagr'], bull=bull and bull['price_cagr'],
               base_price=base['future_price'], exit_date=res.get('exit_date'), warnings=sum(1 for c in res.get('checks') or [] if c.get('level') == 'warn'),
               price_at_save=rec.get('data_price'), data_fetched=rec.get('data_fetched'))
    if price_now and price_now > 0:
        row['now'] = at_price(res, price_now)
    if plan and plan.get('buy_below'):
        row['at_buy_below'] = at_price(res, plan['buy_below'])
    if plan and plan.get('target_price') and base['future_price']:
        row['target_vs_base'] = plan['target_price'] / base['future_price'] - 1
    return row


def stale_reasons(row, price_now, reviews, assumptions, drivers, today):
    """Plain reasons a saved valuation may no longer describe the company. Facts only; it never changes the saved numbers."""
    out = []
    saved = _dt(row.get('saved_at'))
    if saved:
        for r in reviews or []:
            at = _dt(r.get('created_at'))
            if at and at > saved and r.get('kind') != 'check' and (r.get('level') == 'material' or r.get('kind') == 'rebaseline'):
                out.append(dict(kind='thesis', text=f'The thesis was materially updated on {at.date().isoformat()}, after this valuation was saved. Check that its assumptions still hold.'))
                break
    p0 = row.get('price_at_save')
    if p0 and price_now and p0 > 0 and abs(price_now / p0 - 1) >= PRICE_MOVE:
        out.append(dict(kind='price', text=f'The price has moved {(price_now / p0 - 1) * 100:+.0f}% since the data behind this valuation was fetched ({p0:,.2f} to {price_now:,.2f}).'))
    fetched = _dt(row.get('data_fetched'))
    if fetched and (today - fetched).days > DATA_DAYS:
        out.append(dict(kind='data', text=f'The data is {(today - fetched).days} days old; a new quarter has probably been reported. Import again.'))
    mine = METHOD_DRIVERS.get(row.get('method'), ())
    stat = {a['id']: a for a in assumptions or []}
    weak = [stat[i] for i, d in (drivers or {}).items() if d in mine and i in stat and stat[i].get('status') in WEAK]
    for a in weak:
        out.append(dict(kind='assumption', text=f'Assumption {a["id"]} is {a["status"]} and is tied to "{DRIVERS.get(drivers[a["id"]])}" in this method: {a.get("statement", "")}'))
    return out


def driver_table(assumptions, drivers):
    """For each driver, the thesis assumptions the user tied to it, with their current status."""
    stat = {a['id']: a for a in assumptions or []}
    out = []
    for key, label in DRIVERS.items():
        ids = [i for i, d in (drivers or {}).items() if d == key and i in stat]
        out.append(dict(key=key, label=label, methods=[METHOD_LABEL[m] for m, ds in METHOD_DRIVERS.items() if key in ds],
                        assumptions=[dict(id=i, statement=stat[i].get('statement', ''), status=stat[i].get('status')) for i in ids]))
    return out


def question_for(summary):
    """A note the user may save: the thing the valuation says has to be true, in one sentence, from numbers on the page."""
    dis = summary.get('dislocation')
    if dis and dis.get('kind') == 'recovery' and dis.get('ttm_eps') and (dis.get('cons_0y') or dis.get('cons_1y')):
        e = dis.get('cons_0y') or dis.get('cons_1y')
        return (f'The price already assumes EPS recovers from {dis["ttm_eps"]:.2f} (trailing) to about {e:.2f} (analysts). '
                'What evidence would show the recovery is real, and what would show it is only an accounting difference?')
    best = next((a for a in summary.get('analyses') or [] if a.get('method') == 'pe'), None) or next(iter(summary.get('analyses') or []), None)
    if best and best.get('required_return') is not None and best.get('base') is not None and best['base'] < best['required_return']:
        return (f'At today\'s price my saved {best["label"]} Base case returns {best["base"] * 100:.1f}% a year against the {best["required_return"] * 100:.0f}% I require. '
                'Which assumption would have to be better than I wrote, and what would show it?')
    return None


def summarize(data_rec, analyses, price_now, reviews, assumptions, drivers, plan, now=None):
    """Everything the company page needs about valuation, from what is already stored."""
    today = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if not data_rec:
        return dict(available=False, drivers=driver_table(assumptions, drivers), driver_options=DRIVERS)
    d = data_rec['payload']
    dis = d.get('earnings_dislocation')
    rows, seen = [], set()
    for rec in analyses or []:                       # newest first: keep the latest saved analysis per method
        r = analysis_row(rec, price_now, plan, today)
        if r and r['method'] not in seen:
            seen.add(r['method'])
            r['stale'] = stale_reasons(r, price_now, reviews, assumptions, drivers, today)
            rows.append(r)
    try:
        sq = ticker_row(d, 3, 0.10, 0.0, 0.05)
        status_quo = dict(years=3, required_return=0.10, fade_to=0.05, methods=[dict(method=m['method'], label=m['label'], base=m['base'], bear=m['bear'], bull=m['bull'],
                          note=m['note'], implausible=m['implausible']) for m in sq['methods']], review=sq['review'])
    except (ValueError, OverflowError, KeyError, TypeError):
        status_quo = None
    out = dict(available=True, ticker=d.get('ticker'), data_id=data_rec.get('id'), fetched_at=d.get('retrieved_at'), data_price=(d.get('quote') or {}).get('value'),
               currency=(d.get('quote') or {}).get('currency'), price_now=price_now,
               dislocation=None if not dis else {k: dis.get(k) for k in ('kind', 'ttm_eps', 'cons_0y', 'cons_1y', 'jump', 'pe_trailing', 'pe_forward', 'lines')},
               analyses=rows, status_quo=status_quo, plan=plan and dict(buy_below=plan.get('buy_below'), target_price=plan.get('target_price')),
               drivers=driver_table(assumptions, drivers), driver_options=DRIVERS)
    out['question'] = question_for(out)
    out['stale'] = any(r['stale'] for r in rows)
    return out


===== workbench_v2/app/core/__init__.py =====





===== workbench_v2/app/core/thesis.py =====

"""Pure helpers: sector KPI hints for prompts and a rule-based thesis health label."""
from __future__ import annotations

HINTS = [
    (("restaurant", "franchis"), "Restaurants: same-store sales and traffic versus price/mix, franchise versus company-operated mix, unit growth, franchisee profitability, food and labor costs."),
    (("semiconductor",), "Semiconductors: customer capex plans, pricing/ASP and product mix, inventory days, backlog or book-to-bill, capacity additions, competitor share shifts, cyclicality."),
    (("software", "internet", "interactive media", "platform"), "Software/internet: net revenue retention, customer and usage growth, take rate or pricing, gross margin trend, sales efficiency, competitive intensity."),
    (("bank", "insurance", "capital markets", "asset management"), "Financials: net interest margin, credit quality and provisions, capital ratios, deposit or premium growth, combined ratio for insurers."),
    (("reit",), "REITs: occupancy, same-property NOI growth, lease expiries, funds from operations, leverage and debt maturities."),
    (("pharma", "biotech", "drug"), "Pharma/biotech: patent expiries, pipeline stage and readouts, pricing and reimbursement, key-product concentration."),
    (("oil", "gas", "coal", "uranium"), "Energy: commodity price sensitivity, production and reserve replacement, breakeven cost, capital discipline, balance sheet."),
    (("utilit",), "Utilities: allowed returns and rate cases, rate-base growth, regulatory and liability exposure, leverage."),
    (("auto",), "Autos: unit volume and mix, pricing and incentives, gross margin, inventory days, capex needs."),
    (("telecom",), "Telecom: subscriber net adds, ARPU, churn, capex intensity, spectrum and leverage."),
    (("retail", "discount", "apparel", "footwear", "department", "grocery", "household", "beverage", "packaged", "luxury", "cosmetic"),
     "Retail/consumer: same-store sales, traffic versus ticket, gross margin, inventory turns, e-commerce mix, market share."),
]
SUFFIX = " List any of these the supplied data cannot test under missing_evidence."


def sector_hint(company: dict | None) -> str | None:
    co = company or {}
    text = f"{co.get('industry') or ''} {co.get('sector') or ''}".lower()
    for words, hint in HINTS:
        if any(w in text for w in words):
            return hint + SUFFIX
    return None


def health(assumptions: list[dict]) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Intact / Watch / At risk, from structured fields only (never from model-written scores)."""
    watch = None
    for a in assumptions:
        mat, st, i = a.get("materiality") or "medium", a.get("status"), a.get("id")
        if st == "broken" or (st == "weakened" and mat == "high"):
            return "At risk", f"{i} {st} ({mat} materiality)"
        if watch is None and (st == "weakened" or (st == "unverified" and mat == "high")):
            watch = f"{i} {st}"
    return ("Watch", watch) if watch else ("Intact", "No assumption weakened or untested at high materiality")




===== workbench_v2/app/core/calendar.py =====

"""Pure helpers for the trade calendar: market-local dates and simple behaviour flags. No I/O."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo


def market_tz(symbol: str) -> str:
    s = (symbol or "").upper()
    return "Asia/Hong_Kong" if s.endswith(".HK") else "Asia/Singapore" if s.endswith(".SI") else "America/New_York"


def local_date(executed_at: str, symbol: str) -> str:
    """The calendar day the trade happened on in its own market (UTC would put some Hong Kong trades on the wrong day)."""
    d = datetime.fromisoformat(executed_at)
    if d.tzinfo is None:
        d = d.replace(tzinfo=ZoneInfo("UTC"))
    return d.astimezone(ZoneInfo(market_tz(symbol))).date().isoformat()


def round_trips(trades: list[dict], days: int = 10) -> set[int]:
    """Ids of trades where the same symbol was bought and sold within `days` days of each other (a fact, not a verdict)."""
    by: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
    for t in trades:
        by.setdefault(t["symbol"], []).append(t)
    out: set[int] = set()
    for rows in by.values():
        for a in rows:
            for b in rows:
                if a["side"] == "buy" and b["side"] == "sell" and abs((date.fromisoformat(a["date"]) - date.fromisoformat(b["date"])).days) <= days:
                    out.update((a["id"], b["id"]))
    return out


def since_pct(price: float | None, now: float | None) -> float | None:
    return round(now / price - 1, 4) if price and now else None




===== workbench_v2/app/core/valuation.py =====

"""Deterministic scenario/return tests; market references never become assumptions silently."""
from datetime import date
from typing import Literal
import math
from math import isclose
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from .valuation_context import reference_price

class Assumptions(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(allow_inf_nan=False, extra='forbid')
    method: Literal['pe']='pe'
    anchor_id: str
    years: int = Field(ge=1,le=10)
    required_return: float = Field(gt=-1,le=1)
    growth: float = Field(gt=-1,le=2)
    exit_pe: float = Field(gt=0,le=500)
    growth_spread: float = Field(ge=0,le=1)
    fade_to: float | None = Field(default=None,gt=-1,le=2)
    pe_spread: float = Field(ge=0,lt=1)
    acknowledge_basis: bool

class EbitdaAssumptions(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(allow_inf_nan=False,extra='forbid')
    method: Literal['ev_ebitda']
    anchor_id: str
    years: int = Field(ge=1,le=10)
    required_return: float = Field(gt=-1,le=1)
    growth: float = Field(gt=-1,le=2)
    growth_spread: float = Field(ge=0,le=1)
    fade_to: float | None = Field(default=None,gt=-1,le=2)
    margin: float = Field(gt=0,le=1)
    margin_spread: float = Field(ge=0,lt=1)
    exit_multiple: float = Field(gt=0,le=300)
    multiple_spread: float = Field(ge=0,lt=1)
    target_net_debt: float = Field(ge=-1e15,le=1e15)
    target_shares: float = Field(gt=0,le=1e15)
    other_claims: float = Field(ge=0,le=1e15)
    balance_assumption: Literal['user_forecast','hold_current_constant']
    acknowledge_basis: bool


class PfcfAssumptions(BaseModel):
    """Price / free-cash-flow exit multiple. growth is growth of total FCF; share_change is the yearly change in share count
    (negative = buybacks, positive = dilution), so FCF per share grows at (1+growth)/(1+share_change)-1."""
    model_config = ConfigDict(allow_inf_nan=False,extra='forbid')
    method: Literal['p_fcf']
    anchor_id: str
    years: int = Field(ge=1,le=10)
    required_return: float = Field(gt=-1,le=1)
    growth: float = Field(gt=-1,le=2)
    growth_spread: float = Field(ge=0,le=1)
    fade_to: float | None = Field(default=None,gt=-1,le=2)
    share_change: float = Field(gt=-.5,lt=.5)
    exit_multiple: float = Field(gt=0,le=300)
    multiple_spread: float = Field(ge=0,lt=1)
    acknowledge_basis: bool


def growth_factor(g0,g1,gy,total=None):
    """Compound factor over gy years (may be fractional). g1=None: constant growth g0. Otherwise the yearly growth moves in a
    straight line from g0 in the first year to g1 in the last of `total` years (default gy). A part-year at the end is
    compounded at that year's rate for its fraction."""
    if gy<=0: return (1+g0)**gy if gy<0 else 1.0
    if g1 is None or abs(g1-g0)<1e-12: return (1+g0)**gy
    n=max(1,math.ceil((total if total is not None else gy)-1e-9));f=1.0;k=0;left=gy
    while left>1e-9:
        w=min(1.0,left)
        f*=(1+g0+(g1-g0)*(min(k,n-1)/(n-1) if n>1 else 0))**w
        left-=w;k+=1
    return f


def fade_of(a,shift):
    return None if a.fade_to is None else a.fade_to+shift*a.growth_spread


def anniversary(day,years):
    try: return day.replace(year=day.year+years)
    except ValueError: return day.replace(year=day.year+years,day=28)


def base_context(data,a,key):
    if not a.acknowledge_basis: raise ValueError('Confirm the financial basis and assumptions before calculating.')
    anchor=next((x for x in data.get(key,[]) if x['id']==a.anchor_id),None)
    if not anchor: raise ValueError('Selected financial anchor is unavailable.')
    if anchor.get('value') is None or anchor['value']<=0: raise ValueError('A positive financial anchor is required.')
    price=data['quote'].get('value')
    if not price or price<=0: raise ValueError('A positive observed price is required.')
    currency=data['quote'].get('currency')
    if not currency or currency!=anchor.get('currency'):
        fx=data.get('fx') or {}
        raise ValueError('The price is in %s but this figure is in %s, and no exchange rate was applied (%s). Import the data again, or pick a figure in %s.'%(currency or 'an unknown currency',anchor.get('currency') or 'an unknown currency',fx.get('reason') or 'none stored with this import',currency or 'the quote currency'))
    asof=date.fromisoformat(data['retrieved_at'][:10]);end=anniversary(asof,a.years)
    if a.growth-a.growth_spread<=-1: raise ValueError('Bear growth must exceed -100%.')
    # Growth is counted for exactly the holding period. The starting value and the exit multiple are on the same basis (trailing,
    # or a future year), so the basis is carried forward one holding period; no extra years are added for the date gap.
    return anchor,price,currency,asof,end,float(a.years)


def outputs(data,a,anchor,price,currency,asof,end,gy,rows,reverse):
    target=price*(1+a.required_return)**a.years
    for row in rows:
        future=row['future_price']
        row.update(discounted_terminal=future/(1+a.required_return)**a.years,
                   price_cagr=(future/price)**(1/a.years)-1,price_change=future/price-1,
                   meets_required=future>=target)
        row['return_gap']=row['price_cagr']-a.required_return
    return dict(method=a.method,formula_version='valuation-v3',currency=currency,
                valuation_date=asof.isoformat(),exit_date=end.isoformat(),growth_years=gy,
                anchor=anchor,assumptions=a.model_dump(),scenarios=rows,reverse=reverse,
                required_exit_price=target,
                limitations=(['Reported figures in %s were converted to %s at one spot rate and the rate is held constant to the exit date, so a move in the exchange rate would change the result by about the same percentage.'%(anchor['fx_from'],currency)] if anchor.get('fx_from') else [])+['Price return excludes dividends, tax and fees.',
                  'Discounted terminal price is not a complete cash-flow valuation.',
                  'Bear/Base/Bull are user assumptions, not probabilities, bounds or analyst targets.',
                  'Growth is counted for exactly the holding period from the starting value. The exit multiple is read on the same basis as that starting value, so today\'s multiple on it (price ÷ starting value) adds nothing when held flat.'+(' Growth fades linearly from the starting rate to the fade rate by the exit date.' if getattr(a,'fade_to',None) is not None else ''),
                  'Reverse expectations are conditional on the selected multiple and other assumptions.'])


def base_path(a,anchor,price,gy,metric_of,per_year_factor=None):
    """Year-by-year Base path: metric (EPS or FCF/share) × multiple. The multiple moves in a straight line from today's
    (price ÷ starting value) to the exit multiple; the metric follows the growth path from the starting value, so year 0 reproduces
    today's price. Year 1 is comparable with analyst 12-month price targets."""
    mkey='exit_pe' if a.method=='pe' else 'exit_multiple'
    exit_m=getattr(a,mkey);start_m=price/anchor['value'];g0=a.growth;g1=a.fade_to
    drag=1+getattr(a,'share_change',0.0)
    if a.method=='p_fcf':
        g0=(1+g0)/drag-1;g1=None if g1 is None else (1+g1)/drag-1
    out=[]
    for t in range(1,a.years+1):
        value=anchor['value']*growth_factor(g0,g1,t,total=a.years)
        m=start_m+(exit_m-start_m)*t/a.years
        p=value*m
        out.append(dict(year=t,date=anniversary(date.fromisoformat(metric_of),t).isoformat(),value=value,multiple=m,price=p,
                        price_change=p/price-1,annualised=(p/price)**(1/t)-1 if p>0 else None))
    return out


def decompose(res,price,anchor,years,mkey):
    """Split each scenario's annual price return into an earnings (or cash-flow) part and a multiple part.
    Exact identity: future/price = (X_exit/X_anchor) * (M_exit/M_start), with M_start = price/X_anchor."""
    start=price/anchor['value'];res['start_multiple']=start
    for row in res['scenarios']:
        me=(row[mkey]/start)**(1/years)-1
        row['multiple_effect']=me;row['earnings_effect']=(1+row['price_cagr'])/(1+me)-1
    return res


def range_checks(data,a,anchor,gy):
    """Plausibility notes next to the result. They never change the numbers; level 'warn' means look before trusting."""
    out=[];cm=data.get('current_multiples') or {};price=data['quote']['value']
    def add(level,text): out.append(dict(level=level,text=text))
    est={e.get('period'):e.get('avg') for e in data.get('estimates',[]) if e.get('metric')=='eps'}
    today=price/anchor['value'] if anchor['value']>0 else None   # today's multiple on this very starting value
    try:
        age=(date.fromisoformat(data['retrieved_at'][:10])-date.fromisoformat(anchor['period_end'])).days/30.44
    except (ValueError,KeyError,TypeError): age=0
    if a.method=='pe':
        if today and a.exit_pe>1.5*today: add('warn',f'Exit P/E {a.exit_pe:.1f}x is more than 1.5x what the market pays today on this starting point ({today:.1f}x = price ÷ {anchor["value"]:.2f}): the result leans on multiple expansion.')
        elif today and a.exit_pe<0.5*today: add('note',f'Exit P/E {a.exit_pe:.1f}x is less than half of today\'s {today:.1f}x on this starting point: a deep de-rating is assumed.')
        ttm=cm.get('trailing_eps')
        if anchor['id'].startswith('annual:') and ttm and ttm>0 and abs(ttm/anchor['value']-1)>.25:
            add('warn',f'Annual anchor EPS {anchor["value"]:.2f} differs from TTM EPS {ttm:.2f} by more than 25%: the anchor is stale or contains one-offs.')
        if anchor['value']>0 and price/anchor['value']>60:
            add('warn',f'Price is {price/anchor["value"]:.0f}x this anchor EPS: a small EPS base makes the projection very sensitive.')
        if est.get('0y') and est.get('+1y') and est['0y']>0:
            g=est['+1y']/est['0y']-1
            if a.growth>max(2*g,g+.05): add('warn',f'Base growth {a.growth*100:.1f}% is well above the analyst +1y/0y EPS growth of {g*100:.1f}%.')
        dis=data.get('earnings_dislocation')
        if dis and dis.get('kind')=='recovery' and anchor.get('category')!='market_consensus':
            add('warn',f'Reported EPS {dis["ttm_eps"]:.2f} is far below analyst EPS {(dis.get("cons_0y") or dis.get("cons_1y")):.2f}: the price already expects a recovery, so a multiple read on trough earnings is distorted. Start from the analyst year instead, and use the "what you must believe" grid.')
    elif a.method=='p_fcf':
        if today and a.exit_multiple>1.5*today: add('warn',f'Exit P/FCF {a.exit_multiple:.1f}x is more than 1.5x what the market pays today on this starting point ({today:.1f}x): the result leans on multiple expansion.')
        elif today and a.exit_multiple<0.5*today: add('note',f'Exit P/FCF {a.exit_multiple:.1f}x is less than half of today\'s {today:.1f}x on this starting point.')
        if anchor['value']>0 and price/anchor['value']>60: add('warn',f'Price is {price/anchor["value"]:.0f}x this FCF per share: a small base makes the projection very sensitive.')
        hist=[r for r in data.get('fcf_history',[]) if r.get('conversion') is not None and not r.get('ttm')]
        if hist and (hist[0]['conversion']<.5 or hist[0]['conversion']>1.5): add('warn',f'Free cash flow is {hist[0]["conversion"]*100:.0f}% of net income in the latest year: cash conversion is unusual, so check capex timing and one-offs.')
        latest=next((r for r in data.get('fcf_history',[]) if r.get('ttm')),None) or (data.get('fcf_history') or [None])[0]
        if latest and latest.get('sbc') and latest.get('fcf') and latest['fcf']>0 and latest['sbc']/latest['fcf']>.25 and not anchor['id'].startswith('fcfsbc'):
            add('note',f'Stock-based compensation is {latest["sbc"]/latest["fcf"]*100:.0f}% of FCF. Consider the after-SBC anchor.')
        if anchor['id'].startswith('fcfsbc') and a.share_change>0:
            add('note','The after-SBC cash flow already treats stock compensation as a cost; a positive share-count change counts the same dilution a second time.')
        gs=((data.get('defaults') or {}).get('p_fcf') or {}).get('growth_source')
        if gs in ('eps_cons','rev_cons'): add('note','No analyst forecast exists for free cash flow: the default growth is the analyst '+('EPS' if gs=='eps_cons' else 'revenue')+' growth used as a stand-in, so P/E and P/FCF agreeing is not independent confirmation.')
    else:
        hist=[r.get('ebitda_margin') for r in data.get('historical',[]) if r.get('ebitda_margin') is not None]
        if hist and a.margin>1.5*max(hist): add('warn',f'Base margin {a.margin*100:.1f}% is above 1.5x the highest reported EBITDA margin ({max(hist)*100:.1f}%).')
        cur=cm.get('enterprise_to_ebitda')
        if cur and a.exit_multiple>1.5*cur: add('warn',f'Exit EV/EBITDA {a.exit_multiple:.1f}x is more than 1.5x today\'s {cur:.1f}x.')
        if anchor.get('category')=='market_consensus': add('note','The revenue starting point is a future year but the EV/EBITDA multiple from the provider is trailing: forward EBITDA times a trailing multiple overstates the result. Prefer the TTM revenue starting point.')
    if (anchor.get('category')=='historical_reported' or anchor['id'].startswith('annual:')) and age>15:
        add('warn',f'This starting point ended {age:.0f} months ago. It is grown for the holding period only, so growth since then is not in the base. Prefer the TTM or analyst starting point.')
    return out


def belief_grid(data,a,res):
    """What you must believe: the exit price for different earnings (or cash-flow) outcomes against different exit multiples.
    Rows scale the Base starting value (shortfalls of 20% and 40%, plus the reported trailing level when the start is an analyst
    year); columns are today's multiple on the starting point, your exit multiple and the reference multiples. Cell = annual price
    return. Pure arithmetic on stated inputs; it ranks nothing and picks no cell."""
    if a.method not in ('pe','p_fcf'): return None
    base=res['scenarios'][1];price=data['quote']['value'];anchor=res['anchor']
    key='eps' if a.method=='pe' else 'fcf_per_share';mkey='exit_pe' if a.method=='pe' else 'exit_multiple'
    mine=getattr(a,mkey);today=price/anchor['value'];e0=base[key]
    rows=[dict(label='Starting value delivered, then your growth',factor=1.0),dict(label='Falls 20% short',factor=.8),dict(label='Falls 40% short',factor=.6)]
    if a.method=='pe':
        ttm=next((x['value'] for x in data.get('anchors',[]) if x['id']=='ttm' and x.get('value') and x['value']>0),None)
        if ttm and anchor.get('category')=='market_consensus' and ttm/anchor['value']<.75:
            rows.append(dict(label=f'No recovery: reported trailing EPS {ttm:.2f} is the base',factor=ttm/anchor['value']))
    cols=[dict(label='Today on this starting point',value=today),dict(label='Your exit multiple',value=mine)]
    ctx=(data.get('multiple_context') or {}).get(a.method) or {}
    for pt in ctx.get('points') or []:
        k=pt.get('key')
        if k=='today' and pt.get('anchor') and pt['anchor']!=a.anchor_id: cols.append(dict(label=pt['label']+' (other basis)',value=pt['low']))
        elif k=='history': cols+=[dict(label='Own history · low (trailing)',value=pt['low']),dict(label='Own history · high (trailing)',value=pt['high'])]
        elif k=='peers' and pt.get('median'): cols.append(dict(label='Peers · median',value=pt['median']))
    uniq=[]
    for c in cols:
        if c['value'] and c['value']>0 and not any(abs(c['value']/u['value']-1)<.03 for u in uniq): uniq.append(c)
    uniq=sorted(uniq,key=lambda c:c['value'])[:6]
    ret=a.required_return
    for r in rows:
        r['value']=e0*r['factor'];r['cells']=[]
        for c in uniq:
            fp=r['value']*c['value'];cg=(fp/price)**(1/a.years)-1 if fp>0 else None
            r['cells'].append(dict(price=fp,cagr=cg,meets=cg is not None and cg>=ret))
    return dict(method=a.method,noun='EPS' if a.method=='pe' else 'FCF/share',columns=uniq,rows=rows,required_return=ret,
        note='Each cell: Base-path value at exit (shown in the row) × the multiple in the column, as an annual price return from today\'s price. The multiple should sit on the same basis as the starting value. References marked trailing are on past-year earnings, so on a forward basis read them as somewhat high.')


def calculate(data,a):
    if isinstance(a,EbitdaAssumptions): return calculate_ebitda(data,a)
    if isinstance(a,PfcfAssumptions): return calculate_pfcf(data,a)
    anchor,price,cur,asof,end,gy=base_context(data,a,'anchors');eps=anchor['value'];rows=[]
    for label,shift in (('Bear',-1),('Base',0),('Bull',1)):
        growth=a.growth+shift*a.growth_spread;pe=a.exit_pe*(1+shift*a.pe_spread)
        fade=fade_of(a,shift)
        if fade is not None and fade<=-1: raise ValueError('Bear fade growth must exceed -100%.')
        projected=eps*growth_factor(growth,fade,gy)
        rows.append(dict(scenario=label,growth=growth,exit_pe=pe,eps=projected,future_price=projected*pe))
    needed=price*(1+a.required_return)**a.years/a.exit_pe
    reverse=dict(required_eps=needed,required_eps_growth=(needed/eps)**(1/gy)-1,
                 required_exit_pe=price*(1+a.required_return)**a.years/rows[1]['eps'],
                 exit_pe=a.exit_pe,required_return=a.required_return)
    res=outputs(data,a,anchor,price,cur,asof,end,gy,rows,reverse)
    res['path']=base_path(a,anchor,price,gy,res['valuation_date'])
    decompose(res,price,anchor,a.years,'exit_pe')
    res['checks']=range_checks(data,a,anchor,gy)
    res['grid']=belief_grid(data,a,res)
    return res


def calculate_pfcf(data,a):
    anchor,price,cur,asof,end,gy=base_context(data,a,'fcf_anchors');base=anchor['value'];rows=[]
    drag=1+a.share_change
    for label,shift in (('Bear',-1),('Base',0),('Bull',1)):
        growth=a.growth+shift*a.growth_spread;multiple=a.exit_multiple*(1+shift*a.multiple_spread)
        if growth<=-1: raise ValueError('Bear growth must exceed -100%.')
        fade=fade_of(a,shift)
        if fade is not None and fade<=-1: raise ValueError('Bear fade growth must exceed -100%.')
        per_share=base*growth_factor((1+growth)/drag-1,None if fade is None else (1+fade)/drag-1,gy)
        rows.append(dict(scenario=label,growth=growth,exit_multiple=multiple,fcf_per_share=per_share,
                         fcf_yield=1/multiple,future_price=per_share*multiple))
    needed=price*(1+a.required_return)**a.years/a.exit_multiple
    reverse=dict(required_fcf_per_share=needed,required_fcf_per_share_growth=(needed/base)**(1/gy)-1,
                 required_exit_multiple=price*(1+a.required_return)**a.years/rows[1]['fcf_per_share'],
                 exit_multiple=a.exit_multiple,required_return=a.required_return)
    res=outputs(data,a,anchor,price,cur,asof,end,gy,rows,reverse)
    res['path']=base_path(a,anchor,price,gy,res['valuation_date'])
    decompose(res,price,anchor,a.years,'exit_multiple')
    res['checks']=range_checks(data,a,anchor,gy)
    res['grid']=belief_grid(data,a,res)
    res['limitations']+=['Free cash flow = operating cash flow − capital expenditure as supplied by the provider; leases, acquisitions and working-capital swings are not adjusted.',
                         'Per-share FCF uses the share count named in the anchor basis; buybacks and dilution are the share_change assumption.']
    return res


def calculate_ebitda(data,a):
    anchor,price,cur,asof,end,gy=base_context(data,a,'revenue_anchors')
    if a.margin-a.margin_spread<=0 or a.margin+a.margin_spread>1:
        raise ValueError('All EBITDA margins must be greater than zero and at most 100%.')
    baseline=data.get('market_baseline') or {}
    if a.balance_assumption=='hold_current_constant':
        if baseline.get('currency')!=cur: raise ValueError('Current balance currency does not match the quote.')
        if baseline.get('net_debt') is None or baseline.get('shares') is None: raise ValueError('Current net debt and shares are required for this assumption.')
        if not isclose(a.target_net_debt,baseline['net_debt'],rel_tol=1e-12,abs_tol=.001) or not isclose(a.target_shares,baseline['shares'],rel_tol=1e-12,abs_tol=.001):
            raise ValueError('Hold-current assumption must match the saved provider balance and share values.')
    rows=[]
    for label,shift in (('Bear',-1),('Base',0),('Bull',1)):
        growth=a.growth+shift*a.growth_spread;margin=a.margin+shift*a.margin_spread
        multiple=a.exit_multiple*(1+shift*a.multiple_spread)
        fade=fade_of(a,shift)
        if fade is not None and fade<=-1: raise ValueError('Bear fade growth must exceed -100%.')
        revenue=anchor['value']*growth_factor(growth,fade,gy);ebitda=revenue*margin;ev=ebitda*multiple
        equity=ev-a.target_net_debt-a.other_claims
        rows.append(dict(scenario=label,growth=growth,margin=margin,exit_multiple=multiple,
            revenue=revenue,ebitda=ebitda,enterprise_value=ev,equity_value=equity,
            future_price=max(0,equity)/a.target_shares,
            equity_floor_applied=equity<0))
    needed=(price*(1+a.required_return)**a.years*a.target_shares+a.target_net_debt+a.other_claims)/a.exit_multiple
    reverse=dict(required_ebitda=needed,required_margin=needed/rows[1]['revenue'],
                 required_exit_multiple=(price*(1+a.required_return)**a.years*a.target_shares+a.target_net_debt+a.other_claims)/rows[1]['ebitda'],
                 required_return=a.required_return)
    result=outputs(data,a,anchor,price,cur,asof,end,gy,rows,reverse)
    result['checks']=range_checks(data,a,anchor,gy)
    result['limitations']+=['Current net debt/shares, if reused, are explicitly assumed constant to exit; other claims are user-entered.',
                           'EBITDA excludes capex, working capital, tax and financing costs. SBC and lease definitions may differ.',
                           'Negative residual equity is floored at zero for per-share price, not represented as a negative stock price.']
    return result


def consensus_bridge(data,pe,spread):
    if not 0<pe<=500 or not 0<=spread<1: raise ValueError('Invalid P/E or range.')
    rows=[]
    for estimate in data.get('estimates',[]):
        if estimate.get('metric')!='eps' or estimate.get('period') not in ('0y','+1y'): continue
        row=dict(period=estimate['period'],eps=estimate.get('avg'),source=estimate.get('source'),
                 period_end=estimate.get('period_end'),basis=estimate.get('basis'),prices=[])
        try:
            for name,shift in (('Lower P/E',-1),('Selected P/E',0),('Higher P/E',1)):
                m=pe*(1+shift*spread);value=reference_price(estimate,m,data['quote'].get('currency'))
                price=data['quote'].get('value')
                row['prices'].append(dict(label=name,pe=m,price=value,change=value/price-1 if price and price>0 else None))
        except ValueError as exc: row['error']=str(exc)
        rows.append(row)
    return dict(rows=rows,pe=pe,spread=spread,
        explanation='Annual consensus mean EPS × user-selected P/E. A valuation sensitivity, not an analyst target. No annualized return: exact forecast dates and EPS basis remain unverified. Current price / this same EPS × this EPS reproduces the current price by identity.')




===== workbench_v2/app/core/valuation_context.py =====

"""Comparable valuation references. Never treats reconstructed annual P/E as point-in-time TTM."""
from datetime import date, datetime
from statistics import median
import math


def num(value):
    if value is None or isinstance(value,bool): return None
    try:
        v=float(value)
        return v if math.isfinite(v) else None
    except (ValueError,TypeError): return None


def ratio(price,eps):
    p,e=num(price),num(eps)
    return p/e if p is not None and p>0 and e is not None and e>0 else None


def historical_pe(rows, prices, quote_currency):
    """Close is split-adjusted but not dividend-adjusted in Yahoo auto_adjust=False.
    Annual EPS is as currently supplied/restated, hence inherently ex-post.
    Only nearby prior trading dates are accepted; never select a future close.
    """
    out=[]
    for row in rows:
        end=date.fromisoformat(row['period_end'])
        candidates=[x for x in prices if 0 <= (end-date.fromisoformat(x['date'])).days <= 7]
        close=max(candidates,key=lambda x:x['date']) if candidates else None
        price=close['close'] if close else None
        pe=ratio(price,row.get('diluted_eps')) if row.get('currency')==quote_currency and quote_currency else None
        out.append(dict(period_end=row['period_end'],eps=row.get('diluted_eps'),currency=row.get('currency'),
                        price_date=close['date'] if close else None,close=price,pe=pe,
                        basis='Ex-post fiscal-end close / same-year reported EPS; not point-in-time TTM',
                        status='available' if pe is not None else 'Missing comparable price/EPS/currency, or non-positive EPS'))
    available=[x['pe'] for x in out if x['pe'] is not None]
    return dict(rows=out,median=median(available) if available else None,low=min(available) if available else None,
                high=max(available) if available else None,count=len(available),
                limitations='Sparse annual observations; current/restated EPS and provider split conventions. Not a historical trading P/E series, not suitable for backtesting or a fair-value rule.')


def reference_price(estimate, pe, currency):
    if estimate.get('metric')!='eps' or estimate.get('period') not in ('0y','+1y'):
        raise ValueError('Use annual EPS consensus. Quarterly EPS is not annualized.')
    if not currency or estimate.get('currency')!=currency: raise ValueError('Consensus and quote currencies must match.')
    eps=num(estimate.get('avg')); multiple=num(pe)
    if eps is None or eps<=0 or multiple is None or multiple<=0: raise ValueError('Positive EPS and P/E required.')
    return eps*multiple




# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dates, anchors and defaults. Pure functions so they can be tested without a network.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def to_date(value):
    """Epoch seconds, ISO text or a date/datetime -> date; anything else -> None."""
    if value is None or isinstance(value, bool):
        return None
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        return value.date()
    if isinstance(value, date):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        try:
            return datetime.fromtimestamp(value, tz=__import__('datetime').timezone.utc).date()
        except (OverflowError, OSError, ValueError):
            return None
    try:
        return date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
    except ValueError:
        return None


def add_years(day, years):
    try:
        return day.replace(year=day.year + years)
    except ValueError:                      # 29 February
        return day.replace(year=day.year + years, day=28)


def consensus_period_end(info, period):
    """Map Yahoo's relative annual periods to dates. 0y = the fiscal year now in progress, ending at nextFiscalYearEnd;
    +1y = the one after. Returns an ISO date, or None when the provider gave no fiscal year end."""
    end = to_date((info or {}).get('nextFiscalYearEnd'))
    if end is None or period not in ('0y', '+1y'):
        return None
    return (end if period == '0y' else add_years(end, 1)).isoformat()


def earnings_quality_flags(historical):
    """Generic one-off detection from annual rows (newest first): a net margin far from the prior years' median."""
    margins = []
    for r in historical or []:
        ni, rev = num(r.get('net_income')), num(r.get('revenue'))
        margins.append((r.get('period_end'), ni / rev if ni is not None and rev and rev > 0 else None))
    if len(margins) < 3 or margins[0][1] is None:
        return []
    prior = [m for _, m in margins[1:4] if m is not None]
    if len(prior) < 2:
        return []
    base = median(prior)
    now = margins[0][1]
    out = []
    if now is not None and base > 0.01 and (now > 2 * base or now < 0.4 * base):
        out.append(f"Net margin {now * 100:.1f}% in the latest year versus a median of {base * 100:.1f}% in the prior years: the "
                   "reported EPS may include one-off items. Check the filing before compounding it as recurring earnings.")
    return out


def pct_or_none(v):
    return None if v is None else round(v * 100, 2)


def add_months(day, months):
    y, m = divmod(day.month - 1 + months, 12)
    y, m = day.year + y, m + 1
    for d in (day.day, 30, 29, 28):
        try:
            return date(y, m, d)
        except ValueError:
            continue


def consensus_quarter_end(info, period):
    """Approximate end of Yahoo's relative quarters: 0q = the quarter after the latest reported one, +1q = the next. Quarter ends
    shift by a few days (52-week calendars), so the date is approximate."""
    last = to_date((info or {}).get('mostRecentQuarter'))
    if last is None or period not in ('0q', '+1q'):
        return None
    return add_months(last, 3 if period == '0q' else 6).isoformat()


def growth_refs(data):
    """Growth rates the data itself offers, in percent, each with its basis. No market forecast exists for free cash flow; the
    nearest forward-looking proxies are analyst EPS and revenue growth."""
    est = {(e.get('metric'), e.get('period')): num(e.get('avg')) for e in data.get('estimates') or []}
    hist = sorted(data.get('historical') or [], key=lambda r: str(r.get('period_end')), reverse=True)
    fh = sorted([r for r in data.get('fcf_history') or [] if not r.get('ttm')], key=lambda r: str(r.get('period_end')), reverse=True)
    g = lambda a, b: a / b - 1 if a is not None and b is not None and a > 0 and b > 0 else None
    r1 = lambda v: None if v is None else round(v * 100, 1)
    ends = {e.get('period'): e.get('period_end') for e in data.get('estimates') or [] if e.get('period_end')}
    span = f' (fiscal years to {ends.get("0y")} and {ends.get("+1y")})' if ends.get('0y') and ends.get('+1y') else ''

    def row(i, label, v, basis, methods):
        return dict(id=i, label=label, value=r1(v), basis=basis, methods=methods)
    return [
        row('eps_cons', 'Analyst EPS growth, next year over this year', g(est.get(('eps', '+1y')), est.get(('eps', '0y'))),
            'Consensus mean EPS' + span + '. The only forward-looking earnings growth available.', ['pe', 'p_fcf']),
        row('rev_cons', 'Analyst revenue growth, next year over this year', g(est.get(('revenue', '+1y')), est.get(('revenue', '0y'))),
            'Consensus mean revenue' + span + '. Matches the EV/EBITDA revenue path.', ['ev_ebitda', 'p_fcf']),
        row('rev_last', 'Last reported annual revenue growth', g(num(hist[0].get('revenue')) if hist else None, num(hist[1].get('revenue')) if len(hist) > 1 else None),
            'Latest fiscal year over the one before; backward-looking.', ['ev_ebitda', 'p_fcf']),
        row('eps_last', 'Last reported annual EPS growth', g(num(hist[0].get('diluted_eps')) if hist else None, num(hist[1].get('diluted_eps')) if len(hist) > 1 else None),
            'Latest fiscal year over the one before; backward-looking and noisy.', ['pe']),
        row('fcf_last', 'Last annual free cash flow per share growth', g(fh[0]['fcf_per_share'] if fh else None, fh[1]['fcf_per_share'] if len(fh) > 1 else None),
            'Latest fiscal year over the one before; swings widely when the base is small.', ['p_fcf']),
    ]


DEFAULT_FADE = 5.0   # percent a year; a long-run nominal growth assumption, shown and editable


def pick_pe_anchor(data):
    """Default starting EPS: the furthest analyst year (analysts' numbers first, our growth only after them), else TTM, else the
    latest reported year. Consensus years are used as given; their dates only matter for display."""
    rows = [a for a in data.get('anchors') or [] if (num(a.get('value')) or 0) > 0]
    by = {a['id']: a for a in rows}
    for i in ('consensus:+1y', 'consensus:0y', 'ttm'):
        if i in by:
            return by[i]
