    return next((a for a in rows if a.get('category') == 'historical_reported'), None)


def multiple_by_anchor(price, anchors):
    """Today's multiple on each starting point = price / that value. Holding it flat adds nothing from the multiple, whatever
    the anchor: the multiple always sits on the same basis (trailing, or a future year) as the number it multiplies."""
    p = num(price)
    return {a['id']: round(p / a['value'], 2) for a in anchors or []
            if p and p > 0 and num(a.get('value')) and a['value'] > 0 and a.get('id')}


def status_quo(data):
    """Starting values taken from the company's own numbers, in percent units for the form. They describe 'nothing changes',
    not a view; the user edits them. Missing inputs stay None. The exit multiple is today's price divided by the SAME starting
    value the growth is applied to (trailing for TTM, a future year for consensus), so that growth, not a change of basis, is
    what moves the result."""
    cm = data.get('current_multiples') or {}
    hist = sorted(data.get('historical') or [], key=lambda r: str(r.get('period_end')), reverse=True)
    refs = {r['id']: r for r in growth_refs(data)}
    val = lambda i: (refs.get(i) or {}).get('value')
    price = (data.get('quote') or {}).get('value')
    m_ttm = num(data.get('ttm_ebitda_margin'))
    m_now = m_ttm if m_ttm is not None else next((r.get('ebitda_margin') for r in hist if r.get('ebitda_margin') is not None), None)
    pe_anchor = pick_pe_anchor(data)
    pe_map = multiple_by_anchor(price, data.get('anchors'))
    pe_now = pe_map.get(pe_anchor['id']) if pe_anchor else None
    if pe_now is None:
        pe_now = num(cm.get('trailing_pe')) or num(cm.get('forward_pe'))
    ev_now = num(cm.get('enterprise_to_ebitda'))
    r1 = lambda v: None if v is None else round(v, 1)
    fade = lambda g: DEFAULT_FADE if g is not None and g > DEFAULT_FADE else None
    g_pe = val('eps_cons')
    g_fcf, src_fcf = next(((val(i), i) for i in ('eps_cons', 'rev_cons', 'fcf_last') if val(i) is not None), (None, None))
    g_ev, src_ev = next(((val(i), i) for i in ('rev_cons', 'rev_last') if val(i) is not None), (None, None))
    return {
        'unit': 'percent / multiples',
        'note': ('Status quo starting points: start from the furthest analyst year where there is one (else trailing twelve months), '
                 f'grow it at the analyst rate fading to a long-run {DEFAULT_FADE:.0f}% by the exit date, and keep the multiple the market pays '
                 'today on that same starting number. A neutral start, not a view; change one input at a time.'),
        'pe': {'anchor_id': pe_anchor['id'] if pe_anchor else None, 'multiple_by_anchor': pe_map,
               'growth': g_pe, 'growth_source': 'eps_cons' if g_pe is not None else None, 'fade_to': fade(g_pe), 'exit_pe': r1(pe_now),
               'growth_spread': 3.0, 'pe_spread': 10.0},
        'p_fcf': {'growth': g_fcf, 'growth_source': src_fcf, 'fade_to': fade(g_fcf), 'share_change': 0.0, 'growth_spread': 3.0, 'multiple_spread': 10.0},
        'ev_ebitda': {'growth': g_ev, 'growth_source': src_ev, 'fade_to': fade(g_ev), 'margin': r1(pct_or_none(m_now)),
                      'margin_source': 'ttm' if m_ttm is not None else 'last_annual', 'exit_multiple': r1(ev_now),
                      'growth_spread': 2.0, 'margin_spread': 1.0, 'multiple_spread': 10.0},
    }


def pfcf_defaults(data, use_after_sbc):
    """(anchor, {anchor_id: price/value}) for P/FCF. The after-stock-compensation anchor gets its own multiple, so numerator and
    denominator always match."""
    rows = [a for a in data.get('fcf_anchors') or [] if (num(a.get('value')) or 0) > 0]
    by = {a['id']: a for a in rows}
    pick = (by.get('fcfsbc:ttm') if use_after_sbc else None) or by.get('fcf:ttm') or next((a for a in rows if a['id'].startswith('fcf:')), None)
    return pick, multiple_by_anchor((data.get('quote') or {}).get('value'), rows)


def _eps(anchors, i):
    return next((num(a.get('value')) for a in anchors or [] if a.get('id') == i), None)


def earnings_dislocation(data):
    """Detect a trailing EPS far from what analysts expect next (a trough or peak being recovered or given back). Facts only: the
    numbers, the multiples they produce, and the reasons the gap may not be a real recovery. Returns None when nothing stands out."""
    anchors = data.get('anchors') or []
    cm = data.get('current_multiples') or {}
    ttm = _eps(anchors, 'ttm')
    if ttm is None:
        ttm = num(cm.get('trailing_eps'))
    c0, c1 = _eps(anchors, 'consensus:0y'), _eps(anchors, 'consensus:+1y')
    cons = c0 if c0 and c0 > 0 else (c1 if c1 and c1 > 0 else None)
    if ttm is None or cons is None:
        return None
    pe_t, pe_f = num(cm.get('trailing_pe')), num(cm.get('forward_pe'))
    ends = {a['id']: a.get('period_end') for a in anchors}
    kind, jump = None, None
    if ttm <= 0:
        kind = 'loss_to_profit'
    else:
        jump = cons / ttm - 1
        gap = pe_t / pe_f if pe_t and pe_f and pe_f > 0 else None
        if jump >= 0.5 or (gap is not None and gap >= 1.6 and jump > 0.2):
            kind = 'recovery'
        elif jump <= -0.33:
            kind = 'decline'
    if kind is None:
        return None
    fx = lambda v: f'{v:,.2f}'
    hist = [(r.get('period_end'), num(r.get('diluted_eps'))) for r in data.get('historical') or [] if num(r.get('diluted_eps')) is not None]
    peak = max(hist, key=lambda x: x[1]) if hist else None
    lines = []
    parts = [f'Reported trailing EPS {fx(ttm)}']
    if c0:
        parts.append(f'analyst EPS {fx(c0)} for the year to {ends.get("consensus:0y") or "this fiscal year"}' + (f' ({(c0 / ttm - 1) * 100:+.0f}%)' if ttm > 0 else ''))
    if c1:
        parts.append(f'{fx(c1)} for the year to {ends.get("consensus:+1y") or "next year"}' + (f' ({(c1 / ttm - 1) * 100:+.0f}%)' if ttm > 0 else ''))
    lines.append('; '.join(parts) + '.')
    if pe_t and pe_f:
        lines.append(f'Trailing P/E {pe_t:.1f}× against forward {pe_f:.1f}×: the market is pricing the analysts\' numbers, not the trailing ones.')
    if peak and peak[1] > 0 and peak[0]:
        d = cons / peak[1] - 1
        lines.append(f'Highest annual EPS in the reported table: {fx(peak[1])} ({peak[0]}). Analyst EPS is {abs(d) * 100:.0f}% {"above" if d >= 0 else "below"} it.')
    lines.append('Analyst EPS is usually on an adjusted basis; reported EPS includes impairments and other one-off charges. Part of the gap may be accounting basis, not recovery: check the filing before treating the jump as a business turnaround.')
    return dict(kind=kind, ttm_eps=ttm, cons_0y=c0, cons_1y=c1, jump=jump, pe_trailing=pe_t, pe_forward=pe_f,
                peak_eps=peak[1] if peak else None, peak_end=peak[0] if peak else None, lines=lines)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Free cash flow per share (P/FCF method). Pure functions; the adapter supplies raw provider rows.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def fcf_of(row):
    """Free cash flow = the provider's FCF if given, else operating cash flow minus capital expenditure."""
    f = num((row or {}).get('fcf'))
    if f is None:
        o, c = num((row or {}).get('ocf')), num((row or {}).get('capex'))
        if o is not None and c is not None:
            f = o - abs(c)
    return f


def shares_for(hist_row, fallback):
    h = hist_row or {}
    s = num(h.get('diluted_shares'))
    if s and s > 0:
        return s, 'reported diluted average shares'
    ni, eps = num(h.get('net_income')), num(h.get('diluted_eps'))
    if ni and eps and ni > 0 and eps > 0:
        return ni / eps, 'implied from net income / diluted EPS'
    if fallback and fallback > 0:
        return fallback, 'current shares outstanding (approximation)'
    return None, None


def fcf_anchors(cash_rows, ttm_row, historical, shares_now, currency, ticker, now, source, ttm_end=None):
    """Per-share FCF anchors, before and after stock-based compensation. Returns (anchors, rows).
    rows: one dict per period for the history table (fcf, sbc, shares, per-share values, conversion to net income)."""
    by_end = {r.get('period_end'): r for r in historical or []}
    url = f'https://finance.yahoo.com/quote/{ticker}/'
    anchors, rows = [], []

    def one(tag, end, raw, hist_row, ttm, extra=''):
        fcf = fcf_of(raw)
        if fcf is None:
            return
        sbc = num((raw or {}).get('sbc'))
        sh, how = shares_for(hist_row, shares_now)
        if not sh:
            return
        ps, ps_sbc = fcf / sh, (fcf - sbc) / sh if sbc is not None else None
        ni = num((hist_row or {}).get('net_income'))
        rows.append(dict(period_end=end, ttm=ttm, fcf=fcf, sbc=sbc, shares=sh, shares_basis=how, fcf_per_share=ps,
                         fcf_after_sbc_per_share=ps_sbc, conversion=fcf / ni if ni and ni > 0 else None))
        for kind, val, label in (('fcf', ps, 'free cash flow (operating cash flow − capex)'),
                                 ('fcfsbc', ps_sbc, 'free cash flow after stock-based compensation')):
            if val is None:
                continue
            anchors.append(dict(id=f'{kind}:{tag}', value=val, category='ttm_reported' if ttm else 'historical_reported',
                                source=source, currency=currency, period_end=end, published_at=None, retrieved_at=now,
                                unit='currency_per_share', source_url=url,
                                basis=f'{"Trailing twelve months" if ttm else "Annual"} {label} per share; shares: {how}.{extra}'))

    own = to_date((ttm_row or {}).get('period_end'))     # the cash flow's own period end wins; the latest quarter is only a fallback
    end_ttm = own.isoformat() if own else (str(ttm_end)[:10] if ttm_end else None)
    if ttm_row and end_ttm:
        one('ttm', end_ttm, ttm_row, None, True, '' if own else ' Period end taken from the latest reported quarter: the provider gave no date for this figure.')
    for raw in sorted(cash_rows or [], key=lambda r: str(r.get('period_end')), reverse=True):
        end = str(raw.get('period_end'))[:10]
        one(end, end, raw, by_end.get(end), False)
    return anchors, rows


def fcf_status_quo(rows, price, shares_now=None):
    """Starting growth (latest annual per-share FCF change) and multiple (price / TTM or latest FCF per share)."""
    ann = [r for r in rows if not r.get('ttm') and r.get('fcf_per_share')]
    ann.sort(key=lambda r: str(r['period_end']), reverse=True)
    g = ann[0]['fcf_per_share'] / ann[1]['fcf_per_share'] - 1 if len(ann) > 1 and ann[1]['fcf_per_share'] > 0 and ann[0]['fcf_per_share'] > 0 else None
    cur = next((r for r in rows if r.get('ttm')), ann[0] if ann else None)
    mult = price / cur['fcf_per_share'] if cur and price and cur.get('fcf_per_share') and cur['fcf_per_share'] > 0 else None
    return g, mult


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Multiple references: several rulers for the exit multiple, none of them chosen for the user.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def industry_summary(peers, label):
    """Medians over a peer set. A median needs at least 3 usable values; PEG = forward P/E ÷ expected EPS growth in %."""
    peers = [p for p in peers or [] if not p.get('error')]
    if len(peers) < 3:
        return None

    def med(key):
        v = [p[key] for p in peers if num(p.get(key)) is not None and p[key] > 0]
        return median(v) if len(v) >= 3 else None
    pegs = []
    for p in peers:
        pe, g = num(p.get('forward_pe')), num(p.get('eps_growth'))
        if pe and pe > 0 and g and g > 0.03:
            pegs.append(pe / (g * 100))
    return dict(label=label, count=len(peers), forward_pe=med('forward_pe'), trailing_pe=med('trailing_pe'), ev_ebitda=med('ev_ebitda'),
                price_to_fcf=med('price_to_fcf'), peg=median(pegs) if len(pegs) >= 3 else None, peg_count=len(pegs),
                growth_basis='provider forward EPS ÷ trailing EPS − 1')


===== workbench_v2/app/core/statements.py =====

"""Annual and quarterly statement rows: merge, margins and growth. Pure Python; all arithmetic is done here, not by the model."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

FIELDS = ("revenue", "gross_profit", "operating_income", "net_income", "diluted_eps",
          "operating_cash_flow", "capital_expenditure", "free_cash_flow")


def _n(v: Any) -> float | None:
    return float(v) if isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) else None


def _end(row: dict) -> str | None:
    v = row.get("period_ending")
    return str(v)[:10] if v else None


def merge(income: list[dict], cash: list[dict], n: int) -> list[dict]:
    """Join income and cash-flow rows on period end, newest first, keeping only the fields we use."""
    by: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for src in (income or [], cash or []):
        for row in src:
            e = _end(row)
            if not e:
                continue
            d = by.setdefault(e, {"period_ending": e})
            for k in ("fiscal_year", "fiscal_period"):
                if row.get(k) is not None and d.get(k) is None:
                    d[k] = row[k]
            for k in FIELDS:
                v = _n(row.get(k))
                if v is not None and d.get(k) is None:
                    d[k] = v
    out = [by[k] for k in sorted(by, reverse=True)][:n]
    for d in out:
        if d.get("free_cash_flow") is None and d.get("operating_cash_flow") is not None and d.get("capital_expenditure") is not None:
            capex = d["capital_expenditure"]
            d["free_cash_flow"] = d["operating_cash_flow"] + (capex if capex <= 0 else -capex)
    return out


def _ratio(a, b):
    return round(a / b, 4) if a is not None and b not in (None, 0) else None


def _growth(new, old):
    return round(new / old - 1, 4) if new is not None and old not in (None, 0) and old > 0 else None


def analyse(rows: list[dict], lag: int) -> list[dict]:
    """Add margins and growth. `lag` is how many rows back is the same period a year ago (4 for quarters, 1 for years)."""
    out = []
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        prior = rows[i + lag] if i + lag < len(rows) else None
        prev = rows[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(rows) else None
        d = dict(r)
        d["gross_margin"] = _ratio(r.get("gross_profit"), r.get("revenue"))
        d["operating_margin"] = _ratio(r.get("operating_income"), r.get("revenue"))
        d["net_margin"] = _ratio(r.get("net_income"), r.get("revenue"))
        d["fcf_margin"] = _ratio(r.get("free_cash_flow"), r.get("revenue"))
        d["revenue_yoy"] = _growth(r.get("revenue"), prior.get("revenue")) if prior else None
        d["eps_yoy"] = _growth(r.get("diluted_eps"), prior.get("diluted_eps")) if prior else None
        d["revenue_qoq"] = _growth(r.get("revenue"), prev.get("revenue")) if (prev and lag == 4) else None
        out.append(d)
    return out


def view(statements: dict | None) -> dict:
    s = statements or {}
    return {"annual": analyse(s.get("annual") or [], 1), "quarterly": analyse(s.get("quarterly") or [], 4)}


def latest_quarter_end(statements: dict | None) -> str | None:
    q = (statements or {}).get("quarterly") or []
    return _end(q[0]) if q else None


def quarterly_trend(statements: dict | None) -> dict | None:
    """Last four quarters in a form small enough to send to the model."""
    q = view(statements)["quarterly"][:4]
    if not q:
        return None
    rows = [{k: v for k, v in {"period_end": r["period_ending"], "revenue_yoy": r.get("revenue_yoy"),
                               "gross_margin": r.get("gross_margin"), "operating_margin": r.get("operating_margin"),
                               "fcf_margin": r.get("fcf_margin"), "eps_yoy": r.get("eps_yoy")}.items() if v is not None} for r in q]
    out: dict = {"basis": "single reported quarters, newest first; margins and growth computed in code (growth vs the same quarter a year earlier)",
                 "quarters": rows}
    a, b = q[0].get("operating_margin"), (q[4 - 1].get("operating_margin") if len(q) >= 4 else None)
    if a is not None and b is not None:
        out["operating_margin_change_over_4q_pp"] = round((a - b) * 100, 1)
    return out




===== workbench_v2/app/core/fx.py =====

"""Currency conversion for companies that quote in one currency and report in another (0883.HK: HKD price, CNY accounts).

The valuation arithmetic needs the price and the starting value in the same currency. Rather than refuse such companies,
the reported figures are converted to the quote currency at one spot rate, and that rate is held constant to the exit date.
That is an assumption, and every converted figure says so. Pure functions; the rate itself is fetched in the adapter.
"""
MINOR = {'GBp': ('GBP', 100), 'GBX': ('GBP', 100), 'ZAc': ('ZAR', 100), 'ZAC': ('ZAR', 100), 'ILA': ('ILS', 100)}
EPS_KEYS = ('avg', 'low', 'high', 'yearAgoEps', 'yearAgoRevenue')


def split(code):
    """'GBp' -> ('GBP', 100): the major currency and how many quote units make one of it."""
    return MINOR.get(code, (code, 1))


def plan(fin, quote, lookup):
    """How to turn `fin`-currency figures into `quote`-currency ones.
    lookup(from_major, to_major) -> dict(rate, observed, pair) or None. Returns a dict:
      applied False + reason   (nothing to do, or no rate available)
      applied True + rate, from, to, observed, pair, text."""
    if not fin or not quote:
        return dict(applied=False, needed=False, reason='currency unknown')
    if fin == quote:
        return dict(applied=False, needed=False)
    f_major, f_div = split(fin)
    q_major, q_div = split(quote)
    if f_major == q_major:                      # pence vs pounds: a fixed factor, no market rate needed
        rate, observed, pair = q_div / f_div, None, None
    else:
        got = lookup(f_major, q_major)
        if not got or not got.get('rate') or got['rate'] <= 0:
            return dict(applied=False, needed=True, reason=f'no exchange rate {f_major}→{q_major} could be fetched')
        rate = got['rate'] * q_div / f_div
        observed, pair = got.get('observed'), got.get('pair')
    when = f' (spot, {observed})' if observed else ''
    return dict(applied=True, needed=True, rate=rate, **{'from': fin, 'to': quote}, observed=observed, pair=pair,
                text=f'Reported in {fin}, converted at 1 {fin} = {rate:.4f} {quote}{when}; the rate is held constant to the exit date.')


def conv_rows(rows, rate, keys):
    for r in rows or []:
        for k in keys:
            if isinstance(r.get(k), (int, float)) and not isinstance(r.get(k), bool):
                r[k] = r[k] * rate


def conv_estimates(estimates, p):
    """Consensus rows reported in the financial currency -> quote currency, in place. Keeps the original average."""
    for x in estimates or []:
        if x.get('currency') != p['from']:
            continue
        x['avg_original'] = x.get('avg')
        conv_rows([x], p['rate'], EPS_KEYS)
        x['currency'] = p['to']
        x['fx_from'] = p['from']


===== workbench_v2/app/core/multiples.py =====

"""Where might an exit multiple sit? Reference points for judging the multiple you choose.

Nothing here is a fair value. A multiple is what the market pays for a growth and risk profile, and it changes as that profile
changes: a company growing 20% today may grow 10% in three years and be priced accordingly. So the page shows several anchors
side by side (today, own history, peers you chose, a rough sector band, growth-implied) and lets the user pick.

SECTOR_BANDS are rough long-run forward P/E ranges kept as plain data so they can be edited (reviewed 2026-10). They are
heuristics, not market data. Live peers, when given, take priority over them.
"""
from statistics import median
from .valuation_context import num

SECTOR_BANDS = {  # (low, high) forward P/E
    'Technology': (18, 30), 'Communication Services': (14, 24), 'Consumer Cyclical': (14, 24),
    'Consumer Defensive': (16, 24), 'Healthcare': (15, 25), 'Financial Services': (9, 15),
    'Industrials': (15, 23), 'Energy': (8, 14), 'Utilities': (13, 19), 'Basic Materials': (11, 18),
}
INDUSTRY_BANDS = {
    'Semiconductors': (12, 22), 'Semiconductor Equipment & Materials': (14, 24), 'Auto Manufacturers': (8, 16),
    'Software - Infrastructure': (22, 35), 'Software - Application': (20, 33), 'Discount Stores': (20, 28),
}
CYCLICAL = {'Semiconductors', 'Semiconductor Equipment & Materials', 'Auto Manufacturers', 'Steel', 'Chemicals',
            'Oil & Gas E&P', 'Oil & Gas Integrated', 'Copper', 'Aluminum', 'Airlines'}
# Rough long-run ranges for the other two multiples, same caveats: hand-set rules of thumb (reviewed 2026-10), not market data.
FCF_BANDS = {'Technology': (20, 35), 'Communication Services': (14, 24), 'Consumer Cyclical': (14, 25), 'Consumer Defensive': (18, 28),
             'Healthcare': (16, 26), 'Industrials': (16, 26), 'Energy': (7, 14), 'Basic Materials': (10, 18)}
FCF_INDUSTRY = {'Semiconductors': (15, 30), 'Semiconductor Equipment & Materials': (18, 30), 'Auto Manufacturers': (8, 16)}
EV_BANDS = {'Technology': (14, 24), 'Communication Services': (7, 12), 'Consumer Cyclical': (8, 14), 'Consumer Defensive': (11, 17),
            'Healthcare': (11, 18), 'Industrials': (10, 16), 'Energy': (4, 8), 'Utilities': (9, 13), 'Basic Materials': (6, 10)}
EV_INDUSTRY = {'Semiconductors': (10, 20), 'Semiconductor Equipment & Materials': (12, 20), 'Auto Manufacturers': (5, 10)}
BAND_SOURCE = 'hand-set long-run range, not computed from data; edit in core/multiples.py; peers override it'
PEG = (1.0, 2.0)   # price/earnings-to-growth range used for the growth-implied check


def current_multiple(data, method):
    d = data.get('defaults') or {}
    return {'pe': (d.get('pe') or {}).get('exit_pe'), 'p_fcf': (d.get('p_fcf') or {}).get('exit_multiple'),
            'ev_ebitda': (d.get('ev_ebitda') or {}).get('exit_multiple')}.get(method)


def sector_band(data, method='pe'):
    ind, sec = data.get('industry'), data.get('sector')
    by_ind, by_sec = {'pe': (INDUSTRY_BANDS, SECTOR_BANDS), 'p_fcf': (FCF_INDUSTRY, FCF_BANDS), 'ev_ebitda': (EV_INDUSTRY, EV_BANDS)}[method]
    if ind in by_ind:
        return by_ind[ind], f'industry rule of thumb ({ind})'
    if sec in by_sec:
        return by_sec[sec], f'sector rule of thumb ({sec})'
    return None, None


def peer_values(data, method):
    key = {'pe': 'forward_pe', 'p_fcf': 'price_to_fcf', 'ev_ebitda': 'ev_ebitda'}[method]
    vals = []
    for p in data.get('peers') or []:
        v = num(p.get(key)) if method != 'pe' else (num(p.get('forward_pe')) or num(p.get('trailing_pe')))
        if v and v > 0:
            vals.append(v)
    return vals


def _today_points(data, method):
    """'Today' rows: price divided by each starting value the method can use, so every row says what the market pays on THAT number."""
    d = data.get('defaults') or {}
    key = {'pe': 'pe', 'p_fcf': 'p_fcf'}.get(method)
    mm = (d.get(key) or {}).get('multiple_by_anchor') or {} if key else {}
    ends = {a['id']: a.get('period_end') for a in (data.get('anchors') or []) + (data.get('fcf_anchors') or [])}
    out = []
    if method == 'pe':
        for i, label, basis in (('ttm', 'Today · on trailing EPS', 'Price ÷ the last twelve months of reported EPS'),
                                ('consensus:0y', 'Today · on this year\'s analyst EPS', 'Price ÷ analyst EPS for the year to {end}'),
                                ('consensus:+1y', 'Today · on next year\'s analyst EPS', 'Price ÷ analyst EPS for the year to {end}')):
            if i in mm:
                out.append(dict(key='today', anchor=i, label=label, low=mm[i], high=mm[i],
                                basis=basis.format(end=ends.get(i) or 'that year') + '. Use the row that matches your starting point: holding it flat adds nothing from the multiple.'))
    elif method == 'p_fcf':
        for i, label in (('fcf:ttm', 'Today · before stock compensation'), ('fcfsbc:ttm', 'Today · after stock compensation')):
            if i in mm:
                out.append(dict(key='today', anchor=i, label=label, low=mm[i], high=mm[i], basis='Price ÷ trailing free cash flow per share on this basis. Use the row that matches your starting point.'))
    if not out:
        cur = current_multiple(data, method)
        if cur:
            out.append(dict(key='today', anchor=None, label='Today', low=cur, high=cur, basis='What the market pays now (default exit multiple)'))
    return out


def multiple_context(data, method):
    """Reference points for the chosen method: list of {key,label,low,high,basis}; plus the single `reference` value used when a
    de-rating case is shown. The reference comes only from data: the median of at least 3 peers, else the company's own annual
    P/E history (P/E, at least 3 years). The hand-set sector bands are shown but never enter a calculation."""
    cur = current_multiple(data, method)
    pts, ref, ref_basis = _today_points(data, method), None, None
    peers = peer_values(data, method)
    if len(peers) >= 2:
        lo, hi, med = min(peers), max(peers), median(peers)
        auto = data.get('peer_source', '').startswith('Yahoo')
        pts.append(dict(key='peers', label=f'{"Industry peers" if auto else "Your peers"} ({len(peers)})', low=lo, high=hi, median=med,
                        basis=f'median {med:.1f}x; ' + (data.get('peer_source') + ', the largest companies, not an industry average' if auto else 'a set you chose, not an industry average')
                              + ('' if len(peers) >= 3 else '; fewer than 3 peers, so not used for the de-rating case')))
        if len(peers) >= 3:
            ref, ref_basis = med, f'median of {len(peers)} ' + ('industry peers' if auto else 'your peers')
    if method == 'pe':
        hp = data.get('historical_pe') or {}
        if (hp.get('count') or 0) >= 2 and hp.get('low') is not None:
            pts.append(dict(key='history', label='Own history (annual, trailing)', low=hp['low'], high=hp['high'],
                            basis=f'median {hp["median"]:.1f}x over {hp["count"]} years; fiscal-year-end price over same-year EPS'))
            if ref is None and (hp.get('count') or 0) >= 3 and hp.get('median'):
                ref, ref_basis = hp['median'], 'own historical median (annual, trailing basis)'
    band, why = sector_band(data, method)
    if band:
        pts.append(dict(key='sector', label='Sector rule of thumb', low=band[0], high=band[1], basis=why + '; ' + BAND_SOURCE + '; shown for context, never used in a calculation'))
    notes = []
    if method == 'pe' and data.get('industry') in CYCLICAL:
        notes.append('Cyclical industry: earnings swing with the cycle, so a P/E on peak or trough EPS misleads. Prefer a mid-cycle earnings base or P/FCF, and expect the multiple to look low at the peak.')
    if method == 'ev_ebitda' and data.get('sector') in ('Financial Services', 'Real Estate'):
        notes.append('EV/EBITDA is not meaningful for banks, insurers and REITs.')
    peg, peg_basis = PEG, 'rule of thumb: PEG 1 to 2'
    ind = data.get('industry_ref') or {}
    if ind.get('peg'):
        peg, peg_basis = (round(ind['peg'] * 0.8, 2), round(ind['peg'] * 1.2, 2)), f'peer median PEG {ind["peg"]:.2f} ± 20% ({ind["peg_count"]} peers)'
    return dict(method=method, current=cur, points=pts, reference=ref, reference_basis=ref_basis, peg=peg, peg_basis=peg_basis, notes=notes)


def derate_target(data, method, held):
    """Exit multiple for the de-rating case: the reference if it is below the held multiple, else the held multiple."""
    ctx = multiple_context(data, method)
    ref = ctx['reference']
    if ref is None or held is None:
        return held, None
    return min(held, ref), ctx['reference_basis']


def method_fit(data):
    """How well each method suits this company, with reasons: {method: {level: fit|caution|avoid, reasons: [...]}, 'recommended': id, 'why': text}.
    Rules of thumb on public data (industry, sector, earnings quality, cash conversion, stock compensation); they guide, they do not decide."""
    ind, sec = data.get('industry'), data.get('sector')
    flags = data.get('earnings_quality_flags') or []
    cm = data.get('current_multiples') or {}
    fcf_rows = [r for r in data.get('fcf_history') or []]
    latest = next((r for r in fcf_rows if r.get('ttm')), fcf_rows[0] if fcf_rows else None)
    out = {m: dict(level='fit', reasons=[]) for m in ('pe', 'p_fcf', 'ev_ebitda')}

    def mark(m, level, why):
        rank = {'fit': 0, 'caution': 1, 'avoid': 2}
        if rank[level] > rank[out[m]['level']]:
            out[m]['level'] = level
        out[m]['reasons'].append(why)
    cyc = ind in CYCLICAL
    fin = sec in ('Financial Services', 'Real Estate')
    if cyc:
        mark('pe', 'caution', 'Cyclical industry: EPS at a peak or trough misleads. If you use P/E, normalise the earnings base.')
        mark('ev_ebitda', 'caution', 'Cyclical industry: EBITDA swings with the cycle and ignores heavy capital spending.')
        out['p_fcf']['reasons'].append('Cash flow is also cyclical, but it already nets out heavy capital spending, so it is the steadier base here.')
    if (num(cm.get('trailing_eps')) or 0) <= 0:
        mark('pe', 'avoid', 'Trailing EPS is zero or negative.')
    for f in flags:
        mark('pe', 'caution', 'Reported earnings look unusual versus prior years (possible one-off).')
        break
    dis = data.get('earnings_dislocation')
    if dis and dis.get('kind') in ('recovery', 'decline'):
        mark('pe', 'caution', 'Reported EPS and analyst EPS are far apart (earnings recovering or falling back): start from the analyst year, not trailing earnings, and read the "what you must believe" grid.')
    if fin:
        mark('ev_ebitda', 'avoid', 'Not meaningful for banks, insurers and REITs.')
        mark('p_fcf', 'avoid', 'Free cash flow is not meaningful for banks and insurers.')
    anchors = [a for a in data.get('fcf_anchors') or [] if (a.get('value') or 0) > 0]
    if not anchors:
        mark('p_fcf', 'avoid', 'No positive free cash flow per share.')
    elif latest:
        conv = latest.get('conversion')
        if conv is not None and (conv < .5 or conv > 1.5):
            mark('p_fcf', 'caution', f'Free cash flow is {conv * 100:.0f}% of net income: unusual conversion, check capex timing and one-offs.')
        if latest.get('sbc') and latest.get('fcf') and latest['fcf'] > 0 and latest['sbc'] / latest['fcf'] > .25:
            out['p_fcf']['use_after_sbc'] = True
            mark('p_fcf', 'caution', f'Stock-based compensation is {latest["sbc"] / latest["fcf"] * 100:.0f}% of free cash flow: use the "after stock compensation" starting point.')
    if (data.get('market_baseline') or {}).get('net_debt') is None or not (cm.get('enterprise_to_ebitda')):
        mark('ev_ebitda', 'avoid', 'No net debt or EV/EBITDA from the provider.')
    ec = data.get('ev_check')
    if ec and not ec.get('ok'):
        mark('ev_ebitda', 'avoid', 'Share count, net debt and provider enterprise value do not reconcile (ADR or share-class mismatch).')
    sc = data.get('share_check') or {}
    if sc.get('differs'):
        mark('p_fcf', 'caution', 'Provider share count and market cap / price differ by more than 25% (share classes or ADR ratio); per-share cash flow is approximate.')
    order = ('p_fcf', 'pe', 'ev_ebitda') if cyc else ('pe', 'p_fcf', 'ev_ebitda')
    good = [m for m in order if out[m]['level'] == 'fit'] or [m for m in order if out[m]['level'] == 'caution']
    rec = good[0] if good else None
    why = {'p_fcf': 'cash flow is the steadier, cost-aware base for this company', 'pe': 'earnings are positive and no rule flags them',
           'ev_ebitda': 'the other methods do not fit'}.get(rec)
    out['recommended'] = rec
    out['why'] = why
    return out


===== workbench_v2/app/core/compare.py =====

"""Side-by-side comparison: for each ticker, run every applicable method from data-derived status-quo assumptions and report
annualised price returns on a common horizon. Nothing here is a view or a target; it shows what the company's own numbers,
analyst expectations and today's multiples imply, so different tickers can be put on the same scale.

Defaults start from the furthest analyst year when there is one (else trailing twelve months), grow it at the analyst rate fading
to a long-run rate, and keep today's multiple ON THAT SAME STARTING NUMBER (price ÷ starting value). So holding the multiple flat
adds nothing and the result is the growth, not a change of basis.

Methods are never averaged. A ticker is ranked by its most conservative Base result only when every default input passed its
checks; a result that looks implausible stays visible with its numbers but the row is not ranked, because dropping it would
flatter the ones left."""
from .multiples import derate_target
from .valuation import (Assumptions, EbitdaAssumptions, PfcfAssumptions, calculate)
from .valuation_context import pick_pe_anchor, pfcf_defaults

METHODS = ('pe', 'p_fcf', 'ev_ebitda')
LABEL = {'pe': 'P/E', 'p_fcf': 'P/FCF', 'ev_ebitda': 'EV/EBITDA'}
GROWTH_SOURCE = {'eps_cons': 'analyst EPS growth (next year over this year)', 'rev_cons': 'analyst revenue growth (next year over this year)',
                 'fcf_last': 'last annual free cash flow per share growth', 'rev_last': 'last reported annual revenue growth'}


def _frac(v):
    return None if v is None else v / 100


def _what(anchor):
    kind = {'market_consensus': 'analyst EPS for the year to', 'ttm_reported': 'trailing-twelve-month figure to',
            'historical_reported': 'reported annual figure for the year to'}.get(anchor.get('category'), 'figure to')
    return f'{kind} {anchor.get("period_end")}'


def _growth_text(g, fade_to, source):
    s = f'growth {g}%/yr ({GROWTH_SOURCE.get(source, "provider data")})'
    return s + (f' fading to {fade_to * 100:.1f}%' if fade_to is not None else ', held flat')


def build(data, method, years, required_return, shift, fade_to=None):
    """Return (assumptions, note) or (None, reason)."""
    d = data.get('defaults') or {}
    price = data['quote'].get('value')
    ret = required_return if required_return is not None else 0.0
    shift = shift or 0.0
    if method == 'pe':
        q = d.get('pe') or {}
        anchor = pick_pe_anchor(data)
        if not anchor:
            return None, 'no positive EPS anchor'
        if q.get('growth') is None:
            return None, 'no analyst EPS growth (needs 0y and +1y consensus)'
        if not price or price <= 0:
            return None, 'no price'
        m0 = price / anchor['value']
        if m0 * (1 + shift) > 500:
            return None, f'REVIEW:P/E: today\'s multiple is {m0:.0f}× on the starting EPS, so the base is too small for a meaningful projection.'
        return Assumptions(method='pe', anchor_id=anchor['id'], years=years, required_return=ret, growth=_frac(q['growth']), fade_to=fade_to,
                           exit_pe=m0 * (1 + shift), growth_spread=_frac(q['growth_spread']), pe_spread=_frac(q['pe_spread']),
                           acknowledge_basis=True), \
            f'starts from {_what(anchor)} ({anchor["value"]:.2f}); {_growth_text(q["growth"], fade_to, q.get("growth_source"))}; exit P/E {m0 * (1 + shift):.1f}× = price ÷ that starting EPS' + (f' {shift * 100:+.0f}%' if shift else '')
    if method == 'p_fcf':
        q = d.get('p_fcf') or {}
        after = ((data.get('method_fit') or {}).get('p_fcf') or {}).get('use_after_sbc')
        anchor, _ = pfcf_defaults(data, after)
        if not anchor:
            return None, 'no positive free cash flow per share'
        g, src = q.get('growth'), q.get('growth_source')
        if g is None:
            g, src = (d.get('pe') or {}).get('growth'), 'eps_cons'
        if g is None:
            return None, 'no growth estimate for FCF'
        if not price or price <= 0:
            return None, 'no price'
        m0 = price / anchor['value']
        if m0 * (1 + shift) > 300:
            return None, f'REVIEW:P/FCF: today\'s multiple is {m0:.0f}× on the starting value, so the base is too small for a meaningful projection.'
        proxy = src in ('eps_cons', 'rev_cons')
        return PfcfAssumptions(method='p_fcf', anchor_id=anchor['id'], years=years, required_return=ret, growth=_frac(g), fade_to=fade_to,
                               growth_spread=_frac(q['growth_spread']), share_change=0.0, exit_multiple=m0 * (1 + shift),
                               multiple_spread=_frac(q['multiple_spread']), acknowledge_basis=True), \
            f'starts from {anchor["id"]} {anchor["value"]:.2f}/share ({"after" if anchor["id"].startswith("fcfsbc") else "before"} stock compensation); {_growth_text(g, fade_to, src)}' + \
            ('; there is no free cash flow consensus, so this is a stand-in and not independent of the P/E result' if proxy else '') + f'; exit P/FCF {m0 * (1 + shift):.1f}× = price ÷ that starting value; no share count change'
    q = d.get('ev_ebitda') or {}
    b = data.get('market_baseline') or {}
    anchor = next((a for a in data.get('revenue_anchors') or [] if a['id'] == 'revenue:ttm' and (a.get('value') or 0) > 0), None)
    if not anchor:
        return None, 'no trailing-twelve-month revenue'
    for key, why in (('growth', 'no revenue growth'), ('margin', 'no EBITDA margin'), ('exit_multiple', 'no current EV/EBITDA')):
        if q.get(key) is None:
            return None, why
    if b.get('net_debt') is None or not b.get('shares') or b.get('currency') != data['quote'].get('currency'):
        return None, 'current net debt / shares unavailable or in another currency'
    return EbitdaAssumptions(method='ev_ebitda', anchor_id=anchor['id'], years=years, required_return=ret, growth=_frac(q['growth']), fade_to=fade_to,
                             growth_spread=_frac(q['growth_spread']), margin=_frac(q['margin']), margin_spread=_frac(q['margin_spread']),
                             exit_multiple=q['exit_multiple'] * (1 + shift), multiple_spread=_frac(q['multiple_spread']),
                             target_net_debt=b['net_debt'], target_shares=b['shares'], other_claims=0.0,
                             balance_assumption='hold_current_constant', acknowledge_basis=True), \
        f'starts from trailing revenue; {_growth_text(q["growth"], fade_to, q.get("growth_source"))}; margin {q["margin"]}% ({"trailing, EBITDA ÷ revenue" if q.get("margin_source") == "ttm" else "last reported annual"}); current net debt and shares held constant'


def _run(data, m, years, required_return, shift, fade_to=None):
    a, note = build(data, m, years, required_return, shift, fade_to)
    if a is None:
        return None, note, None
    return a, note, calculate(data, a)


def ticker_row(data, years, required_return=None, shift=0.0, fade_to=None):
    dis = data.get('earnings_dislocation')
    out = dict(ticker=data['ticker'], name=data.get('name'), sector=data.get('sector'), industry=data.get('industry'), price=data['quote'].get('value'), currency=data['quote'].get('currency'),
               methods=[], skipped=[], review=[], flags=list(data.get('earnings_quality_flags') or []),
               dislocation=None if not dis else {k: dis.get(k) for k in ('kind', 'ttm_eps', 'cons_0y', 'cons_1y', 'jump')})
    fit = data.get('method_fit') or {}
    for m in METHODS:
        f = fit.get(m) or {}
        if f.get('level') == 'avoid':
            out['skipped'].append(dict(method=m, reason='not suitable: ' + ' '.join(f.get('reasons') or []))); continue
        try:
            a, note, r = _run(data, m, years, required_return, shift, fade_to)
        except (ValueError, OverflowError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
            a, note, r = None, str(e)[:160], None
        if r is None:
            if note.startswith('REVIEW:'):
                note = note[7:]; out['review'].append(note)
            out['skipped'].append(dict(method=m, reason=note)); continue
        derate, ref_basis, ref_value = None, None, None
        held = a.exit_pe if m == 'pe' else a.exit_multiple
        m0 = held / (1 + (shift or 0))                     # today's multiple on the starting point, before any shift
        target, ref_basis = derate_target(data, m, held)
        if ref_basis and target < held - 1e-9:
            try:
                _, _, rd = _run(data, m, years, required_return, target / m0 - 1, fade_to)
                derate, ref_value = rd, target
            except (ValueError, OverflowError):
                pass
        sc = {x['scenario']: x for x in r['scenarios']}
        implausible = not -.5 <= sc['Base']['price_cagr'] <= .6
        if implausible:
            out['review'].append(f'{LABEL[m]}: Base {sc["Base"]["price_cagr"] * 100:.0f}%/yr from default inputs looks implausible, usually a data mismatch (ADR ratio, share class) or a very small base. Open the company to inspect.')
        if m in ('pe', 'p_fcf') and m0 > 100:
            out['review'].append(f'{LABEL[m]}: today\'s multiple is {m0:.0f}× on the starting value, so the base is too small for a meaningful projection.')
        if m == 'p_fcf' and (data.get('share_check') or {}).get('differs'):
            out['review'].append('P/FCF: the provider share count and market cap ÷ price differ by more than 25% (ADR ratio or several share classes), so per-share cash flow is unverified.')
        out['methods'].append(dict(method=m, label=LABEL[m], anchor=r['anchor']['id'], note=note, growth_years=r['growth_years'],
            bear=sc['Bear']['price_cagr'], base=sc['Base']['price_cagr'], bull=sc['Bull']['price_cagr'],
            base_price=sc['Base']['future_price'], bear_price=sc['Bear']['future_price'], bull_price=sc['Bull']['future_price'],
            warnings=[c['text'] for c in r['checks'] if c['level'] == 'warn'], notes=[c['text'] for c in r['checks'] if c['level'] != 'warn'],
            assumptions=r['assumptions'], implausible=implausible,
            earnings_effect=sc['Base'].get('earnings_effect'), multiple_effect=sc['Base'].get('multiple_effect'),
            exit_multiple=sc['Base'].get('exit_pe', sc['Base'].get('exit_multiple')),
            derate_base=None if derate is None else next(x['price_cagr'] for x in derate['scenarios'] if x['scenario'] == 'Base'),
            derate_multiple=ref_value, reference_basis=ref_basis if derate else None,
            fit=f.get('level', 'fit'), fit_reasons=f.get('reasons', [])))
    ms = out['methods']
    if ms:
        base = [m['base'] for m in ms]
        rec = fit.get('recommended')
        pref = [m for m in ms if m['method'] == rec] or [m for m in ms if m['method'] == 'pe' and not out['flags']] or [m for m in ms if m['method'] != 'pe'] or ms
        out.update(primary=pref[0]['method'], conservative=min(base), dispersion=max(base) - min(base),
                   worst_bear=min(m['bear'] for m in ms),
                   conservative_derate=min([m['derate_base'] if m['derate_base'] is not None else m['base'] for m in ms]), warn_count=sum(len(m['warnings']) for m in ms),
                   disagree=(max(base) - min(base)) > .10)
    return out


def compare(datas, years, required_return=None, shift=0.0, fade_to=None):
    rows = [ticker_row(d, years, required_return, shift, fade_to) for d in datas]
    ranked = sorted([r for r in rows if r['methods'] and not r['review']], key=lambda r: r['conservative'], reverse=True)
    for i, r in enumerate(ranked, 1):
        r['rank'] = i
    rest = [r for r in rows if r['methods'] and r['review']] + [r for r in rows if not r['methods']]
    return dict(years=years, required_return=required_return, multiple_shift=shift, fade_to=fade_to, rows=ranked + rest,
                notes=['Default scenarios: start from the furthest analyst year where there is one (else trailing twelve months), grow it at the analyst rate ' + ('fading linearly to %.1f%% by the exit date' % (fade_to * 100) if fade_to is not None else 'held flat for the whole period') + ', and keep today\'s multiple on that same starting number (price ÷ starting value) ± the shift. '
                       'They show what current numbers imply; they are not forecasts of your own.',
                       'Ranked by the lowest Base annual price return among the applicable methods, with that multiple held. "If the multiple de-rates" repeats the calculation with the exit multiple lowered to a reference when today\'s is above it; the reference is the median of at least 3 peers, else the company\'s own annual P/E history, and a hand-set sector range is never used. Methods are not averaged; a gap above 10 points is flagged.',
                       'A row with an implausible default result, or a share-count mismatch, keeps its numbers but is not ranked: dropping the odd method would make the others look better. Open the company to check.',
                       'Annual price return excludes dividends. Analyst numbers are provider consensus with an unverified reported/adjusted basis; where a turnaround is flagged, part of the gap may be accounting basis. Not a forecast, a rating or a prediction track record.'])


===== workbench_v2/app/models.py =====

"""Core tables. A Review is a full, self-contained thesis state, so any two
reviews can be diffed and the timeline is just the list of reviews."""
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Integer, JSON, String, Text, Index
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column
from .database import Base


def utcnow() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


class Company(Base):
    __tablename__ = "companies"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    ticker: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), unique=True, index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200))
    language: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10), default="en")
    status: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True, default="watching")   # holding|watching|idea|archived
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class Snapshot(Base):
    """Normalized evidence at a point in time (raw provider payload is not stored)."""
    __tablename__ = "snapshots"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    company_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("companies.id"), index=True)
    retrieved_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    price: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    fingerprint: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32))
    payload: Mapped[dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(JSON)


class Review(Base):
    """kind: baseline | rebaseline | update (LLM) | check (deterministic, no LLM).
    level: none | minor | material. view: constructive | neutral | cautious."""
    __tablename__ = "reviews"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    company_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("companies.id"), index=True)
    snapshot_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("snapshots.id"))
    prev_review_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("reviews.id"), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    level: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10))
    level_reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    view: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    view_change: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10))          # new | up | unchanged | down
    one_liner: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    state: Mapped[dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(JSON)           # profile, assumptions, monitor
    changes: Mapped[list | None] = mapped_column(JSON, nullable=True)   # [{what, so_what}]
    narrative: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    delta: Mapped[dict | None] = mapped_column(JSON, nullable=True)
    price: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    language: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10), default="zh-CN")
    provider: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="none")
    model: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), default="")
    prompt_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(60), default="")
    input_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True, index=True)
    usage: Mapped[dict | None] = mapped_column(JSON, nullable=True)     # token counts
    zh: Mapped[dict | None] = mapped_column(JSON, nullable=True)        # {"items": {key: Chinese text}, "model", "usage"}
    __table_args__ = (Index("ix_review_company_time", "company_id", "created_at", "id"),)


class Note(Base):
    __tablename__ = "notes"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    company_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("companies.id"), index=True)
    review_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("reviews.id"), nullable=True)
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), default="comment")   # decision | comment | source | question
    body: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    assumption_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=True)
    price: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)     # captured automatically
    revisit_on: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)   # YYYY-MM-DD
    done: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class AssumptionDriver(Base):
    """Which valuation input a thesis assumption bears on. Chosen by the user; the program never guesses a link."""
    __tablename__ = "assumption_drivers"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    company_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("companies.id"), index=True)
    assumption_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8))
    driver: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    __table_args__ = (Index("ux_driver_company_assumption", "company_id", "assumption_id", unique=True),)


class Holding(Base):
    """Latest imported snapshot of broker positions (replaced on each import)."""
    __tablename__ = "holdings"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    symbol: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)      # data-source symbol, e.g. 9961.HK
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), default="")
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="stock")    # stock | etf | cash
    currency: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(4), default="USD")
    quantity: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    price: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    cost: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)           # broker's diluted cost
    pnl: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    pnl_pct: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    market_value: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    weight: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)         # broker-computed, fraction of account
    imported_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class Trade(Base):
    """Filled quantities only. `key` de-duplicates repeated imports."""
    __tablename__ = "trades"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), unique=True)
    code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    symbol: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), default="")
    side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(4))
    quantity: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    price: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    amount: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    fee: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0)
    currency: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(4), default="USD")
    market: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10), default="")
    executed_at: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), index=True)


class Plan(Base):
    """Your own price discipline for a symbol (works for holdings with no analysis, and for watch-list names)."""
    __tablename__ = "plans"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    symbol: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), unique=True, index=True)
    style: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12), default="investment")      # investment | trade | defensive
    buy_below: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)     # target cost / add zone
    target_price: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    stop_price: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    rules: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")                      # add / trim / exit rules, free text
    target_amount: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)  # position value you intend to hold, in the stock's currency
    max_amount: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)     # position value above which you review concentration
    strategy: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="")              # holding strategy for this position (see Guide)
    rationale: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")                   # the standing logic behind the position; trades inherit it
    rationale_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class TradeReason(Base):
    """Why you made a trade, and how it turned out. One row per imported trade."""
    __tablename__ = "trade_reasons"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    trade_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("trades.id"), unique=True, index=True)
    strategy: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="")           # long_term | swing | short_term | defensive | rebalance
    fundamental: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    technical: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    revisit_on: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)
    review: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")                   # outcome, written later
    reviewed_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    decided_on: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)      # day the decision was made (defaults to the trade day)
    outcome: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="")                    # "" | open | success | mixed | failed
    followup: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")                        # what you will do next
    action: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10), default="")                    # role in the position: initial | add | trim | exit | swap
    from_plan: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)                # logic text was copied from the plan at the time


class JournalEntry(Base):
    """Portfolio-level diary: decisions, periodic reviews, macro ideas, outside sources. Private; never sent to a model."""
    __tablename__ = "journal_entries"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12), default="decision")        # decision | review | macro | source
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), default="")
    body: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    tickers: Mapped[list | None] = mapped_column(JSON, nullable=True)       # symbols this entry is about
    revisit_on: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)
    event_on: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)      # the day a market event happened (war, outbreak, ...)
    done: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    snapshot: Mapped[dict | None] = mapped_column(JSON, nullable=True)      # portfolio as it stood when written
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)




===== workbench_v2/app/database.py =====

from collections.abc import Generator
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Session, sessionmaker
from . import config

_args = {"check_same_thread": False} if config.DATABASE_URL.startswith("sqlite") else {}
engine = create_engine(config.DATABASE_URL, connect_args=_args)
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False, autocommit=False)


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


def get_session() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    s = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield s
    finally:
        s.close()


def normalize_symbols() -> None:
    """One-off fix: BRK.B -> BRK-B in rows imported before symbols were normalised (never overwrites an existing row)."""
    from sqlalchemy import select, update
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    from . import models as m
    from .portfolio import norm_symbol
    with Session(engine) as s:
        have = set(s.scalars(select(m.Company.ticker)))
        for col, model in ((m.Holding.symbol, m.Holding), (m.Trade.symbol, m.Trade), (m.Plan.symbol, m.Plan), (m.Company.ticker, m.Company)):
            for old in set(s.scalars(select(col))):
                new = norm_symbol(old)
                if new != old and not (model is m.Company and new in have) and not (model is m.Plan and s.scalar(select(m.Plan.id).where(m.Plan.symbol == new))):
                    s.execute(update(model).where(col == old).values({col.key: new}))
        s.commit()


def ensure_columns() -> None:
    """Tiny migration: add columns that newer models have but an existing SQLite table lacks."""
    from sqlalchemy import inspect, text
    insp = inspect(engine)
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        for table in Base.metadata.sorted_tables:
            if not insp.has_table(table.name):
                continue
            have = {c["name"] for c in insp.get_columns(table.name)}
            for col in table.columns:
                if col.name not in have:
                    conn.execute(text(f'ALTER TABLE {table.name} ADD COLUMN {col.name} {col.type.compile(engine.dialect)}'))




===== workbench_v2/app/security.py =====

"""Request guards for a local, single-user app. Pure functions so they can be unit-tested without a web server.

Threat model: the server listens on 127.0.0.1 and has no login. The realistic attacks are therefore a web page you visit
in another tab that tries to talk to localhost (cross-site request forgery) or a DNS-rebinding page that makes your
browser treat an attacker's hostname as 127.0.0.1. Both are stopped by checking the Host and Origin headers."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from urllib.parse import urlparse

SAFE_METHODS = {"GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"}
_DEFAULT_HOSTS = {"127.0.0.1", "localhost", "[::1]", "testserver"}


def allowed_hosts() -> set[str]:
    extra = {h.strip().lower() for h in os.getenv("ALLOWED_HOSTS", "").split(",") if h.strip()}
    return _DEFAULT_HOSTS | extra


def hostname(host_header: str | None) -> str:
    h = (host_header or "").strip().lower()
    if h.startswith("["):                       # IPv6 literal, e.g. [::1]:8000
        return h.split("]")[0] + "]"
    return h.rsplit(":", 1)[0] if ":" in h else h


def host_ok(host_header: str | None) -> bool:
    return hostname(host_header) in allowed_hosts()


def origin_ok(method: str, origin: str | None, host_header: str | None) -> bool:
    """State-changing requests that carry an Origin must come from this same host. No Origin (curl, scripts) is allowed."""
    if method.upper() in SAFE_METHODS or not origin:
        return True
    if origin == "null":
        return False
    return urlparse(origin).netloc.lower() == (host_header or "").strip().lower()


MAX_UPLOAD = 5 * 1024 * 1024                    # a broker export is a few hundred KB

CSP = ("default-src 'self'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data:; "
       "connect-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'")
HEADERS = {"X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff", "X-Frame-Options": "DENY", "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer"}
