if(c.changes.length)L.push("","Thesis changes:",...c.changes.map(x=>`- ${x.symbol}: view ${x.view}${x.view_change==="up"?" (upgraded)":x.view_change==="down"?" (downgraded)":""}${x.assumptions.length?"; "+x.assumptions.map(a=>`${a.id} ${a.from||"new"} → ${a.to}`).join(", "):""}`));
if(c.plan_alerts.length)L.push("","Plan alerts:",...c.plan_alerts.map(a=>`- ${a.symbol}: ${JP[a.status]||a.status}`));
L.push("","What changed in my thinking:","","What I will do next, and why:","","What would prove me wrong:","");return L.join("\n")}
const GUIDE=`<div class="card"><h3>How the workbench fits together</h3><ol class="gl"><li><b>Think</b> (Company → Brief, Thesis, Fundamentals). Say why you own or watch a company: the thesis and its assumptions. The model only reads public data and your unverified notes; it never sees holdings, plans or reasons.</li><li><b>Decide</b> (Company → Position &amp; plan). Once per stock: strategy, the position logic (why you hold it and how you will act), target and max position (amounts), price levels. Status (On plan, Near, Over max position…) is computed by code.</li><li><b>Act</b> (Portfolio → import trades). Import filled orders from the broker. Nothing is traded here.</li><li><b>Record the action</b> (Add reason, from Portfolio, Calendar or Company). The position logic is copied from the plan, so you only pick the role (open, add, trim, exit, swap) and what triggered the trade. Several trades can share one entry, or use the plan's "Use this logic" button.</li><li><b>Review the trade</b> (Calendar, due items). At the review date mark the result: Worked, Mixed, Did not work, Too early, and write what happened and what next. Buy: were your judgement and assumptions right? Sell: did it reach the target you set?</li><li><b>Review the portfolio</b> (Overview digest, Journal checklist). A code-built look-back for a period: movers, trades, thesis changes, plan alerts, stale analyses, due reviews. Write the conclusion as a Journal portfolio review.</li><li><b>Calibrate</b> (Portfolio → Trade outcomes). Compare strategies: which kind of trade tends to work for you? Adjust the rules (entry, size, stop, review date) and the thesis, then go round again.</li></ol></div>
<div class="card" style="margin-top:14px"><h3>Modules and what they are for</h3><table class="ov"><thead><tr><th>Module</th><th>What it holds</th><th>Feeds</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Company: Brief / Thesis / Fundamentals / Timeline / Notes</td><td>AI-assisted view of one company, assumptions with health, public numbers, history of changes, your notes</td><td>Overview, Journal checklist</td></tr><tr><td>Position &amp; plan</td><td>Your purpose, target, stop, size for a holding</td><td>Plan alerts, Portfolio</td></tr><tr><td>Portfolio</td><td>Holdings, allocation, trades needing a reason, outcome counts</td><td>Overview, Calendar</td></tr><tr><td>Calendar</td><td>All trades by day, since-trade change, due reviews, journal events; where reasons and results are filled in</td><td>Trade outcomes, Journal</td></tr><tr><td>Journal</td><td>Portfolio-level diary and the period checklist</td><td>Calendar events</td></tr><tr><td>Overview</td><td>Period digest and the company table</td><td>Where to look first</td></tr></tbody></table></div>`;

const CAL={mode:"month",anchor:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),q:"",sd:"",st:"",nr:false,lt:true,ld:true,lj:true,sel:null,data:null};
const dU=s=>new Date(s+"T00:00:00Z"),yU=d=>d.toISOString().slice(0,10),aD=(s,n)=>yU(new Date(dU(s).getTime()+n*864e5));
function calRange(){const a=dU(CAL.anchor);if(CAL.mode==="list"){const f=new Date(Date.UTC(a.getUTCFullYear(),a.getUTCMonth()-35,1)),l=new Date(Date.UTC(a.getUTCFullYear(),a.getUTCMonth()+1,0));return[yU(f),yU(l)]}if(CAL.mode==="week"){const s=aD(CAL.anchor,-a.getUTCDay());return[s,aD(s,6)]}
const f=new Date(Date.UTC(a.getUTCFullYear(),a.getUTCMonth(),1)),l=new Date(Date.UTC(a.getUTCFullYear(),a.getUTCMonth()+1,0));return[aD(yU(f),-f.getUTCDay()),aD(yU(l),6-l.getUTCDay())]}
function calTitle(){const a=dU(CAL.anchor),[s,e]=calRange();return CAL.mode==="list"?"Last 36 months":CAL.mode==="week"?`${s} – ${e}`:a.toLocaleString("en-US",{month:"long",year:"numeric",timeZone:"UTC"})}
function calTrades(d){return((CAL.data.days[d]||{}).trades||[]).filter(t=>(!CAL.q||(t.symbol+" "+(t.name||"")).toLowerCase().includes(CAL.q.toLowerCase()))&&(!CAL.sd||t.side===CAL.sd)&&(!CAL.st||(t.reason&&t.reason.strategy)===CAL.st)&&(!CAL.nr||!t.reason))}
const LEG=`<span class="cc buy">B</span>buy <span class="cc sell">S</span>sell <span class="cc buy nr">○</span>dashed: no reason yet <span class="cc buy">●</span>reason written <span class="cc buy rv">✓</span>reviewed <span class="cc du">⏰</span>review or note due <span class="cc ev">◆</span>journal entry or market event · darker day = more trades · "+N more" = click the day`;
const mark=t=>!t.reason?"○":({success:"✔",failed:"✗",mixed:"◐"})[t.reason.outcome]||(t.reason.reviewed?"✓":"●");
const OC={success:"Worked",mixed:"Mixed",failed:"Did not work",open:"Too early"},ocChip=t=>t.reason&&t.reason.outcome?`<span class="chip oc-${t.reason.outcome}" style="font-size:11px;padding:0 6px">${OC[t.reason.outcome]}</span>`:"";
const SEL=new Set(),selBox=t=>`<input type="checkbox" data-sel="${t.id}" aria-label="Select trade"${SEL.has(t.id)?" checked":""}>`,
selBar=()=>SEL.size?`<div class="banner selbar"><b>${SEL.size} selected</b> <button class="btn sm" data-bulk="1">Add one reason to all</button> <button class="btn ghost sm" data-selclr="1">Clear</button></div>`:"";
const drawSel=()=>document.querySelectorAll(".selbar-h").forEach(x=>x.innerHTML=selBar());
function outcomeCard(o){const tot=o.total;if(!tot.total)return"";const rows=Object.entries(o.by_strategy).sort((a,b)=>b[1].total-a[1].total),rated=d=>d.success+d.mixed+d.failed,nm=k=>k==="none"?"No strategy set":STG[k]||k;
const bar=d=>{const r=rated(d);return r?`<div class="wb" style="min-width:90px" title="${d.success} worked, ${d.mixed} mixed, ${d.failed} did not work"><i style="width:${d.success/r*100}%"></i></div>`:'<span class="sub">—</span>'};
return`<div class="card" style="margin-top:14px"><h3>Trade outcomes ${tp("Counts of trades you rated after the fact. Small samples mislead, so this shows counts, not scores. Rate a trade in its reason dialog: Worked, Mixed, Did not work, or Too early.")}<span class="tag2">${rated(tot)} of ${tot.total} rated</span></h3><div style="overflow:auto"><table class="ov"><thead><tr><th>Strategy</th><th>Trades</th><th>Buy / sell</th><th>Worked</th><th>Mixed</th><th>Did not</th><th>Too early</th><th>Not rated</th><th>Worked share</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(([k,d])=>`<tr style="cursor:default"><td>${E(nm(k))}</td><td>${d.total}</td><td>${d.buy} / ${d.sell}</td><td>${d.success}</td><td>${d.mixed}</td><td>${d.failed}</td><td>${d.open}</td><td>${d.unrated}</td><td>${rated(d)?Math.round(d.success/rated(d)*100)+"%":"—"} ${bar(d)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>${rated(tot)<10?'<div class="sub">Fewer than 10 rated trades: read this as a diary, not as evidence.</div>':""}</div>`}
function chip(t){return`<span class="cc ${t.side} ${t.reason?"":"nr"} ${t.reason&&t.reason.reviewed?"rv":""}" title="${E(t.symbol)} ${t.side} ${t.quantity} @ ${t.price}">${mark(t)} ${t.side==="buy"?"B":"S"} ${E(t.code||t.symbol)}</span>`}
function cell(d,today,inMonth){const D=CAL.data.days[d]||{},tr=calTrades(d),wk=CAL.mode==="week",lim=wk?99:3;
return`<div class="cd${inMonth?"":" out"}${d===today?" today":""}${d===CAL.sel?" sel":""}${wk?" wk":""}" data-day="${d}" style="--n:${Math.min(tr.length,6)}"><div class="dn"><span>${+d.slice(8)}</span><span>${tr.length?tr.length+" trade"+(tr.length>1?"s":""):""}</span></div>
${CAL.lj?(D.events||[]).slice(0,wk?9:1).map(x=>`<span class="cc ev" title="${E(x.title)}">◆ ${E(x.title)}</span>`).join(""):""}${tr.slice(0,lim).map(chip).join("")}${tr.length>lim?`<span class="cc more">+${tr.length-lim} more</span>`:""}${CAL.ld&&(D.due||[]).length?`<span class="cc du">⏰ ${D.due.length} due</span>`:""}</div>`}
function calDetail(){const d=CAL.sel;if(!d)return'<div class="sub">Click a day to see its trades, due items and events.</div>';const D=CAL.data.days[d]||{},tr=calTrades(d);
const pend=tr.filter(t=>!t.reason).map(t=>t.id);return`<h3>${d}</h3><div class="selbar-h">${selBar()}</div>${pend.length>1?`<div class="acts" style="margin:4px 0"><button class="btn ghost sm" data-selall="${pend.join(",")}">Select the ${pend.length} trades without a reason</button></div>`:""}${tr.map(t=>{TR[t.id]=t;return`<div class="card" style="margin:8px 0">${selBox(t)} <span class="chip ${t.side==="buy"?"constructive":"cautious"}">${t.side==="buy"?"Bought":"Sold"}</span> <b><a href="#" data-t="${E(t.symbol)}">${E(t.symbol)}</a></b> ${t.quantity} @ ${CU(t.currency)}${(+t.price).toFixed(2)} ${t.name?`<span class="sub">${E(t.name)}</span>`:""}
<div class="sub">${t.reason&&t.reason.strategy?STG[t.reason.strategy]+" · ":""}${t.since_pct!=null?`${pct(t.since_pct)} ${t.side==="buy"?"since you bought":"since you sold"} (now ${CU(t.currency)}${(+t.now).toFixed(2)})`:"No current price on file"}${t.pre_split?" · pre-split price?":""}${t.round_trip?" · bought and sold within 10 days":""} ${ocChip(t)}</div>
${t.reason?`<div style="margin-top:4px">${E((t.reason.fundamental||t.reason.technical||"").slice(0,300))}${t.reason.review?`<div class="sub">Review: ${E(t.reason.review.slice(0,200))}</div>`:""}${t.reason.followup?`<div class="sub">Next: ${E(t.reason.followup.slice(0,160))}</div>`:""}</div>`:'<div class="sub">No reason recorded yet.</div>'}
<div class="acts" style="margin-top:6px"><button class="btn ghost sm" data-reason="${t.id}">${t.reason?"Edit reason / review":"Add reason"}</button></div></div>`}).join("")||'<div class="sub">No trades on this day (with the current filters).</div>'}
<div class="acts" style="margin:8px 0"><button class="btn ghost sm" data-jnday="${d}">+ Journal note or event for ${d}</button></div>${CAL.ld?(D.due||[]).map(x=>`<div class="banner"><b>Due:</b> ${E(x.symbol)} ${E(x.text)}</div>`).join(""):""}${CAL.lj?(D.events||[]).map(x=>`<div class="banner"><b>${x.is_event?"Event":"Journal"}:</b> ${E(x.title)} ${x.tickers.map(k=>`<span class="chip">${E(k)}</span>`).join(" ")}</div>`).join(""):""}`}
function calSummary(){const all=Object.values(CAL.data.days).flatMap(x=>x.trades),b=all.filter(t=>t.side==="buy").length,top={};all.forEach(t=>top[t.symbol]=(top[t.symbol]||0)+1);const tp3=Object.entries(top).sort((a,c)=>c[1]-a[1]).slice(0,3).map(([k,v])=>`${E(k)} ×${v}`).join(", ");
return`<div class="sub">${all.length} trades in view: ${b} buys, ${all.length-b} sells · ${all.filter(t=>!t.reason).length} without a reason · ${all.filter(t=>t.round_trip).length} in quick round trips${tp3?" · most active: "+tp3:""}</div>`}
function drawCal(){const today=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),[s,e]=calRange(),a=dU(CAL.anchor).getUTCMonth();let h="";
if(CAL.mode==="list"){const all=[];Object.keys(CAL.data.days).sort().reverse().forEach(d=>calTrades(d).forEach(t=>all.push(t)));
h=`<div style="overflow:auto"><div class="selbar-h">${selBar()}</div><div class="acts" style="margin:4px 0"><button class="btn ghost sm" data-selall="${all.filter(t=>!t.reason).map(t=>t.id).join(",")}">Select all ${all.filter(t=>!t.reason).length} shown without a reason</button></div><table class="ov"><thead><tr><th></th><th>Date</th><th>Side</th><th>Symbol</th><th>Qty</th><th>Price</th><th>Strategy</th><th>Since</th><th>Notes</th></tr></thead><tbody>${all.map(t=>{TR[t.id]=t;return`<tr style="cursor:default"><td>${selBox(t)}</td><td>${t.date}</td><td class="${t.side==="buy"?"up":"dn"}">${t.side==="buy"?"Buy":"Sell"}</td><td><b>${E(t.symbol)}</b></td><td>${t.quantity}</td><td>${CU(t.currency)}${(+t.price).toFixed(2)}</td><td>${t.reason&&t.reason.strategy?STG[t.reason.strategy]:'<span class="sub">no reason</span>'} ${mark(t)} ${ocChip(t)}</td><td>${t.since_pct!=null?pct(t.since_pct):"—"}</td><td><button class="btn ghost sm" data-reason="${t.id}">${t.reason?"Edit":"Add reason"}</button>${t.round_trip?' <span class="chip">round trip</span>':""}${t.pre_split?' <span class="chip">pre-split?</span>':""}</td></tr>`}).join("")||'<tr><td colspan="9" class="empty">No trades in this period.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div>`}
else{h='<div class="cal">'+["Sun","Mon","Tue","Wed","Thu","Fri","Sat"].map(x=>`<div class="dh2">${x}</div>`).join("");for(let d=s;d<=e;d=aD(d,1))h+=cell(d,today,CAL.mode==="week"||dU(d).getUTCMonth()===a);h+="</div>"}
$("#calb2").innerHTML=h+(CAL.mode==="list"?"":`<div class="card" style="margin-top:12px" id="cald">${calDetail()}</div>`);$("#cals").innerHTML=calSummary()}
async function calendar(){CUR="cal";T=null;tab="brief";lst();$("#tabs").innerHTML="";const[s,e]=calRange();
$("#head").innerHTML=`<div class="eye">PORTFOLIO</div><h2>Trade calendar</h2><div class="sub">When you acted, and what is still missing. Dashed chips have no reason yet; ● has a reason; ✓ is reviewed; ✔ ◐ ✗ mark a trade that worked, mixed or did not work; ⏰ is a review or note due. Days follow each market's own date.</div>`;
CAL.data=await api(`/calendar?start=${s}&end=${e}`);
$("#view").innerHTML=`<div class="card"><div class="cs">${CAL.mode==="list"?"":`<button class="btn ghost sm" data-cal="prev">‹</button>`}<b style="min-width:150px;text-align:center">${E(calTitle())}</b>${CAL.mode==="list"?"":`<button class="btn ghost sm" data-cal="next">›</button><button class="btn ghost sm" data-cal="today">Today</button>`}
<select id="cmode" aria-label="View">${[["month","Month"],["week","Week"],["list","List"]].map(([k,v])=>`<option value="${k}"${CAL.mode===k?" selected":""}>${v}</option>`).join("")}</select>
<input type="search" id="cq" placeholder="Symbol…" value="${E(CAL.q)}" style="width:110px" aria-label="Symbol"><select id="csd" aria-label="Side">${opts([["","Buy & sell"],["buy","Buys"],["sell","Sells"]],CAL.sd)}</select><select id="cst" aria-label="Strategy">${opts([["","Any strategy"],...Object.entries(STG)],CAL.st)}</select>
<label><input type="checkbox" id="cnr"${CAL.nr?" checked":""}> No reason only</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="cld"${CAL.ld?" checked":""}> Due</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="clj"${CAL.lj?" checked":""}> Journal &amp; events</label></div><div class="sub lgd" style="margin:4px 0">${LEG} ${tp("How to read the calendar.\nChips: B = buy, S = sell, followed by the symbol.\nDashed outline and ○: no reason written yet.\n●: a reason exists. ✓ and bold: reviewed.\n⏰: a trade review, note or journal item is due that day.\n◆: a journal entry or a market event you logged.\nA darker day has more trades (count, not amount).\nClick a day for details, or the + button there to log a journal note or event on that date.")}</div><div id="cals"></div><div id="calb2" style="margin-top:8px"></div></div>`;drawCal()}
async function companies(){CL=await api("/companies");lst();api("/health").then(v=>{$("#ver").textContent="v"+v.version}).catch(()=>0);api("/usage").then(u=>{$("#usage").textContent=u.calls?`AI analyses today: ${u.calls} (${Object.entries(u.by_model).map(([m,c])=>m.replace("gemini-","")+" "+c).join(", ")})`:"No AI analyses today"}).catch(()=>0)}
function head(b){const q=b?.quote||{},us=v=>v==null?"—":(!q.currency||q.currency==="USD"?"$":"")+(+v).toFixed(2),ch=q.day_change_pct;
$("#head").innerHTML=`<div class="hd"><div><div class="eye">COMPANY RESEARCH WORKSPACE</div><h2>${E(T)}${b?` <span>· ${E(b.name)}</span>`:""}</h2>${q.sector?`<div class="sub">${E(q.sector)}${q.industry?" · "+E(q.industry):""}</div>`:""}
<div class="tags"><a href="${E(su(q.yahoo_url)||"https://finance.yahoo.com/quote/"+encodeURIComponent(T))}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Yahoo Finance ↗</a>${q.market_cap?`<span>Mkt cap ${big(q.market_cap,CU(q.currency))}</span>`:""}${q.target?`<span>Analyst target ${CU(q.currency)}${(+q.target).toFixed(2)}${q.target_upside_pct!=null?` (${pct(q.target_upside_pct)})`:""}</span>`:""}${b?`<span>Analysed ${dt(b.analysed_at)}</span>`:""}</div></div>
<div class="px">${q.price!=null?`<div class="price">${us(q.price)}</div><small class="${ch>=0?"up":"dn"}">${ch!=null?pct(ch)+" today":"&nbsp;"}</small>`:""}${q.year_low!=null?`<small>52W ${us(q.year_low)} – ${us(q.year_high)}</small>${rb(q.year_low,q.year_high,q.price)}`:""}<div class="acts" style="justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:10px">${b?`<select id="st" aria-label="Group">${GO.map(g=>`<option value="${g}" ${b.status===g?"selected":""}>${GN[g]}</option>`).join("")}</select><button class="btn ghost sm" id="del">Delete</button>`:""}<button class="btn ghost sm" id="vl" title="Show the analysis text in Chinese (translated on demand; numbers, tickers and labels stay English)">${VL==="zh"?"中文 ✓":"中文"}</button><a class="btn ghost sm" href="/valuation?ticker=${encodeURIComponent(T)}">Valuation</a><button class="btn" id="upd">Update</button></div></div></div>`;

async function refresh(force){if(!T)return;if(!$("#upd"))head(null);const b=$("#upd");b.disabled=true;b.textContent="Analysing…";try{const r=await api(`/companies/${T}/refresh`,{method:"POST",headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json"},body:JSON.stringify({language:lang,force_rebaseline:!!force})});toast(r.llm_called?"Analysis complete: "+r.level_reason:"No thesis-relevant change; no model call. "+r.level_reason)}catch(e){toast(e.message);await companies();if(!CL.some(c=>c.ticker===T))return home()}await open(T)}
async function open(t){T=t;await companies();show()}

Numbers, tickers and labels stay in English.</span></div>`:"")+await({brief:()=>brief(b),plan:()=>planTab(b),thesis,val:valTab,fund,time,notes})[tab]();if(tab==="plan")planVal()}catch(e){v.innerHTML=`<div class="empty">${E(e.message)}</div>`}}
function brief(b){const n=Object.entries(b.health_counts),tot=n.reduce((s,[,c])=>s+c,0)||1,ch=b.top_changes||[],pc=b.price_since_analysis_pct;
return`${((b.data_notes||{}).gaps||[]).map(g=>`<div class="banner">Data note: ${E(g)}</div>`).join("")}${(b.due_notes||[]).map(n=>`<div class="banner"><b>Revisit due ${E(n.revisit_on)}:</b> ${E(n.body)}</div>`).join("")}${b.rebaseline_suggested?'<div class="banner">The model suggests rebuilding the thesis (the business model or management may have changed).<button class="btn ghost" id="rb" style="margin-left:8px">Rebuild baseline</button></div>':""}
<div class="card hero"><span class="chip ${b.view}">${VW[b.view]}${VC[b.view_change]?" · "+VC[b.view_change]:""}</span>${tp(GL.view)} <span class="chip" title="${E(b.health_reason)}" style="margin-left:6px;color:var(--${b.health==="At risk"?"bad":b.health==="Watch"?"warn":"up"})">Thesis: ${E(b.health)}</span>${tp(GL.health)}${b.plan_status?" "+pchip(b.plan_status):""}<div class="one">${E(b.one_liner)}</div>
<div class="strip">${n.map(([k,c])=>`<i class="${k}" style="--n:${c}" title="${ST[k]} ${c}"></i>`).join("")}</div>
<div class="legend">${n.map(([k,c])=>`<span class="${k}"><i class="dot"></i>${ST[k]} ${c}/${tot}</span>`).join("")}</div>
<div class="meta" style="margin-top:12px">Analysed ${dt(b.analysed_at)} · Last checked ${dt(b.checked_at)}${b.last_check_level==="none"&&b.meta.kind!=="baseline"?" · no thesis-relevant change":""}${pc!=null&&Math.abs(pc)>=.005?` · price ${pct(pc)} since analysis`:""}</div></div>
<div class="card"><h3>Recent changes</h3>${ch.length?ch.map(c=>`<div class="row"><b>${E(c.what)}</b><span class="sub">${E(c.so_what)}</span></div>`).join(""):'<div class="sub">Baseline analysis — no changes recorded yet.</div>'}</div>
${b.valuation&&b.valuation.implication?`<div class="card"><h3>What the price implies</h3><div>${E(b.valuation.implication)}</div>${b.valuation.consensus_gap?`<div class="sub" style="margin-top:6px">Where this differs from the market: ${E(b.valuation.consensus_gap)}</div>`:""}</div>`:""}
<div class="card"><h3>Core assumptions${tp(GL.status)}</h3>${b.assumptions.map(a=>`<div class="row ${a.status}"><span class="chip" style="color:var(--c)">${ST[a.status]}</span>${a.prev_status&&a.prev_status!==a.status?` <span class="sub">was ${ST[a.prev_status]}</span>`:""} ${E(a.statement)}${ad(a)}<div class="sub">Kill criteria: ${E(a.kill_criteria)}</div></div>`).join("")}</div>
<div class="card"><h3>What to watch next</h3>${b.monitor.map(m=>`<div class="row"><b>${E(m.indicator)}</b><span class="sub">${E(m.trigger)}${m.why_this_level?" ("+E(m.why_this_level)+")":""} · ${E(m.why)}</span></div>`).join("")}</div>
<div class="card"><h3>Recent headlines</h3>${(b.news||[]).map(n=>`<div class="row">${su(n.url)?`<a class="nl" href="${E(su(n.url))}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${E(n.title)}</a>`:E(n.title)}${n.is_new?' <span class="chip" style="color:var(--pri);font-size:11px;padding:0 7px">New</span>':""}<div class="sub">${E(n.source||"")}${n.date?" · "+E(String(n.date).slice(0,10)):""}</div></div>`).join("")||`<div class="sub">No headlines. ${E(((b.data_notes||{}).warnings||[]).filter(w=>/news/i.test(w)).join(" ")||"The data source returned no news for this ticker.")}</div>`}<div class="sub" style="margin-top:6px">"New" = not seen at the last analysis.</div></div>
<details><summary>Analysis details</summary><div class="meta">model ${E(b.meta.model||"—")} · prompt ${E(b.meta.prompt_version)} · review #${b.meta.review_id} · tokens ${E(JSON.stringify(b.meta.usage||{}))}</div></details>`}
const su=u=>/^https?:\/\//i.test(String(u||""))?String(u):"";
const md=t=>String(t||"").split("\n").map(l=>/^#+\s/.test(l)?`<h4>${E(l.replace(/^#+\s/,""))}</h4>`:l.trim()?`<p>${E(l).replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g,"<b>$1</b>")}</p>`:"").join("");
const VKIND={market_consensus:"analyst EPS year",ttm_reported:"trailing twelve months",historical_reported:"past reported year"},DRV={eps_growth:"Earnings or cash-flow growth",revenue_growth:"Revenue growth",margin:"Margins",multiple:"What the market will pay (exit multiple)",shares:"Share count or dilution",balance:"Net debt or balance sheet"};
let VS=null;const rr=v=>v==null?"—":(v*100).toFixed(1)+"%",f2=v=>v==null?"—":(+v).toFixed(2),fl2=v=>Math.floor(v*100)/100;
function staleBox(s){const seen=new Set(),items=[];for(const a of s.analyses||[])for(const x of a.stale||[]){if(!seen.has(x.text)){seen.add(x.text);items.push(x.text)}}if(!items.length)return"";
return`<div class="banner"><b>Your saved valuation may be out of date.</b><ul style="margin:4px 0 0 18px;padding:0">${items.map(t=>`<li>${E(t)}</li>`).join("")}</ul><div class="sub">Nothing was changed. Open the valuation lab, import again and save a new snapshot; the old one stays.</div></div>`}
function drvCard(s){const rows=s.drivers||[];return`<div class="card"><h3>What each valuation input rests on ${tp("You link a thesis assumption to the valuation input it bears on (Thesis page, under each assumption). If a linked assumption is weakened or broken, the methods that use that input are flagged as possibly out of date. The program never guesses a link.")}</h3>${rows.some(r=>r.assumptions.length)?`<div style="overflow:auto"><table class="ov"><thead><tr><th>Valuation input</th><th>Used by</th><th>Thesis assumptions you linked</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(r=>`<tr style="cursor:default"><td>${E(r.label)}</td><td class="sub">${r.methods.map(E).join(", ")}</td><td>${r.assumptions.length?r.assumptions.map(a=>`<div><span class="chip" style="color:var(--c);font-size:12px" title="${E(ST[a.status]||a.status||"")}"><span class="${a.status}"><i class="dot"></i></span>${E(a.id)}</span> ${E(a.statement)}</div>`).join(""):'<span class="sub">none linked</span>'}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`:'<div class="sub">No assumptions linked yet. On the Thesis page, choose under each assumption which valuation input it bears on.</div>'}</div>`}
async function valTab(){const s=await api(`/companies/${T}/valuation`);VS=s;const cu=CU(s.currency),lab=`/valuation?ticker=${encodeURIComponent(T)}${VL==="zh"?"&lang=zh":""}`;
const head=`<div class="card"><h3>Valuation <span class="tag2">from the valuation lab</span><a class="btn ghost sm" style="float:right" href="${lab}">Open the lab</a></h3><div class="sub">Read-only here. These are the numbers you saved in the lab, set against your thesis and your plan. Nothing on this page changes the plan, and none of it is sent to a model.</div></div>`;
if(!s.available)return head+`<div class="card"><div class="sub">No valuation data for ${E(T)} yet. Open the lab, import the latest data and save a snapshot.</div></div>`+drvCard(s);
const dk=s.dislocation,dis=dk?`<div class="card"><h3>${({recovery:"Earnings expected to recover",loss_to_profit:"A loss expected to turn to profit",decline:"Earnings expected to fall back"})[dk.kind]||"Reported and expected earnings differ a lot"}</h3><div class="kv"><span>Trailing EPS</span><b>${f2(dk.ttm_eps)}</b></div><div class="kv"><span>Analysts · this year / next year</span><b>${f2(dk.cons_0y)} / ${f2(dk.cons_1y)}</b></div><div class="kv"><span>P/E trailing / forward</span><b>${Xx(dk.pe_trailing)} / ${Xx(dk.pe_forward)}</b></div><div class="sub" style="margin-top:6px">The price already reflects the analysts' numbers. A multiple measured on the low trailing earnings would count the recovery twice, so the lab starts from the analyst year and shows what you would have to believe. Whether the recovery happens is a thesis question; analyst EPS is usually adjusted while reported EPS includes one-offs.</div></div>`:"";
const A=s.analyses||[],tpx=s.price_now;
const imp=A.length?`<div class="card"><h3>What your saved assumptions imply ${tp("Annual price return from today's price to the Base price you saved for the exit date. Bear and Bull are your own ranges, not probabilities. Dividends are not included. This is arithmetic on your assumptions, not a forecast.")}</h3><div style="overflow:auto"><table class="ov"><thead><tr><th>Method</th><th>Starts from</th><th>Growth</th><th>Exit multiple</th><th>Base return a year (Bear to Bull)</th><th>Saved</th></tr></thead><tbody>${A.map(a=>{const n=a.now||{};return`<tr style="cursor:default"><td><b>${E(a.label)}</b></td><td class="sub">${E(VKIND[a.anchor_kind]||"—")}${a.anchor_end?" to "+E(a.anchor_end):""}</td><td>${rr(a.growth)}${a.fade_to!=null?" → "+rr(a.fade_to):" flat"}</td><td>${Xx(a.exit_multiple)}</td><td><b class="${n.base!=null&&a.required_return!=null&&n.base>=a.required_return?"up":"dn"}">${rr(n.base??a.base)}</b> <span class="sub">(${rr(n.bear??a.bear)} to ${rr(n.bull??a.bull)}) over ${a.years}y, against your ${rr(a.required_return)} requirement${n.base!=null&&a.price_at_save&&Math.abs(tpx/a.price_at_save-1)>.005?` · at the price when saved (${cu}${f2(a.price_at_save)}): ${rr(a.base)}`:""}</span></td><td class="sub">${dt(a.saved_at)}${(a.stale||[]).length?' <span class="chip" style="color:var(--warn);font-size:12px">may be out of date</span>':""}</td></tr>`}).join("")}</tbody></table></div></div>`:
`<div class="card"><h3>No saved valuation yet</h3><div class="sub">You have not saved a calculation for ${E(T)}. The lab's starting point, from the company's own numbers and not your assumptions, is below; open the lab to make it yours and save it.</div>${s.status_quo?`<div style="overflow:auto;margin-top:8px"><table class="ov"><thead><tr><th>Method</th><th>Base a year (Bear to Bull)</th><th>How</th></tr></thead><tbody>${s.status_quo.methods.map(m=>`<tr style="cursor:default"><td><b>${E(m.label)}</b></td><td>${rr(m.base)} <span class="sub">(${rr(m.bear)} to ${rr(m.bull)})</span></td><td class="sub">${E(m.note)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div><div class="sub">${s.status_quo.years} years, ${rr(s.status_quo.required_return)} required, growth fading to ${rr(s.status_quo.fade_to)}.</div>`:""}</div>`;
const pl=s.plan||{},buy=pl.buy_below,pr=A.length?`<div class="card"><h3>At your plan price ${tp("'Return at your buy-below' uses your saved Base exit price and the price in your plan. 'Most you could pay' is the Base exit price divided by (1 + your required return) for the years held: the highest entry that still earns your requirement if the Base case happens. It is arithmetic, not a price target. The button copies it into the plan form; nothing is saved until you save the plan.")}</h3>${buy?"":'<div class="sub">Your plan has no buy-below price yet. The figures below show the most you could pay.</div>'}<div style="overflow:auto"><table class="ov"><thead><tr><th>Method</th><th>Base price at exit</th>${buy?`<th>Base return at ${cu}${buy}</th>`:""}<th>Most you could pay for your requirement</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${A.map(a=>{const b=a.at_buy_below||{},mx=(a.now||{}).max_entry;return`<tr style="cursor:default"><td><b>${E(a.label)}</b></td><td>${cu}${f2(a.base_price)} <span class="sub">${E(a.exit_date||"")}</span></td>${buy?`<td><b class="${b.base!=null&&b.base>=a.required_return?"up":"dn"}">${rr(b.base)}</b> <span class="sub">(${rr(b.bear)} to ${rr(b.bull)})</span></td>`:""}<td><b>${mx!=null?cu+f2(mx):"—"}</b> <span class="sub">for ${rr(a.required_return)} a year</span>${a.target_vs_base!=null?`<div class="sub">Your target ${cu}${f2(pl.target_price)} is ${rr(Math.abs(a.target_vs_base))} ${a.target_vs_base>=0?"above":"below"} this Base exit price.</div>`:""}</td><td>${mx!=null?`<button class="btn ghost sm" data-usebuy="${fl2(mx)}">Use ${cu}${fl2(mx).toFixed(2)} as buy-below</button>`:""}</td></tr>`}).join("")}</tbody></table></div><div class="sub">A lower entry price raises the return only if the Base case happens. Your plan is not changed by the lab.</div></div>`:"";
const qc=s.question?`<div class="card"><h3>A question to write down ${tp("Made from your saved numbers. Edit the wording, pick a date to look again if you like, and save it to Notes as a question. Questions you save may be passed to the model on the next analysis, labelled as unverified, so keep holdings and amounts out of it.")}</h3><div class="sub">The valuation leaves something you can check. This is a suggestion; change it to your own words.</div><textarea id="vqtxt" rows="3" style="width:100%;margin-top:6px">${E(s.question)}</textarea><div class="acts" style="margin-top:6px"><label class="sub">Look again on <input type="date" id="vqdate"></label> <button class="btn ghost sm" data-vq="1">Save to Notes as a question</button></div></div>`:"";
return head+staleBox(s)+dis+imp+qc+pr+drvCard(s)}

el.innerHTML=`<div class="kv"><span>Valuation (${E(a.label)}, saved)</span><b>Base ${rr(n.base??a.base)} a year${b&&b.base!=null?` · at your buy-below ${rr(b.base)}`:""}</b></div><div class="sub">${n.max_entry!=null?`Most you could pay for ${rr(a.required_return)} a year: ${cu}${f2(n.max_entry)}. `:""}${(a.stale||[]).length?'<span class="chip" style="color:var(--warn);font-size:12px">may be out of date</span> ':""}<a href="#" data-vtab="1">See the Valuation tab</a></div>`}catch(e){}}

async function thesis(){const [t,vs]=await Promise.all([api(LQ(`/companies/${T}/thesis`)),api(`/companies/${T}/valuation`).catch(()=>null)]),p=t.profile,f=(l,v)=>`<div class="row"><b>${l}</b><span class="sub">${v}</span></div>`,AD=(vs&&vs.assumption_drivers)||{};
return`${vs&&vs.available?staleBox(vs):""}<div class="card"><h3>Assumptions & status history</h3>${t.assumptions.map(a=>`<div class="row ${a.status}"><span class="chip" style="color:var(--c)">${ST[a.status]}</span> ${E(a.statement)}${ad(a)}<div class="sub">Kill criteria: ${E(a.kill_criteria)}</div><div class="sub">Valuation input this bears on${tp("Optional. Link this assumption to the valuation input it affects, so a weakened or broken assumption flags the valuation methods that rest on it. Only you set this; nothing is guessed.")} <select data-drv="${E(a.id)}" aria-label="Valuation input for ${E(a.id)}"><option value="">not linked</option>${Object.entries(DRV).map(([k,l])=>`<option value="${k}"${AD[a.id]===k?" selected":""}>${E(l)}</option>`).join("")}</select></div>${a.evidence.length?`<div class="sub">Evidence: ${a.evidence.map(E).join("; ")}</div>`:""}${(a.notes||[]).map(n=>`<div class="sub note">✎ ${E(n.body)}</div>`).join("")}<div class="legend" style="margin-top:6px"><span class="sub">Status at each analysis, oldest → latest:</span> ${a.history.map(h=>`<span class="${h.status}" title="${dt(h.at)} · ${ST[h.status]}"><i class="dot"></i></span>`).join("")}</div></div>`).join("")}</div>

<details class="card rep"><summary>Full baseline report</summary>${VL==="zh"&&!t.report_zh&&t.report_review_id?`<div class="banner">The baseline report is a separate, older analysis and has not been translated. <button class="btn sm" data-trl="${t.report_review_id}">Translate baseline report</button></div>`:""}${md(t.report_markdown)}</details>`}
function stmTable(rows,title,tag,sy="$"){if(!rows||!rows.length)return"";const c0=rows.slice().reverse();c0.forEach(r=>{r._cc=r.free_cash_flow!=null&&r.net_income>0?r.free_cash_flow/r.net_income:null});const per=r=>E(r.fiscal_period&&r.fiscal_year?`${r.fiscal_year} ${r.fiscal_period}`:String(r.period_ending||"").slice(0,7)),
L=[["Revenue","revenue",v=>big(v,sy)],["Growth vs year earlier","revenue_yoy",Pg],["Gross margin","gross_margin",Pc],["Operating margin","operating_margin",Pc],["Net income","net_income",v=>big(v,sy)],["Free cash flow","free_cash_flow",v=>big(v,sy)],["Free cash flow ÷ net income","_cc",Xx],["Diluted EPS","diluted_eps",v=>(+v).toFixed(2)],["EPS growth vs year earlier","eps_yoy",Pg]].filter(([,k])=>c0.some(r=>r[k]!=null)),
c=c0.filter(r=>L.some(([,k])=>r[k]!=null));
return`<div class="card"><h3>${title} <span class="tag2">${tag}</span></h3><div style="overflow:auto"><table class="ov"><thead><tr><th></th>${c.map(r=>`<th title="${E(r.period_ending||"")}">${per(r)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${L.map(([l,k,f])=>`<tr style="cursor:default"><td>${l}</td>${c.map(r=>`<td>${r[k]==null?"—":f(r[k])}</td>`).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div></div>`}
async function fund(){const d=await api(`/companies/${T}/fundamentals`),N=d.now,A=d.at_analysis||{},px=N.market?.current_price,an=N.analyst_consensus||{},fh=N.financial_health||{},S=CU(d.currency);
const card=([t,n,rows])=>{const body=rows.map(([sec,k,l,f])=>{const v=N[sec]?.[k];if(v==null)return"";const o=A[sec]?.[k],c=o!=null&&Math.abs(v-o)>Math.abs(o)*.01;return`<div class="kv"><span>${l}</span><b>${f(v)}</b>${c?`<small>was ${f(o)}</small>`:""}</div>`}).join("");return body?`<div class="card"><h3>${t} <span class="tag2">${n}</span></h3>${body}</div>`:""};
const cf=(fh.cash_flow_history||[]).filter(r=>r.free_cash_flow!=null).slice().reverse(),mx=Math.max(1,...cf.map(r=>Math.abs(r.free_cash_flow)));
const up=an.target_consensus&&px?an.target_consensus/px-1:null;
const cons=an.target_consensus!=null||an.target_median!=null?`<div class="card"><h3>Analyst consensus <span class="tag2">Forward-looking${an.total_analysts?` · ${an.total_analysts} analysts`:""}</span></h3>${[["Consensus target",an.target_consensus],["Median target",an.target_median],["High",an.target_high],["Low",an.target_low]].map(([l,v])=>v==null?"":`<div class="kv"><span>${l}</span><b>${Dl(v,S)}</b></div>`).join("")}${up!=null?`<div class="kv"><span>Implied vs price</span><b class="${up>=0?"up":"dn"}">${pct(up)}</b></div>`:""}${rb(an.target_low,an.target_high,px,an.target_consensus)}<div class="sub">Line = current price · dot = consensus</div></div>`:"";
const imp=(()=>{const dv=d.derived||{},DV=[["fcf_yield","FCF yield",Pc],["ev_to_fcf","EV / FCF",Xx],["earnings_yield_ttm","Earnings yield (TTM)",Pc],["earnings_yield_forward","Earnings yield (forward)",Pc],["consensus_implied_eps_change","Consensus-implied EPS change",Pc]],rows=DV.filter(([k])=>dv[k]).map(([k,l,f])=>`<div class="kv"><span>${l}<small style="display:block">${E(dv[k].basis)}</small></span><b>${f(dv[k].value)}</b></div>`).join("");return rows||dv.cyclical_flag?`<div class="card"><h3>What the price implies <span class="tag2">Computed in code</span></h3>${rows}${dv.cyclical_flag?`<div class="banner" style="margin:8px 0 0">Cyclical (${E(dv.cyclical_flag.matched)}): ${E(dv.cyclical_flag.caution)}</div>`:""}</div>`:""})();
const st=d.statements,ann=st?stmTable(st.annual,"Annual results","Up to 5 fiscal years",S):"",qtr=st?stmTable(st.quarterly,"Quarterly results","Last 8 reported quarters",S):"";
const fcf=cf.length?`<div class="card"><h3>Free cash flow <span class="tag2">Annual</span></h3>${cf.map(r=>`<div class="cf"><span>${E(r.fiscal_year||String(r.period_ending||"").slice(0,4))}</span><i class="${r.free_cash_flow<0?"neg":""}" style="width:${Math.abs(r.free_cash_flow)/mx*60}%"></i><b>${big(r.free_cash_flow,S)}</b></div>`).join("")}</div>`:"";
const sec=(n,t,s)=>`<div class="fsec"><h2>${n} · ${t}</h2><div class="sub">${s}</div></div>`;
return`<div class="sub">Read top to bottom: what you pay, what you get for it, then the record. “was” shows the value at your last analysis when it has moved more than 1%.</div>
${sec(1,"What you pay","Multiples, what analysts expect, and what the price itself implies.")}<div class="fgrid">${card(G[0])}${imp}${cons}</div>
${sec(2,"What you get","Growth, profitability and balance sheet from the latest reported figures. n/m = too large to read as a rate (usually a very low base).")}<div class="fgrid">${G.slice(1).map(card).join("")}</div>
${sec(3,"The record","Results by year and by quarter, oldest on the left.")}${st?`<div class="fpair">${ann||qtr}${fcf}</div>${ann&&qtr?qtr:""}`:'<div class="card"><div class="sub">Not loaded yet. Refresh this company once to fetch eight quarters and five years.</div></div>'+fcf}
${d.currency&&d.currency!=="USD"?`<div class="sub" style="margin-top:8px">Amounts are shown in ${E(d.currency)}. Statement figures are in the company's reporting currency, which can differ from the trading currency.</div>`:""}
<div class="meta" style="margin-top:12px">Data as of ${dt(d.as_of)} · Yahoo Finance via OpenBB.</div>`}

const items=[...rev.map(x=>({t:x.created_at,r:x})),...tr.map(x=>({t:x.executed_at,tr:x})),...jn.map(x=>({t:x.created_at,j:{k:JK[x.kind],title:x.title,body:x.body,at:x.created_at}})),...nt.items.map(x=>({t:x.created_at,j:{k:"Note · "+(NK[x.kind]||x.kind),title:"",body:x.body,at:x.created_at}}))].sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.t)-new Date(a.t));
return`<div class="tl">${items.map(it=>{if(it.j){const j=it.j;return`<div class="node jn"><b>${dt(j.at)} · ${E(j.k)}</b>${j.title?" "+E(j.title):""}<div class="sub">${E((j.body||"").slice(0,160))}</div></div>`}if(it.tr){const x=it.tr;return`<div class="node trade"><b>${dt(x.executed_at)} · ${x.side==="buy"?"Bought":"Sold"} ${x.quantity} @ ${CU(x.currency)}${(+x.price).toFixed(2)}</b>${x.reason&&x.reason.strategy?` <span class="chip">${STG[x.reason.strategy]}</span>`:""}<div class="sub">${x.reason?E((x.reason.fundamental||x.reason.technical||"").slice(0,160)):`<button class="btn ghost sm" data-reason="${x.id}">Add reason</button>`}</div></div>`}const x=it.r,pc=x._pc;return`<div class="node ${x.kind}" data-id="${x.id}" style="--c:var(--${x.view==="constructive"?"up":x.view==="cautious"?"warn":"hold"})"><b>${dt(x.created_at)} · ${KD[x.kind]}</b>${x.kind!=="check"?` <span class="chip ${x.view}">${VW[x.view]}${VC[x.view_change]?" "+VC[x.view_change]:""}</span>`:""} ${(x.what_changed||[]).map(w=>`<span class="chip">${w} changed</span>`).join(" ")} <span class="sub">${x.price!=null?"$"+(+x.price).toFixed(2):""}${pc!=null?" ("+pct(pc)+")":""}</span>
<div>${x.kind==="check"?`<span class="sub">${E(x.level_reason)}</span>`:E(x.one_liner)}</div>
${x.assumption_changes.length?`<div class="chips">${x.assumption_changes.map(a=>`<span class="${a.to}">${E(a.id)} ${a.from?ST[a.from]+" → ":""}${ST[a.to]}</span>`).join("")}</div>`:""}<div class="x"></div></div>`}).join("")}</div>`}

<h1>What do the numbers imply?</h1>
<p class="sub">Start from reported results and analyst expectations, apply an exit multiple, read the annual return the price implies, and put several companies on the same scale. Plan and holdings are never touched or sent anywhere.</p>

  <p class="sub">Each company is imported, then every method with enough data runs from its own numbers: starting from the furthest analyst year (else trailing earnings), analyst growth held flat or fading to the rate you give, and an exit multiple equal to today's multiple on that same starting number. This shows what current expectations imply, not a forecast. Companies are ranked by their most conservative method; methods are never averaged.</p>

   <div class="fg hidden" id="pfields"><label>Share count change (%/yr; negative = buybacks)<span class="tip" tabindex="0" data-h="sharechg">?</span><input id="sharechg" type="number" min="-49" max="49" step="any" value="0"></label></div>

  <div class="card hidden" id="sidebyside"><div class="eye">SAME COMPANY, DIFFERENT METHODS</div><div id="sbs"></div></div>
  <details class="card"><summary>Reported history: revenue, EPS, free cash flow, margins, reconstructed P/E</summary><div class="scroll" id="historical"></div><p class="sub" id="histnote"></p></details>
  <details class="card" id="peerbox"><summary>Peers</summary><div class="scroll" id="peerTable"></div></details>
  <details class="card"><summary>Data notes and warnings</summary><div id="warnings"></div><div id="methodnotes"></div></details>

<div id="tab-guide" class="pane hidden guide">
 <div class="card">
  <h2>How this page works</h2>
  <p>The page answers two questions, not "is my view right":</p>
  <ol><li><b>What does today's price imply?</b> Take the company's reported or expected earnings (or cash flow), project them forward, multiply by an exit multiple, and read the annual return. If that return is below what you require, the price already asks you to believe more than the numbers show.</li>
  <li><b>Which of several companies offers more?</b> Run the same rules on each and compare annual returns on the same horizon.</li></ol>
  <h3>The workflow</h3>
  <ol><li><b>Compare</b> (first tab): enter 2–5 tickers. Every method with enough data runs from the company's own numbers. Read the table, then open one company.</li>
  <li><b>Company</b>: import the data, look at what the market pays (step 1 and the reference multiples), choose a method, reset to the status quo and change one input at a time. Save a snapshot when you want to keep it.</li></ol>
  <h3>Reading a result</h3>
  <ul><li><b>Annual price return</b> = (scenario price ÷ today's price)<sup>1/years</sup> − 1. Dividends are not included.</li>
  <li><b>Bear / Base / Bull</b> are your ranges around the base case, not probabilities or limits. A Bear case is not a stop level.</li>
  <li><b>Where the return comes from</b> splits it exactly into earnings (or cash-flow) growth and multiple change. A return that depends on the multiple rising is a different bet from one that depends on growth.</li>
  <li><b>What it would take</b> gives the EPS, cash flow or exit multiple needed to reach your required return.</li>
  <li><b>Checks</b> point out inputs that look unusual (an exit multiple far above today's, a stale or one-off earnings base). They never change the numbers.</li></ul>
  <h3>Choosing the exit multiple</h3>
  <p>The multiple is what the market pays for a growth and risk profile at the exit date, not today. A company growing 20% now may grow 10% in three years and be priced like it. The page therefore shows several anchors and does not pick one for you:</p>
  <ul><li><b>Today</b>: what the market pays now on each starting number (trailing, this year's or next year's analyst EPS). The row matching your starting point is the default.</li><li><b>Own history</b>: annual fiscal-year-end P/E, a rough guide to what this company has typically been valued at.</li><li><b>Peers</b>: the median of the companies you enter, or of the largest companies in the stock's Yahoo industry when you tick the option; better evidence than a rule of thumb, but only as good as the set.</li><li><b>Sector rule of thumb</b>: rough long-run bands kept in <code>core/multiples.py</code>; editable and not market data.</li><li><b>Growth-implied</b>: peer median PEG (else 1–2) × the growth rate you expect near exit, a sanity check on whether the growth you assume supports the multiple.</li></ul>
  <p><b>Today's multiple or the historical one?</b> Neither alone. Today's multiple prices today's growth; the historical one prices the growth and rates of another period. At exit the company will probably grow more slowly, so start from today's multiple as the neutral case, then look at where peers and history sit, and ask what growth the exit multiple needs. If the exit multiple is above every reference, the result mostly bets on the multiple.</p>
  <p>In Compare, the "if the multiple de-rates" column repeats the calculation with the exit multiple lowered to the reference when today's is above it. A large gap means much of the return depends on the market continuing to pay today's multiple.</p>
  <h3>The methods</h3>
  <table><thead><tr><th>Method</th><th>Formula</th><th>Best for</th><th>Avoid for</th></tr></thead><tbody>
  <tr><td>P/E</td><td>EPS × (1+growth)<sup>t</sup> × exit P/E</td><td>Profitable companies with stable, representative earnings</td><td>Cyclical peaks or troughs, one-off gains, losses</td></tr>
  <tr><td>P/FCF</td><td>FCF per share × ((1+growth)/(1+share change))<sup>t</sup> × exit P/FCF</td><td>Capital-heavy or cash-generative companies (semiconductors, large tech, consumer)</td><td>Negative or erratic free cash flow</td></tr>
  <tr><td>EV/EBITDA</td><td>(Revenue × margin × multiple − net debt − claims) ÷ shares</td><td>Mature, leveraged, moderate-capex companies</td><td>Banks, insurers, REITs, heavy capex, stock-compensation-heavy tech</td></tr></tbody></table>
  <p>Using more than one method on the same company is a cross-check, not an average. When methods disagree by 10 points or more, the answer depends on which earnings or cash-flow base you trust.</p>
  <h3>How Compare works, and what to use it for</h3>
  <p>For each ticker the page runs every method that fits and has data, from the company's own numbers: the starting point is the latest analyst estimate or trailing result, growth is the analyst forecast (fading to a long-run rate), and the exit multiple is today's multiple on that same starting number. Each result is an annual price return over your holding period. <b>It does not say a stock is cheap or expensive.</b> It shows how much return today's price leaves if expectations come true and the multiple does not change.</p>
  <ul><li><b>Use it to rank by how much has to go right</b>, and to notice where the return depends on the multiple (the de-rating column) or on one method only (the gap flag).</li>
  <li><b>★</b> marks the method the fit rules prefer for that kind of company (for cyclicals, free cash flow rather than P/E). It is a rule-based preference, not a reliability rating, and a method with no flag is not risk-free. The ranking uses the most conservative applicable method, not the star.</li>
  <li><b>Not run</b> means the method does not suit the company or has no usable data. <b>Not ranked</b> means a default result looked implausible (above +60% or below −50% a year, usually a data mismatch such as an ADR share ratio or a very small base) or a share count did not reconcile: the numbers stay visible, but the company is left out of the ranking, because removing the odd method would make the remaining ones look better.</li>
  <li>Companies with no analyst forecasts cannot be compared on growth; they show why.</li></ul>
  <h3>Starting points, and the multiple that goes with them</h3>
  <p><b>TTM</b> is what the company earned over the last four quarters. <b>Analyst consensus (this year, next year)</b> are expectations for fiscal years that end in the future. <b>Reported annual</b> figures are past fiscal years. A multiple always belongs to a number: 27.8× on trailing EPS and 12.1× on next year's analyst EPS are the same price read two ways. The page therefore pairs each starting point with <i>today's multiple on that number</i> (price ÷ it), applies your growth for the holding period from that number, and reads the exit multiple on the same basis. Holding the multiple flat then adds nothing from the multiple: the return is the growth you assume, and a change of basis cannot masquerade as a re-rating.</p>
  <p>By default the page starts from the furthest analyst year, because that is the most recent forward-looking number there is, and grows it at the analyst rate fading to a long-run rate. Switch the starting point in the list to see how much the answer depends on the choice.</p>
  <h3>When earnings are far from what analysts expect</h3>
  <p>A company whose trailing EPS has collapsed (a trough, a write-down, a bad year) can trade at a high trailing P/E and a low forward one, because the market is pricing the recovery analysts forecast. Two readings go wrong here. Applying the high trailing multiple to the <i>recovered</i> earnings counts the recovery twice and gives an absurd return; ignoring the recovery altogether values the company on its worst year. The page shows both numbers side by side, starts from the analyst year, and keeps the multiple the market pays on it. Then the question that matters is a different one: <b>what would you have to believe?</b> The table under the result shows the annual return for a range of exit multiples (today's, own history, peers, sector) and for earnings that fall short or never recover. Whether the recovery will happen is the research question, and belongs in your thesis. Analyst EPS is usually an adjusted figure while reported EPS includes one-offs, so part of a gap can be accounting basis rather than turnaround.</p>
  <h3>Four layers, kept apart</h3>
  <ol><li><b>Reported history</b>: company figures through the data provider, with period and fetch date. “Reported” is not “recurring”.</li><li><b>Market consensus</b>: analyst mean EPS and revenue; not a guarantee, and the adjusted-or-reported basis is unverified.</li><li><b>Assumptions</b>: your growth, fade, exit multiple, and the program's defaults for them. These are not forecasts of fact.</li><li><b>Conditional results</b>: scenario prices and annual returns. “Meets your requirement” only says the arithmetic clears the bar, not that the assumptions are likely.</li></ol>
  <p>Two methods that agree are not two independent opinions when they share the same growth input: P/FCF reuses analyst EPS or revenue growth because no cash-flow forecast exists.</p>
  <h3>What gets saved</h3>
  <ul><li><b>Import latest data</b> stores one data record (what the provider returned). Compare also stores data records for the tickers it loads, and reuses one younger than 6 hours.</li><li><b>Save snapshot</b> stores your assumptions and the result as an analysis record. Typing and switching tabs only preview; nothing is written.</li><li>Nothing is overwritten. The Saved snapshots list shows every record, newest first, in your local time.</li><li>The workbench company page reads two things: the newest data record, and the newest analysis record of each method. Data records you did not choose a number from have no effect on it. Your Plan is never changed by any of this.</li></ul>
  <h3>Free cash flow: before and after stock compensation</h3>
  <p>Free cash flow is operating cash flow minus capital expenditure. “After stock compensation” also subtracts the shares the company pays staff with, which is a real cost to owners that cash flow does not show. For companies where stock compensation is a large share of free cash flow (many technology firms) the after-compensation figure is the fairer base; the page selects it by default when the share is above 25%. Dividends are not part of any figure here: returns are price returns only.</p>
  <h3>Analyst figures versus your scenarios</h3>
  <p>Analyst price targets are typically 12-month views, and consensus EPS covers the current and next fiscal year. Your scenarios run to your holding period, so beyond the analyst horizon the numbers are assumptions, and a three-year result is mostly assumption. The result shows the year-by-year Base path so you can compare year 1 with analyst targets, and states how many years rest on your own assumptions.</p>
  <h3>Where the reference ranges come from</h3>
  <p>“Peers” are computed from data (your list, or Yahoo's largest companies in the industry). “Own history” is computed from the company's reported years. “Sector rule of thumb” is a hand-set long-run range, shown for context but never used in a calculation, not computed from data and not authoritative: it sits in <code>core/multiples.py</code> where you can edit it, and peers override it when available.</p>
  <h3>Data and limits</h3>
  <ul><li>Data comes from Yahoo Finance through OpenBB and yfinance: provider fields, not filings. Check unusual figures against the company's report.</li><li>Analyst consensus is a mean with unverified GAAP/adjusted basis. Its relative periods (0y, +1y) are converted to dates from the provider's fiscal year end.</li><li>Growth is applied for exactly the holding period from the starting number; an older annual starting point therefore leaves out the growth since its year end, which the page warns about.</li><li>There is no walk-forward test of these defaults: the page does not give a hit rate or a confidence level, and the three methods are not independent evidence.</li><li>Growth is held flat unless you set <i>Growth fades to</i>; then it moves in a straight line from the starting rate to that rate by the exit date. Fast growers usually slow down, so a flat base case is optimistic for them.</li><li>No AI is used here: every number comes from code. Valuation snapshots are stored separately and never change your Plan.</li></ul>
 </div>
</div>

的区间，不是概率。", "fade": "增长从上面的速度匀速降到这个速度（到退出日）。留空表示每年保持同一速度。", "multiple": "退出日市场愿意付的倍数，与起点数字同口径。默认 = 今天在该起点上的倍数，所以保持不变不会增加任何东西。参考表列出今天、自身历史、同业和行业区间；都不是公允价值。", "mspread": "Bear = 退出倍数降低这个百分比；Bull = 提高这个百分比。", "sharechg": "每年股数的变化。负数 = 回购（提高每股现金流）；正数 = 稀释。如果起点用的是扣除股权激励后的现金流，稀释已经算进去了，这里保持 0。", "margin": "退出日的 EBITDA ÷ 收入。默认是 TTM 利润率。", "marginspread": "Bear = 利润率降低这么多个点；Bull = 提高。", "netdebt": "退出日的债务减现金，单位百万。下面的按钮把今天的数据源数字保持不变。", "shares": "退出日的流通股数，单位百万。", "claims": "排在股东之前的其他权益（少数股东权益、优先股、你当作债务的租赁），单位百万。", "save": "把你当前的输入和结果作为一条新记录写入 valuation.db，与 Plan const GUIDE_ZH="<div class=\"card\">\n  <h2>这个页面怎么用</h2>\n  <p>页面回答两个问题，而不是“我的判断对不对”：</p>\n  <ol><li><b>今天的价格隐含了什么？</b> 取公司已报告或预期的盈利（或现金流），往后推算，乘以退出倍数，读出年化回报。如果这个回报低于你的要求，说明价格已经要求你相信比数字更多的东西。</li>\n  <li><b>几家公司里哪家给得多？</b> 对每家用同样的规则，在同一期限上比较年化回报。</li></ol>\n  <h3>使用流程</h3>\n  <ol><li><b>Compare</b>（第一个标签）：输入 2 到 5 个代码。数据足够的每种方法都会用公司自己的数字运行。先看表，再打开其中一家。</li>\n  <li><b>Company</b>：导入数据，看市场现在付什么（第 1 步和参考倍数），选一种方法，重置为现状，每次只改一个输入。想留存时按 Save snapshot。</li></ol>\n  <h3>怎么读结果</h3>\n  <ul><li><b>年化价格回报</b> = (情景价格 ÷ 今天价格)<sup>1/年数</sup> − 1。不含股息。</li>\n  <li><b>Bear / Base / Bull</b> 是你围绕基准情景设的区间，不是概率或边界。Bear 不是止损位。</li>\n  <li><b>回报来自哪里</b>把回报精确拆成盈利（或现金流）增长和倍数变化。依赖倍数上升的回报，和依赖增长的回报是不同的赌注。</li>\n  <li><b>What it would take</b> 给出要达到你要求的回报所需的 EPS、现金流或退出倍数。</li>\n  <li><b>Checks</b> 指出看起来异常的输入（退出倍数远高于今天、起点过时或含一次性收益）。它们从不改变数字。</li></ul>\n  <h3>怎么选退出倍数</h3>\n  <p>倍数是市场在退出日为当时的增长和风险付的价，不是今天的。现在增长 20% 的公司三年后可能只增长 10%，也会按那个速度定价。所以页面给出几个参照，不替你选：</p>\n  <ul><li><b>Today</b>：市场现在在每个起点数字上付的倍数（TTM、今年或明年的分析师 EPS）。与你的起点对应的那一行是默认值。</li><li><b>Own history</b>：财年末的年度市盈率，粗略反映这家公司通常被如何估值。</li><li><b>Peers</b>：你输入的公司的中位数，或勾选后 Yahoo 同行业最大公司的中位数；比经验区间更有证据，但只和这组公司一样好。</li><li><b>Sector rule of thumb</b>：保存在 <code>core/multiples.py</code> 里的粗略长期区间；可编辑，不是市场数据。</li><li><b>Growth-implied</b>：同业 PEG 中位数（否则 1 到 2）× 你预期退出时的增速，用来检验你假设的增长是否支撑这个倍数。</li></ul>\n  <p><b>用今天的倍数还是历史的？</b> 都不能单独用。今天的倍数是为今天的增长定价；历史的是另一个时期的增长和利率。退出时公司大概率增长更慢，所以以今天的倍数为中性起点，再看同业和历史在哪里，问问退出倍数需要多少增长来支撑。如果退出倍数高于所有参照，结果主要是在赌倍数。</p>\n  <p>在 Compare 里，“if the multiple de-rates”这一列在今天的倍数高于参照时，用降到参照的退出倍数重算一遍。差距大说明回报很大程度依赖市场继续给今天的倍数。</p>\n  <h3>三种方法</h3>\n  <table><thead><tr><th>方法</th><th>公式</th><th>适合</th><th>避免用于</th></tr></thead><tbody>\n  <tr><td>P/E</td><td>EPS × (1+增长)<sup>t</sup> × 退出 P/E</td><td>盈利为正且稳定、有代表性的公司</td><td>周期的高点或低点、一次性收益、亏损</td></tr>\n  <tr><td>P/FCF</td><td>每股 FCF × ((1+增长)/(1+股数变化))<sup>t</sup> × 退出 P/FCF</td><td>资本密集或现金充裕的公司（半导体、大型科技、消费）</td><td>自由现金流为负或波动大</td></tr>\n  <tr><td>EV/EBITDA</td><td>(收入 × 利润率 × 倍数 − 净债务 − 其他权益) ÷ 股数</td><td>成熟、有杠杆、资本开支适中的公司</td><td>银行、保险、REITs、重资本开支、股权激励很重的科技公司</td></tr></tbody></table>\n  <p>对同一家公司用多种方法是交叉检验，不是求平均。方法之间相差 10 个点以上时，答案取决于你更信哪个盈利或现金流基数。</p>\n  <h3>Compare 怎么工作，用来做什么</h3>\n  <p>对每个代码，页面用公司自己的数字运行每种适合且有数据的方法：起点是最新的分析师预期或 TTM 结果，增长取分析师预测（逐步降到长期速度），退出倍数是今天在同一起点上的倍数。每个结果是你持有期内的年化价格回报。<b>它不说一只股票便宜还是贵。</b>它显示的是：如果预期兑现、倍数不变，今天的价格还给你留下多少回报。</p>\n  <ul><li><b>用它按“需要多少事情做对”来排序</b>，并留意回报依赖倍数（de-rating 列）或只依赖一种方法（分歧标记）的地方。</li>\n  <li><b>★</b> 标记规则偏好的方法（周期股偏向自由现金流而不是 P/E）。这是规则偏好，不是可靠性评级，没有标记的方法也不是没有风险。排名用最保守的适用方法，不看星标。</li>\n  <li><b>Not run</b> 表示方法不适合该公司或没有可用数据。<b>Not ranked</b> 表示默认结果看起来不合理（高于 +60% 或低于 −50% 每年，通常是 ADR 股比或基数太小之类的数据错配），或股本对不上：数字仍然可见，但公司不参与排名，因为去掉异常的方法会让剩下的看起来更好。</li>\n  <li>没有分析师预测的公司无法按增长比较，页面会说明原因。</li></ul>\n  <h3>起点，以及与它配套的倍数</h3>\n  <p><b>TTM</b> 是公司过去四个季度赚的。<b>Analyst consensus（今年、明年）</b>是对未来财年的预期。<b>Reported annual</b> 是过去的财年。倍数永远属于某一个数字：TTM EPS 上的 27.8× 和明年分析师 EPS 上的 12.1× 是同一个价格的两种读法。所以页面把每个起点和<i>今天在这个数字上的倍数</i>（价格 ÷ 它）配成一对，从这个数字开始按你的增长算持有期，并在同一口径上读退出倍数。保持倍数不变就不会从倍数里多出任何东西：回报就是你假设的增长，换口径也不会冒充成重新估值。</p>\n  <p>默认情况下页面从最远的分析师年度出发，因为那是最新的前瞻数字，按分析师速度增长并逐步降到长期速度。在列表里切换起点，可以看到答案对这个选择有多敏感。</p>\n  <h3>当盈利和分析师预期差很远时</h3>\n  <p>TTM EPS 大幅下滑的公司（低谷、减值、糟糕的一年）可能市盈率（TTM）很高而前瞻市盈率很低，因为市场在为分析师预测的恢复定价。这里有两种读法会出错：把高的 TTM 倍数乘在<i>恢复后</i>的盈利上，会把恢复算两遍，得到荒谬的回报；完全忽略恢复，则是按公司最差的一年估值。页面把两个数并排显示，从分析师年度出发，并保持市场在该数字上付的倍数。然后真正重要的问题变成：<b>你必须相信什么？</b>结果下方的表给出一系列退出倍数（今天、自身历史、同业、行业）和盈利不及预期或完全不恢复情形下的年化回报。恢复会不会发生是研究问题，应写进你的论点。分析师 EPS 通常是调整后口径，而报告 EPS 含一次性项目，所以差距的一部分可能只是会计口径，不是反转。</p>\n  <h3>四层信息，分开看</h3>\n  <ol><li><b>历史（Reported history）</b>：通过数据源取得的公司数字，带期间和取数日期。“已报告”不等于“可持续”。</li><li><b>市场一致预期（Market consensus）</b>：分析师平均 EPS 和收入；不是保证，调整后还是报告口径未核实。</li><li><b>假设（Assumptions）</b>：你的增长、衰减、退出倍数，以及程序给的默认值。这些不是事实预测。</li><li><b>条件结果（Conditional results）</b>：情景价格和年化回报。“Meets your requirement”只说明算术上过了门槛，不说明假设很可能发生。</li></ol>\n  <p>两种方法结果一致，但如果用的是同一个增长输入，就不是两个独立的意见：P/FCF 复用分析师 EPS 或收入增速，因为不存在现金流预测。</p>\n  <h3>什么会被保存</h3>\n  <ul><li><b>Import latest data</b> 存一条数据记录（数据源返回的内容）。再次导入同一代码时，没有任何已保存结果使用的旧导入会被替换。Compare 也会为它加载的代码存数据记录，6 小时内的会复用。</li><li><b>Save snapshot</b> 把你的假设和结果存为一条分析记录。输入和切换标签只是预览，不会写入任何东西。</li><li>记录不会被改写。Saved snapshots 列表按时间从新到旧显示每条记录（本地时间）；每条旁边的 ✕ 可以删除它（会先确认），“Remove unused imports”可以清掉没用到的导入。仍被分析或比较使用的导入不能删，要先删用到它的记录。</li><li>工作台的公司页读取两样东西：最新的数据记录，和每种方法最新的分析记录。你的 Plan 不会因此被改变。</li></ul>\n  <h3>自由现金流：扣除股权激励前后</h3>\n  <p>自由现金流 = 经营现金流 − 资本开支。“After stock compensation”还要减去公司用来给员工发薪的股票，这对股东是真实成本，但现金流里看不到。对股权激励占自由现金流比例大的公司（很多科技公司），扣除后的数字是更公平的基数；占比超过 25% 时页面默认选它。股息不在任何数字里：这里的回报只是价格回报。</p>\n  <h3>分析师数字与你的情景</h3>\n  <p>分析师目标价通常是 12 个月的看法，一致预期 EPS 覆盖当前和下一个财年。你的情景要走完你的持有期，所以超出分析师范围的部分是假设，三年的结果大部分是假设。结果会显示逐年的 Base 路径，方便你把第 1 年和分析师目标比较，并说明有几年只靠你自己的假设。</p>\n  <h3>参考区间从哪来</h3>\n  <p>“Peers”由数据计算（你的名单，或 Yahoo 同行业最大公司）。“Own history”由公司已报告的年度计算。“Sector rule of thumb”是手工设定的长期区间，只作参考展示，从不进入计算，不是由数据算出，也不权威：它在 <code>core/multiples.py</code> 里可以编辑，有同业时以同业为准。</p>\n  <h3>数据与局限</h3>\n  <ul><li>数据来自 Yahoo Finance，经 OpenBB 和 yfinance：是数据源字段，不是财报原文。异常数字请对照公司报告。</li><li>分析师一致预期是均值，GAAP/调整后口径未核实。相对期间（0y、+1y）是按数据源的财年结束日换算成日期的。</li><li>增长严格按持有期、从起点数字开始计算；因此较旧的年度起点会漏掉其年末以来的增长，页面会提醒。</li><li>这些默认值没有做过滚动检验：页面不给命中率或置信度，三种方法也不是独立证据。</li><li>除非设置 <i>Growth fades to</i>，增长保持不变；设置后从起始速度匀速降到该速度。高增长公司通常会放缓，所以不衰减的 Base 对它们偏乐观。</li><li>这里不使用 AI：每个数字都由代码算出。估值快照单独存放，从不改变你的 Plan。</li></ul>\n </div>";

function cell(m,derate,prim){if(!m)return '<span class="sub">n/a</span>';const v=derate?(m.derate_base??m.base):m.base;return `${prim?'<span class="star" title="Rule-based preference for this kind of company, not a reliability rating">★</span> ':''}<b>${P(v)}</b>${m.implausible?' <span class="warnc" title="Result looks implausible; check the data before trusting it">(review)</span>':''}${m.fit==='caution'?' <span class="warnc" title="'+E((m.fit_reasons||[]).join(' '))+'">(care)</span>':''}<br><span class="meta">${derate?(m.derate_base==null?'same as held':'exit '+X(m.derate_multiple)):P(m.bear)+' to '+P(m.bull)}</span>${!derate&&m.warnings.length?'<br><span class="warnc" title="'+E(m.warnings.join(' '))+'">⚠ '+m.warnings.length+' check'+(m.warnings.length>1?'s':'')+'</span>':''}`}
function disNote(d){if(!d)return '';const f=v=>v==null?'—':N(v),g=d.jump==null?'':' ('+PP(d.jump)+' vs trailing)';
 if(d.kind==='recovery')return `<div class="note"><b>Earnings recovery priced in.</b> Trailing EPS ${f(d.ttm_eps)}, analysts ${f(d.cons_0y)}${d.cons_1y?' then '+f(d.cons_1y):''}${g}. This row starts from the analyst year and holds the multiple on it; open the company for the what-you-must-believe table.</div>`;
 if(d.kind==='loss_to_profit')return `<div class="note"><b>Loss to profit.</b> Trailing EPS ${f(d.ttm_eps)} is not positive while analysts expect ${f(d.cons_0y||d.cons_1y)}. P/E here rests on a forecast turnaround.</div>`;
 return `<div class="note"><b>Earnings expected to fall back.</b> Trailing EPS ${f(d.ttm_eps)}, analysts ${f(d.cons_0y)}${g}. Trailing multiples look low only because earnings are high now.</div>`}
function renderCompare(s){const r=s.payload.result,by=(row,k)=>row.methods.find(m=>m.method===k);
 const src=x=>{const m=by(x,x.primary);return m&&m.earnings_effect!=null?`${E(m.label)}: earnings/cash flow <b>${PP(m.earnings_effect)}</b>, multiple <b>${PP(m.multiple_effect)}</b> per year`:'—'};
 const H=(t,k,sub)=>t+Q(k)+(sub?'<br><span class="meta">'+sub+'</span>':'');
 $('#cmpout').innerHTML='<div class="card"><div class="scroll">'+table([H('#','h_rank'),H('Company','h_company'),H('Price','h_price'),H(METHODS.pe.label,'h_pe','Base · Bear to Bull'),H(METHODS.p_fcf.label,'h_pfcf','Base · Bear to Bull'),H(METHODS.ev_ebitda.label,'h_ev','Base · Bear to Bull'),H('Most conservative','h_cons','multiple held'),'If the multiple de-rates'+Q('h_derate')+'<br><span class="meta">to reference</span>',H('Where the return comes from','h_src'),H('Notes','h_notes')],r.rows.map(x=>{const deSame=x.methods.length&&x.conservative_derate>=x.conservative-1e-9;return [x.rank||(x.methods.length?'<span class="meta" title="Shown with its numbers but not ranked: see the notes">not ranked</span>':'—'),`<a href="#" data-load="${E(x.data_id)}"><b>${E(x.ticker)}</b></a><br><span class="meta">${E(x.name||'')}${x.sector?' · '+E(x.sector):''}</span>`,x.price==null?'—':N(x.price)+' '+E(x.currency||''),cell(by(x,'pe'),false,x.primary==='pe'),cell(by(x,'p_fcf'),false,x.primary==='p_fcf'),cell(by(x,'ev_ebitda'),false,x.primary==='ev_ebitda'),x.methods.length?'<b>'+P(x.conservative)+'</b>':'—',x.methods.length?(deSame?'<span class="meta">no lower reference</span>':'<b class="'+(x.conservative_derate<(r.required_return??0)?'warnc':'')+'">'+P(x.conservative_derate)+'</b>'):'—',x.methods.length?src(x):'—',disNote(x.dislocation)+(x.disagree?'<div class="note">Methods differ by '+N(x.dispersion*100)+' points: the answer depends on which earnings or cash-flow base you trust.</div>':'')+(x.review||[]).map(f=>'<div class="note"><b>Not ranked.</b> '+E(f)+'</div>').join('')+x.flags.map(f=>'<div class="note">'+E(f)+'</div>').join('')+(x.skipped.length?'<span class="meta">Not run: '+x.skipped.map(k=>E(METHODS[k.method]?.label||k.method)+' ('+E(k.reason)+')').join('; ')+'</span>':'')]}))+'</div></div>'+
 '<div class="card"><h3 style="margin-top:0">How to read this</h3><ul class="sub" style="margin:0;padding-left:18px"><li>Cells show the Base annual price return, then Bear to Bull. Required return: '+(r.required_return!=null?P(r.required_return):'not set')+'. Dividends are excluded.</li>'+r.notes.map(n=>'<li>'+E(n)+'</li>').join('')+'<li>Open a company (click its ticker) to adjust the assumptions and see the reference multiples.</li></ul></div>';
 $('#cmpout').onclick=e=>{const a=e.target.closest('[data-load]');if(a){e.preventDefault();run(async()=>{renderData(await api('/snapshots/'+a.dataset.load));tab('company')})}}}
$('#cmpform').onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();run(async()=>{const t=$('#cmptickers').value.split(',').map(x=>x.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);if(t.length<2||t.length>5)throw Error('Enter 2 to 5 tickers');status('Importing and comparing '+t.join(', ')+'… this can take a minute.');const rr=$('#cmpreturn').value;const s=await post('/compare',{tickers:t,years:Number($('#cmpyears').value),required_return:rr===''?null:Number(rr)/100,multiple_shift:Number($('#cmpshift').value||0)/100,fade_to:$('#cmpfade').value===''?null:Number($('#cmpfade').value)/100,include_industry:$('#cmpind').checked});renderCompare(s);status('Comparison saved. Defaults are status-quo assumptions, not forecasts of your own.')})};

function metric(l,v,d){return `<div><span>${E(l)}</span><b>${v}</b><span>${E(d||'')}</span></div>`}
function estimates(rows,ex){const eps=r=>r.metric==='eps';const head=['Metric · period','Mean','Low / High','Analysts'].concat(ex?['Price ÷ EPS','vs trailing EPS']:[]);
 return rows.length?table(head,rows.map(r=>[E(r.metric)+' · '+E(r.period)+(r.period_end?' <span class="meta">(to '+(r.period_end_approx?'~':'')+E(r.period_end)+')</span>':''),eps(r)?N(r.avg):M(r.avg),(eps(r)?N(r.low):M(r.low))+' / '+(eps(r)?N(r.high):M(r.high)),N(r.numberOfAnalysts)].concat(ex?[eps(r)&&r.avg>0&&ex.price?X(ex.price/r.avg):'—',eps(r)&&r.avg>0&&ex.ttm>0?PP(r.avg/ex.ttm-1):'—']:[]))):'<p class="sub">No analyst estimates returned.</p>'}
function disBlock(d){const dis=d.earnings_dislocation;if(!dis)return '';const price=d.quote.value;
 const head={recovery:'Reported earnings are far below what analysts expect',loss_to_profit:'Reported earnings are a loss; analysts expect a profit',decline:'Reported earnings are above what analysts expect'}[dis.kind]||'Reported and expected earnings differ a lot';
 const step=(l,v,sub)=>v>0?`<div>${E(l)}<b>${N(v)}</b><span class="meta">${price?X(price/v)+' on it':''}${sub?' · '+sub:''}</span></div>`:'';
 const ch=v=>dis.ttm_eps>0?PP(v/dis.ttm_eps-1)+' vs trailing':'';
 return `<div class="dis"><h3>${E(head)}</h3><div class="ladder">${step('Trailing EPS',dis.ttm_eps,'what was earned')}${step('This year · analysts',dis.cons_0y,ch(dis.cons_0y))}${step('Next year · analysts',dis.cons_1y,ch(dis.cons_1y))}</div>`+
 (dis.lines||[]).map(l=>'<p>'+E(l)+'</p>').join('')+
 `<p><b>${Z('How this page handles it.','页面怎么处理。')}</b> ${LANG==='zh'?'计算从最远的分析师年度出发，并保持市场在同一数字上付的倍数，所以结果显示的是分析师年度之后的增长回报，而不是恢复本身的回报（价格已经预期了恢复）。用低谷盈利上的倍数会把恢复算两遍。打开结果下方的“What you must believe”，可以测试不同的退出倍数，以及盈利不及预期或根本不恢复的情形。':'Calculations start from the furthest analyst year and keep the multiple the market pays on that same number, so the result shows the return from growth after the analyst years, not from the recovery itself (the price already expects that). A multiple measured on trough earnings would double-count the recovery. Open <i>What you must believe</i> under the result to test a different exit multiple and earnings that fall short or never recover.'}</p></div>`}
function renderData(s){snap=s;F={};R={};last=null;method='pe';balanceMode='user_forecast';$('#resbox').innerHTML='';$('#sidebyside').classList.add('hidden');$('#co').classList.remove('hidden');
 const d=s.payload,c=d.current_multiples||{};$('#ticker').value=d.ticker;$('#peers').value=(d.peers||[]).map(p=>p.ticker).join(', ');
 $('#coname').innerHTML=E(d.ticker)+' <span>· '+E(d.name)+'</span>';$('#cotags').innerHTML=[d.sector,d.industry,d.quote.currency].filter(Boolean).map(x=>'<span>'+E(x)+'</span>').join('');

 $('#metrics').innerHTML=metric('P/E · TTM',X(c.trailing_pe),'TTM EPS '+N(c.trailing_eps))+metric('P/E · forward',X(c.forward_pe),'horizon unverified')+metric('P/FCF',X(c.price_to_fcf),c.fcf_basis||'free cash flow unavailable')+metric('EV/EBITDA',X(c.enterprise_to_ebitda),'provider');
 const t=d.analyst_targets||{};$('#targets').innerHTML='<div class="kvs">'+metric('Analyst target · mean',N(t.mean),N(t.analysts)+' analysts')+metric('Median',N(t.median),'provider')+metric('Low / high',N(t.low)+' / '+N(t.high),'dispersion, not bounds')+metric('Mean vs price',t.mean&&d.quote.value?PP(t.mean/d.quote.value-1):'—','unannualised; horizon unknown')+'</div>';
 $('#dis').innerHTML=disBlock(d);$('#annual').innerHTML='<h3>Annual consensus</h3>'+estimates(d.estimates.filter(x=>['0y','+1y'].includes(x.period)),{price:d.quote.value,ttm:c.trailing_eps});$('#quarterly').innerHTML=estimates(d.estimates.filter(x=>['0q','+1q'].includes(x.period)));
 const h=d.historical_pe||{},fh=d.fcf_history||[];
 $('#historical').innerHTML=table(['Year end','Revenue','Diluted EPS','EBITDA · margin','Free cash flow','FCF ÷ net income','Close near year end','P/E (reconstructed)'],d.historical.map(r=>{const p=(h.rows||[]).find(x=>x.period_end===r.period_end)||{};return [E(r.period_end),M(r.revenue),N(r.diluted_eps),M(r.ebitda)+' · '+P(r.ebitda_margin),M(r.fcf),r.fcf_conversion==null?'—':P(r.fcf_conversion),N(p.close),X(p.pe)]}));
 $('#histnote').textContent=h.count?`Reconstructed annual P/E: median ${X(h.median)}, range ${X(h.low)} to ${X(h.high)} over ${h.count} years. Year-end price over same-year EPS, which was not yet known then; a rough guide, not a point-in-time series.`:'Historical P/E is unavailable in this snapshot.';
 $('#peerbox').classList.toggle('hidden',!(d.peers||[]).length);$('#peerTable').innerHTML=(d.peers||[]).length?table(['Company','Industry','P/E TTM','P/E forward','P/FCF','EV/EBITDA'],d.peers.map(p=>[E(p.ticker),E(p.industry||p.error),X(p.trailing_pe),X(p.forward_pe),X(p.price_to_fcf),X(p.ev_ebitda)]))+'<p class="sub">Your comparison set; business mix and earnings definitions differ.</p>':'';
 $('#warnings').innerHTML=d.warnings.map(w=>'<div class="note info">'+E(w)+'</div>').join('');$('#methodnotes').innerHTML=(d.method_notes||[]).map(x=>'<p class="sub">'+E(x)+'</p>').join('');
 const b=d.market_baseline||{};$('#balancehint').textContent=`Provider net debt ${M(b.net_debt)} ${b.currency||''}, shares ${M(b.shares)}; balance dates unknown.`;
 renderMethodTabs();setMethod('pe',true)}
function available(m){const d=snap.payload,rows=d[METHODS[m].key]||[];return rows.some(a=>a.value>0)&&(m!=='ev_ebitda'||(d.market_baseline||{}).net_debt!=null)}
const FITWORD={fit:'Suits this company',caution:'Use with care',avoid:'Not suitable'};

function statusQuo(quiet){const d=snap.payload,q=(d.defaults||{})[method]||{},rows=d[METHODS[method].key]||[];
 const pick=(q.anchor_id&&rows.find(a=>a.id===q.anchor_id&&a.value>0))||rows.find(a=>a.category==='ttm_reported'&&a.value>0)||rows.find(a=>a.value>0),o={anchor:pick?.id||''};
 const own=method!=='ev_ebitda'&&pick&&q.multiple_by_anchor?q.multiple_by_anchor[pick.id]:null;
 o.growth=q.growth??'';o.fade=q.fade_to??'';o.spread=q.growth_spread??'';o.multiple=own??(method==='pe'?q.exit_pe:q.exit_multiple)??'';o.multiplespread=(method==='pe'?q.pe_spread:q.multiple_spread)??'';
 if(method==='p_fcf')o.sharechg=q.share_change??0;if(method==='ev_ebitda'){o.margin=q.margin??'';o.marginspread=q.margin_spread??'';o.claims=0;balanceMode='user_forecast'}
 writeForm(o);anchorNote();if(!quiet){status('Reset to status quo. Change one assumption at a time.');preview()}}
function anchorNote(){const d=snap?.payload||{},el=$('#anchor'),a=(d[METHODS[method].key]||[]).find(x=>x.id===el.value);el.dataset.prev=el.value;if(!a){$('#anchorbasis').textContent='';return}
 const yrs=Number($('#years').value)||3,mm=((d.defaults||{})[method]||{}).multiple_by_anchor,today=mm&&mm[a.id]!=null?` Today's multiple on this number: ${X(mm[a.id])}; the exit multiple should be on the same basis.`:'';
 const kind=a.category==='market_consensus'?' Analysts already expect this number, so the growth you enter is growth after it. If it sits far above trailing earnings, the price already assumes a recovery.':a.category==='ttm_reported'?' This is what the company earned over the last four quarters.':' A past fiscal year: growth since then is not in this number, so prefer trailing-twelve-month or analyst figures when they exist.';
 $('#anchorbasis').textContent=(a.basis||'')+` Period end ${a.period_end}${a.period_end_approx?' (estimated)':''}. Your growth rate is applied for the ${yrs} years of your holding period, starting from this number.`+today+kind}
function renderContext(){const d=snap.payload,ctx=(d.multiple_context||{})[method];if(!ctx){$('#mctx').innerHTML='<div class="note info">Reference multiples are missing from this older snapshot. Import the latest data to add them.</div>';return}
 const rows=ctx.points.map(p=>[E(p.label),p.low===p.high?X(p.low):X(p.low)+' – '+X(p.high),'<span class="meta">'+E(p.basis)+'</span>',`<button type="button" class="btn ghost sm" data-use="${p.low===p.high?p.low:((p.low+p.high)/2).toFixed(1)}"${p.anchor?` data-anchor="${E(p.anchor)}"`:''}>${p.anchor?'Use this starting point and ':'Use '}${p.low===p.high?X(p.low):'mid '+X((p.low+p.high)/2)}</button>`]);
 $('#mctx').innerHTML=`<h3>What multiple might the market pay at the exit date?</h3><p class="sub">An exit multiple reflects growth and risk <i>then</i>, usually lower than today for fast growers. These are reference points, not fair values.</p>`+
  (rows.length?table(['Reference','Range','Basis',''],rows):'<p class="sub">No reference multiples available for this method.</p>')+
  '<div id="peg" class="sub" style="margin-top:6px"></div>'+ctx.notes.map(n=>'<div class="note '+(/Prefer|prefer/.test(n)?'rec':'')+'">'+E(n)+'</div>').join('')+growthRefs(d);peg()}
function growthRefs(d){const gr=d.growth_refs||[];if(!gr.length)return '';
 return `<h3>What growth rate might it be?</h3><p class="sub">${method==='p_fcf'?'No analyst forecast exists for free cash flow; analyst EPS and revenue growth are the nearest forward-looking guides. ':method==='ev_ebitda'?'Revenue growth is what this method needs; analyst revenue forecasts are the best guide. ':'Analyst EPS growth is the only forward-looking earnings growth available. '}Rates are per year, for one year; they usually fade, which is what “Growth fades to” is for.</p>`+
 table(['Source','Rate','Basis',''],gr.map(r=>[E(r.label)+(r.methods.includes(method)?' <span class="meta">· fits this method</span>':''),r.value==null?'—':N(r.value)+'%','<span class="meta">'+E(r.basis)+'</span>',r.value==null?'':`<button type="button" class="btn ghost sm" data-g="${r.value}">Use ${N(r.value)}%</button>`]))}
function peg(){const el=$('#peg');if(!el||method!=='pe')return void(el&&(el.textContent=''));const g=Number($('#growth').value),m=Number($('#multiple').value);const ctx=(snap.payload.multiple_context||{}).pe||{},pg=ctx.peg||[1,2],fd=$('#fade').value===''?NaN:Number($('#fade').value),ge=fd>0?fd:g;if(!(ge>0)){el.textContent='Growth-implied check: enter a positive growth rate to see what P/E it supports.';return}
 if(ge<8){el.textContent='Growth-implied check: below about 8% growth a PEG rule gives unrealistically low multiples (even slow growers trade near 10×), so it is not shown. Compare with today, peers and history instead.';return}
 const lo=pg[0]*ge,hi=pg[1]*ge;el.innerHTML=`Growth-implied check (${E(ctx.peg_basis||'PEG 1 to 2')}): at ${fd>0?'exit growth':'growth'} ${N(ge)}% this supports a P/E of about <b>${N(lo)}–${N(hi)}×</b>.`+(m>hi?` Your exit P/E ${N(m)}× is above that range: it needs growth to stay high or the market to keep paying a premium.`:m<lo?` Your exit P/E ${N(m)}× is below that range.`:'')}
$('#mctx').onclick=e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-use]');if(b){if(b.dataset.anchor){$('#anchor').value=b.dataset.anchor;anchorNote()}$('#multiple').value=b.dataset.use;peg();preview();return}const g=e.target.closest('[data-g]');if(g){$('#growth').value=g.dataset.g;peg();preview()}};
$('#anchor').onchange=()=>{const el=$('#anchor'),mm=((snap.payload.defaults||{})[method]||{}).multiple_by_anchor,prev=el.dataset.prev,cur=Number($('#multiple').value);if(mm&&prev&&mm[prev]!=null&&mm[el.value]!=null&&Math.abs(cur-mm[prev])<0.06){$('#multiple').value=mm[el.value];status('Exit multiple moved to today\'s multiple on the new starting point ('+N(mm[el.value])+'×) so both stay on the same basis.')}else if(mm&&mm[el.value]!=null){status('Starting point changed. Today\'s multiple on it is '+N(mm[el.value])+'×; check your exit multiple is on the same basis.')}anchorNote();preview()};$('#years').addEventListener('input',anchorNote);
$('#statusquo').onclick=()=>statusQuo(false);
$('#usebalance').onclick=()=>{const b=snap?.payload.market_baseline;if(b?.net_debt==null||b?.shares==null||b.currency!==snap.payload.quote.currency){status('Comparable current net debt and shares are unavailable.',true);return}$('#netdebt').value=b.net_debt/1e6;$('#shares').value=b.shares/1e6;balanceMode='hold_current_constant';status('Assumption: current net debt and share count stay constant until exit.');preview()};

function assumptionsOf(m,f,years,ret){const n=i=>f[i]===''||f[i]==null?NaN:Number(f[i]);const a={method:m,anchor_id:f.anchor,years,required_return:ret/100,growth:n('growth')/100,growth_spread:n('spread')/100,acknowledge_basis:true};if(f.fade!==''&&f.fade!=null){if(Number.isNaN(Number(f.fade)))return null;a.fade_to=Number(f.fade)/100}
 const need=[years,ret,n('growth'),n('spread'),n('multiple'),n('multiplespread')];if(!f.anchor||need.some(Number.isNaN))return null;
 if(m==='pe')Object.assign(a,{exit_pe:n('multiple'),pe_spread:n('multiplespread')/100});
 else if(m==='p_fcf'){if(Number.isNaN(n('sharechg')))return null;Object.assign(a,{share_change:n('sharechg')/100,exit_multiple:n('multiple'),multiple_spread:n('multiplespread')/100})}
 else{if(['margin','marginspread','netdebt','shares','claims'].some(i=>Number.isNaN(n(i))))return null;Object.assign(a,{margin:n('margin')/100,margin_spread:n('marginspread')/100,exit_multiple:n('multiple'),multiple_spread:n('multiplespread')/100,target_net_debt:n('netdebt')*1e6,target_shares:n('shares')*1e6,other_claims:n('claims')*1e6,balance_assumption:balanceMode})}
 return a}
function preview(){clearTimeout(timer);timer=setTimeout(doPreview,300)}
async function doPreview(){if(!snap)return;const my=++seq,m=method,f=readForm();F[m]=f;peg();const a=assumptionsOf(m,f,Number($('#years').value),Number($('#return').value));
 if(!a){$('#resbox').innerHTML='<p class="sub">Fill the starting point, growth, exit multiple and ranges to see the result. “Reset to status quo” fills them from the company’s own numbers.</p>';delete R[m];sbs();return}
 try{const s=await post('/calculate',{data_id:snap.id,assumptions:a,persist:false});if(my!==seq)return;R[m]=s.payload.result;last=s;renderResult(m,s.payload.result);sbs()}catch(e){if(my===seq){$('#resbox').innerHTML='<div class="note">'+E(e.message)+'</div>';delete R[m];sbs()}}}
