===== workbench_v2/tests/test_thesis.py =====

import unittest
from app.core import thesis as t
from app.adapters import news_fallback as n


class ThesisTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_hints_are_sector_specific(self):
        self.assertIn("same-store", t.sector_hint({"industry": "Restaurants"}))
        self.assertIn("ASP", t.sector_hint({"industry": "Semiconductors"}))
        self.assertNotIn("ASP", t.sector_hint({"industry": "Restaurants"}))
        self.assertIsNone(t.sector_hint({"industry": "Unknownium"}))

    def test_health(self):
        a = lambda i, st, m="medium": {"id": i, "status": st, "materiality": m}
        self.assertEqual(t.health([a("a1", "holding"), a("a2", "strengthened")])[0], "Intact")
        self.assertEqual(t.health([a("a1", "weakened")])[0], "Watch")
        self.assertEqual(t.health([a("a1", "unverified", "high")])[0], "Watch")
        self.assertEqual(t.health([a("a1", "weakened", "high")])[0], "At risk")
        self.assertEqual(t.health([{"id": "a1", "status": "broken"}])[0], "At risk")
        self.assertEqual(t.health([{"id": "a1", "status": "holding"}])[0], "Intact")   # old states lack materiality


class NewsTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_nested_and_flat(self):
        nested = {"content": {"title": " A ", "pubDate": "2026-10-01T10:00:00Z", "canonicalUrl": {"url": "https://x/a"}, "provider": {"displayName": "Reuters"}}}
        flat = {"title": "B", "link": "https://x/b", "publisher": "AP", "providerPublishTime": 1790000000}
        r = n.parse_yf_news([nested, flat, {"foo": 1}, "bad"])
        self.assertEqual([x["title"] for x in r], ["A", "B"])
        self.assertEqual((r[0]["url"], r[0]["source"]), ("https://x/a", "Reuters"))
        self.assertEqual((r[1]["url"], r[1]["source"]), ("https://x/b", "AP"))
        self.assertTrue(r[1]["date"].startswith("2026-"))


class RssTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_rss(self):
        xml = """<rss><channel><item><title> Headline A </title><link>https://x/a</link><pubDate>Mon, 05 Oct 2026 14:00:00 +0000</pubDate></item><item><title></title></item></channel></rss>"""
        r = n.parse_rss(xml)
        self.assertEqual(len(r), 1); self.assertEqual(r[0]["title"], "Headline A"); self.assertTrue(r[0]["date"].startswith("2026-10-05"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()




===== workbench_v2/tests/test_statements.py =====

import unittest
from app.core import statements as st, signals
from app.core.plan import plan_status as ps


def q(end, rev, op, ocf=None, capex=None, fy=2026, fp="Q1"):
    d = {"period_ending": end, "revenue": rev, "operating_income": op, "fiscal_year": fy, "fiscal_period": fp}
    c = {"period_ending": end, "operating_cash_flow": ocf, "capital_expenditure": capex}
    return d, c


ENDS = ["2026-06-30", "2026-03-31", "2025-12-31", "2025-09-30", "2025-06-30", "2025-03-31"]
REV = [130, 120, 115, 110, 100, 95]


def stm(n=6):
    inc, cash = [], []
    for e, r in zip(ENDS[:n], REV[:n]):
        a, b = q(e, r, r * 0.2, ocf=r * 0.3, capex=-r * 0.1)
        inc.append(a); cash.append(b)
    return {"annual": [], "quarterly": st.merge(inc, cash, 8)}


class StatementTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_merge_and_fcf(self):
        m = stm()["quarterly"]
        self.assertEqual(m[0]["period_ending"], "2026-06-30")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(m[0]["free_cash_flow"], 130 * 0.2)       # ocf + (negative capex)

    def test_growth_and_margin(self):
        v = st.view(stm())["quarterly"]
        self.assertAlmostEqual(v[0]["revenue_yoy"], 0.3)                  # 130 vs 100 four quarters earlier
        self.assertAlmostEqual(v[0]["operating_margin"], 0.2)
        self.assertIsNone(v[4]["revenue_yoy"])

    def test_trend_for_model_is_small(self):
        t = st.quarterly_trend(stm())
        self.assertEqual(len(t["quarters"]), 4)
        self.assertIn("operating_margin_change_over_4q_pp", t["basis"] and t)
        self.assertIsNone(st.quarterly_trend({}))

    def test_new_quarter_is_a_change_only_if_anchor_had_quarters(self):
        old = {"statements": stm(5), "financial_health": {}}
        new = {"statements": stm(6), "financial_health": {}}
        self.assertEqual(signals.compute_delta(new, {"statements": stm(6)})["status"], "unchanged")
        # drop the newest row from the anchor: the new evidence shows a later quarter
        a = stm(6); a["quarterly"] = a["quarterly"][1:]
        d = signals.compute_delta(new, {"statements": a})
        self.assertTrue(any(c["metric"] == "statements.new_quarter" for c in d["fundamental_changes"]))
        self.assertEqual(signals.compute_delta(new, {"financial_health": {}})["status"], "unchanged")   # old snapshot without statements

    def test_model_never_gets_raw_statements(self):
        e = signals.compact_evidence({"statements": stm(), "company": {}, "market": {}})
        self.assertNotIn("statements", e)
        self.assertIn("quarterly_trend", e["derived"])


class SizingTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_over_max_flag(self):
        r = ps(100, None, None, None, value=7000, target_amount=5000, max_amount=6000, weight=0.07)
        self.assertEqual(r["status"], "over_max")
        self.assertEqual(r["sizing"]["room_to_max"], -1000)
        self.assertEqual(r["sizing"]["room_to_target"], -2000)
        self.assertEqual(r["sizing"]["pct_of_portfolio"], 7.0)

    def test_room_left(self):
        r = ps(100, None, None, None, value=3200, target_amount=5000, max_amount=6000)
        self.assertEqual(r["status"], "on_plan")
        self.assertEqual((r["sizing"]["room_to_target"], r["sizing"]["room_to_max"]), (1800, 2800))
        self.assertIsNone(ps(100, 90, 130, 85)["sizing"])

    def test_not_held_yet(self):
        r = ps(100, None, None, None, value=0, target_amount=5000)
        self.assertEqual(r["sizing"]["room_to_target"], 5000)
        self.assertFalse(r["sizing"]["over_max"])

    def test_stop_outranks_over_max(self):
        self.assertEqual(ps(80, None, None, 85, value=9000, max_amount=6000)["status"], "stop_breached")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()




===== workbench_v2/tests/test_security.py =====

import unittest

from app import security as s


class SecurityTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_host_check_blocks_rebinding(self):
        for ok in ("127.0.0.1:8000", "localhost:8000", "localhost", "[::1]:8000"):
            self.assertTrue(s.host_ok(ok), ok)
        for bad in ("evil.example:8000", "127.0.0.1.evil.example", "", None):
            self.assertFalse(s.host_ok(bad), bad)

    def test_origin_check_blocks_cross_site_writes(self):
        self.assertTrue(s.origin_ok("POST", "http://127.0.0.1:8000", "127.0.0.1:8000"))
        self.assertTrue(s.origin_ok("POST", None, "127.0.0.1:8000"))                    # curl / scripts
        self.assertTrue(s.origin_ok("GET", "http://evil.example", "127.0.0.1:8000"))    # reads are not state-changing
        self.assertFalse(s.origin_ok("POST", "http://evil.example", "127.0.0.1:8000"))
        self.assertFalse(s.origin_ok("DELETE", "null", "127.0.0.1:8000"))
        self.assertFalse(s.origin_ok("PUT", "http://localhost:3000", "localhost:8000"))

    def test_extra_hosts_from_env(self):
        import os
        os.environ["ALLOWED_HOSTS"] = "mybox.lan"
        try:
            self.assertTrue(s.host_ok("mybox.lan:8000"))
        finally:
            del os.environ["ALLOWED_HOSTS"]
        self.assertFalse(s.host_ok("mybox.lan:8000"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

===== workbench_v2/tests/test_valuation.py =====

import copy
import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from app.core.valuation import Assumptions,calculate
from app.api import valuation as api

@pytest.fixture
def data():
    return dict(ticker='TEST',retrieved_at='2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00',quote=dict(value=50,currency='USD'),anchors=[dict(id='annual:2026-01-01',value=2,currency='USD',period_end='2026-01-01',category='historical_reported')])
def assumptions(**changes):
    return Assumptions(**(dict(anchor_id='annual:2026-01-01',years=3,required_return=.1,growth=.1,exit_pe=20,growth_spread=.05,pe_spread=.2,acknowledge_basis=True)|changes))
def test_formula_and_reverse(data):
    r=calculate(data,assumptions());years=r['growth_years'];expected=2*1.1**years*20
    assert r['scenarios'][1]['future_price']==pytest.approx(expected)
    assert r['scenarios'][1]['discounted_terminal']==pytest.approx(expected/1.1**3)
    assert 2*(1+r['reverse']['required_eps_growth'])**years*20==pytest.approx(50*1.1**3)
    assert r['scenarios'][0]['future_price']<expected<r['scenarios'][2]['future_price']
@pytest.mark.parametrize('value',[None,0,-2])
def test_missing_or_loss_eps(data,value):
    data['anchors'][0]['value']=value
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):calculate(data,assumptions())
def test_currency_mismatch(data):
    data['anchors'][0]['currency']='CAD'
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='currencies'):calculate(data,assumptions())
def test_missing_quote(data):
    data['quote']['value']=None
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='price'):calculate(data,assumptions())
def test_no_ack(data):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='Confirm'):calculate(data,assumptions(acknowledge_basis=False))
def test_growth_boundary(data):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):calculate(data,assumptions(growth=-.8,growth_spread=.3))
@pytest.mark.parametrize('changes',[{'growth':float('nan')},{'exit_pe':float('inf')},{'years':0},{'pe_spread':1}])
def test_invalid_assumptions(changes):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):assumptions(**changes)
def test_no_mutation(data):
    old=copy.deepcopy(data);calculate(data,assumptions());assert data==old

def test_api_append_only_and_offline_replay(tmp_path,monkeypatch,data):
    monkeypatch.setattr(api,'DB',tmp_path/'valuation.db');monkeypatch.setattr(api,'fetch',lambda _,**k:data)
    app=FastAPI();app.include_router(api.router);client=TestClient(app)
    snap=client.post('/api/v1/valuation/TEST/fetch').json()
    req=dict(data_id=snap['id'],assumptions=assumptions().model_dump())
    one=client.post('/api/v1/valuation/calculate',json=req);two=client.post('/api/v1/valuation/calculate',json=req)
    assert one.status_code==two.status_code==200
    assert one.json()['id']!=two.json()['id']
    assert one.json()['payload']['result']==two.json()['payload']['result']
    assert client.get('/api/v1/valuation/snapshots/'+snap['id']).json()['payload']==data   # data snapshot unchanged
    assert len(client.get('/api/v1/valuation/TEST/history').json())==3
    req['data_id']=one.json()['id']
    assert client.post('/api/v1/valuation/calculate',json=req).status_code==422
    assert client.get('/api/v1/valuation/snapshots/missing').status_code==404

def test_provider_mapping_and_consensus_currency(monkeypatch):
    import pandas as pd
    import yfinance
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.adapters.valuation_adapter import fetch,OpenBBAdapter
    def call(operation,fn):
        rows=[dict(last_price=50,currency='USD')] if operation=='quote' else [dict(period_ending='2026-01-31',diluted_earnings_per_share=2.5,total_revenue=1000)]
        return SimpleNamespace(results=rows,provider='yfinance',warnings=None,extra=None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(OpenBBAdapter,'_call',staticmethod(call))
    class Stock:
        def get_info(self): return dict(currency='USD',financialCurrency='USD',regularMarketPrice=50,regularMarketTime=1791576000)
        def get_earnings_estimate(self): return pd.DataFrame([dict(avg=3,currency='USD',numberOfAnalysts=10)],index=['0y'])
        get_revenue_estimate=get_earnings_estimate
    monkeypatch.setattr(yfinance,'Ticker',lambda _:Stock())
    d=fetch('TEST')
    assert d['anchors'][0]['value']==2.5
    assert d['historical'][0]['revenue']==1000
    assert d['quote']['observed_at']=='2026-10-09T20:00:00+00:00'
    assert len(d['estimates'])==2
    assert d['estimates'][0]['published_at'] is None
    assert d['estimates'][0]['period_end'] is None
    assert all(x['category']=='historical_reported' for x in d['anchors'])

def test_provider_failure_does_not_invent(monkeypatch):
    import yfinance
    from app.adapters.valuation_adapter import fetch,OpenBBAdapter
    def fail(*a,**k): raise RuntimeError('offline test')
    monkeypatch.setattr(OpenBBAdapter,'_call',staticmethod(fail))
    monkeypatch.setattr(yfinance,'Ticker',fail)
    d=fetch('TEST')
    assert d['quote']['value'] is None and d['anchors']==[] and d['estimates']==[]
    assert any('offline test' in w for w in d['warnings'])

from datetime import date
from app.core.valuation import EbitdaAssumptions,anniversary,consensus_bridge
from app.core.valuation_context import historical_pe

def ev_assumptions(**changes):
    return EbitdaAssumptions(**(dict(method='ev_ebitda',anchor_id='revenue:2026-01-01',years=2,required_return=.1,
        growth=.1,growth_spread=.05,margin=.2,margin_spread=.05,exit_multiple=15,multiple_spread=.1,
        target_net_debt=100,target_shares=10,other_claims=20,balance_assumption='user_forecast',acknowledge_basis=True)|changes))

@pytest.fixture
def ev_data(data):
    data['revenue_anchors']=[dict(id='revenue:2026-01-01',value=1000,currency='USD',period_end='2026-01-01')]
    data['market_baseline']=dict(currency='USD',net_debt=100,shares=10)
    return data

def test_calendar_anniversary():
    assert anniversary(date(2026,10,10),2)==date(2028,10,10)
    assert anniversary(date(2024,2,29),1)==date(2025,2,28)

def test_ev_equity_bridge_and_reverse(ev_data):
    a=ev_assumptions();r=calculate(ev_data,a);b=r['scenarios'][1]
    assert b['revenue']==pytest.approx(1000*1.1**r['growth_years'])
    assert b['ebitda']==pytest.approx(b['revenue']*.2)
    assert b['future_price']==pytest.approx((b['ebitda']*15-100-20)/10)
    needed=r['reverse']['required_ebitda']
    assert (needed*15-100-20)/10==pytest.approx(50*1.1**2)
    assert r['reverse']['required_margin']*b['revenue']==pytest.approx(needed)
    assert b['ebitda']*r['reverse']['required_exit_multiple']-120==pytest.approx(50*1.1**2*10)
    assert r['required_exit_price']==pytest.approx(60.5)

def test_ev_net_cash_and_limited_equity(ev_data):
    cash=calculate(ev_data,ev_assumptions(target_net_debt=-100))
    debt=calculate(ev_data,ev_assumptions(target_net_debt=100))
    assert cash['scenarios'][1]['future_price']-debt['scenarios'][1]['future_price']==pytest.approx(20)
    loss=calculate(ev_data,ev_assumptions(target_net_debt=1e9))['scenarios'][1]
    assert loss['future_price']==0 and loss['price_cagr']==-1 and loss['equity_floor_applied']

@pytest.mark.parametrize('changes',[dict(margin=.02,margin_spread=.05),dict(margin=.95,margin_spread=.1),dict(balance_assumption='hold_current_constant',target_shares=11)])
def test_ev_invalid_basis(ev_data,changes):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):calculate(ev_data,ev_assumptions(**changes))

def test_ev_fixed_balance_is_explicit(ev_data):
    assert calculate(ev_data,ev_assumptions(balance_assumption='hold_current_constant'))['assumptions']['balance_assumption']=='hold_current_constant'
    ev_data['market_baseline']['net_debt']=None
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):calculate(ev_data,ev_assumptions(balance_assumption='hold_current_constant'))

def test_historical_pe_no_future_price_or_negative_eps():
    rows=[dict(period_end='2025-12-31',diluted_eps=2,currency='USD'),dict(period_end='2024-12-31',diluted_eps=-1,currency='USD')]
    prices=[dict(date='2025-12-30',close=40),dict(date='2026-01-01',close=1000),dict(date='2024-12-31',close=20)]
    r=historical_pe(rows,prices,'USD');assert r['rows'][0]['pe']==20
    assert r['rows'][0]['price_date']=='2025-12-30'
    assert r['rows'][1]['pe'] is None and r['count']==1
    assert historical_pe(rows,prices,'CAD')['count']==0
    assert historical_pe(rows,[dict(date='2025-12-01',close=40)],'USD')['count']==0

def test_consensus_bridge_not_quarter_annualization(data):
    data['estimates']=[dict(metric='eps',period='0q',avg=1,currency='USD'),dict(metric='eps',period='+1y',avg=4,currency='USD')]
    r=consensus_bridge(data,20,.1)
    assert len(r['rows'])==1
    assert [x['price'] for x in r['rows'][0]['prices']]==[72,80,88]
    assert 'price_cagr' not in r['rows'][0]['prices'][0]
    data['estimates'][1]['currency']='CAD'
    assert 'error' in consensus_bridge(data,20,.1)['rows'][0]

def test_reference_enrichment_with_stubbed_history(monkeypatch):
    import pandas as pd
    from app.adapters.valuation_references import enrich
    class Stock:
        def history(self,**kwargs):
            assert kwargs['auto_adjust'] is False
            return pd.DataFrame({'Close':[50]},index=pd.to_datetime(['2025-12-31']))
    d=dict(ticker='TEST',retrieved_at='2026-10-10T00:00:00+00:00',quote=dict(value=60,currency='USD'),estimates=[],historical=[dict(period_end='2025-12-31',currency='USD',diluted_eps=2,revenue=1000,source='fixture')],anchors=[],revenue_anchors=[],warnings=[],raw={},methods={})
    info=dict(financialCurrency='USD',totalCash=10,totalDebt=50,sharesOutstanding=100,targetMeanPrice=70,trailingPE=30,forwardPE=20)
    r=enrich(d,Stock(),info)
    assert r['historical_pe']['rows'][0]['pe']==25
    assert r['market_baseline']['net_debt']==40
    assert r['analyst_targets']['mean']==70 and r['analyst_targets']['horizon'] is None
    assert r['current_multiples']['forward_pe']==20

===== workbench_v2/tests/test_signals.py =====

import unittest
from app.core import signals as s


def ev(price=100, pe=20, gm=0.40, news=()):
    return {"market": {"current_price": price},
            "valuation": {"pe_ttm": pe, "free_cash_flow_yield": 0.04 * 100 / price},
            "financial_health": {"gross_margin": gm, "revenue_growth": 0.08},
            "analyst_consensus": {"target_consensus": 120},
            "recent_news": [{"title": t, "date": "2026-09-01"} for t in news]}


class SignalTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_initial(self):
        d = s.compute_delta(ev(), None)
        self.assertEqual(s.classify(d)[0], "material")

    def test_price_rally_is_not_a_fundamental_change(self):
        # price +20%, P/E +20% (earnings unchanged), FCF yield falls with price
        d = s.compute_delta(ev(price=120, pe=24), ev())
        self.assertEqual(d["valuation_changes"], [])
        self.assertTrue(d["price_driven_valuation"])
        self.assertEqual(s.classify(d)[0], "none")

    def test_multiple_moves_beyond_price_is_flagged(self):
        d = s.compute_delta(ev(price=100, pe=26), ev())  # price flat, P/E +30%
        self.assertEqual(s.classify(d)[0], "material")

    def test_margin_change_minor_vs_material(self):
        self.assertEqual(s.classify(s.compute_delta(ev(gm=0.415), ev()))[0], "minor")
        self.assertEqual(s.classify(s.compute_delta(ev(gm=0.30), ev()))[0], "material")

    def test_news_keywords(self):
        d = s.compute_delta(ev(news=["Company raises guidance for FY27"]), ev())
        self.assertEqual(s.classify(d)[0], "material")
        d = s.compute_delta(ev(news=["公司宣布启动股份回购"]), ev())
        self.assertEqual(s.classify(d)[0], "minor")
        d = s.compute_delta(ev(news=["Stock drifts on quiet day"]), ev())
        self.assertEqual(s.classify(d)[0], "none")

    def test_fingerprint_ignores_price_and_news(self):
        self.assertEqual(s.fingerprint(ev(price=100)), s.fingerprint(ev(price=137, news=["x"])))
        self.assertNotEqual(s.fingerprint(ev(gm=0.40)), s.fingerprint(ev(gm=0.35)))

    def test_compact_drops_nulls(self):
        e = ev(); e["company"] = {"name": "X", "employees": 5, "sector": None, "long_description": "a" * 900}
        c = s.compact_evidence(e)
        self.assertNotIn("sector", c["company"]); self.assertEqual(len(c["company"]["long_description"]), 500)


class BasisTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_stale_statements_flagged(self):
        from datetime import datetime, timezone
        e = {"financial_health": {"income_statement_latest": {"period_ending": "2025-08-28", "fiscal_year": 2025}}}
        now = datetime(2026, 10, 4, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        self.assertTrue(s.evidence_basis(e, now)["statements"]["stale"])
        self.assertTrue(any("days old" in g for g in s.data_gaps(e, now)))
        e["financial_health"]["income_statement_latest"]["period_ending"] = "2026-06-30"
        self.assertFalse(s.evidence_basis(e, now)["statements"]["stale"])

    def test_derived_metrics_and_cyclical(self):
        e = {"company": {"industry": "Semiconductors"}, "market": {"market_cap": 1000.0},
             "valuation": {"pe_ttm": 10.0, "forward_pe": 5.0, "enterprise_value": 900.0},
             "financial_health": {"free_cash_flow": 100.0}}
        d = s.derived_metrics(e)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(d["fcf_yield"]["value"], 0.1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(d["consensus_implied_eps_change"]["value"], 1.0)   # EPS expected to double
        self.assertEqual(d["ev_to_fcf"]["value"], 9.0)
        self.assertIn("cyclical_flag", d)
        e["valuation"]["forward_pe"] = 20.0
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s.derived_metrics(e)["consensus_implied_eps_change"]["value"], -0.5)  # EPS expected to halve

    def test_earnings_dislocation_flag(self):
        e = {"valuation": {"pe_ttm": 27.8, "forward_pe": 12.07}}
        d = s.derived_metrics(e)
        self.assertEqual(d["earnings_dislocation"]["kind"], "recovery_expected")
        e = {"valuation": {"pe_ttm": 20.0, "forward_pe": 17.0}}
        self.assertNotIn("earnings_dislocation", s.derived_metrics(e))
        e = {"valuation": {"pe_ttm": 10.0, "forward_pe": 20.0}}
        self.assertEqual(s.derived_metrics(e)["earnings_dislocation"]["kind"], "decline_expected")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()




===== workbench_v2/tests/test_service.py =====

"""Integration test: fake evidence adapter + mock provider + in-memory SQLite.
Run: python -m pytest -q   (needs the packages in requirements.txt, not OpenBB)."""
import copy
import os
os.environ["DATABASE_URL"] = "sqlite://"
os.environ["AI_PROVIDER"] = "mock"

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app import database, models, service
from app.ai.provider import MockProvider

BASE = {"company": {"symbol": "TST", "name": "Test Co"},
        "market": {"current_price": 100.0},
        "valuation": {"pe_ttm": 20.0, "free_cash_flow_yield": 0.04},
        "financial_health": {"gross_margin": 0.40, "revenue_growth": 0.08},
        "analyst_consensus": {"target_consensus": 120.0},
        "recent_news": [{"title": "Quiet day", "date": "2026-09-01"}]}


class FakeAdapter:
    def __init__(self):
        self.ev = copy.deepcopy(BASE)

    def get_equity_research_snapshot(self, ticker):
        return copy.deepcopy(self.ev), {}


@pytest.fixture()
def session():
    eng = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    database.Base.metadata.create_all(eng)
    s = sessionmaker(bind=eng)()
    yield s
    s.close()


def test_lifecycle(session):
    ad, ai = FakeAdapter(), MockProvider()
    r1 = service.refresh(session, "tst", adapter=ad, provider=ai)
    assert r1.kind == "baseline" and len(r1.state["assumptions"]) == 3

    # price rally only -> deterministic check, no LLM
    ad.ev["market"]["current_price"] = 120.0
    ad.ev["valuation"]["pe_ttm"] = 24.0
    ad.ev["valuation"]["free_cash_flow_yield"] = 0.04 * 100 / 120
    r2 = service.refresh(session, "tst", adapter=ad, provider=ai)
    assert r2.kind == "check" and r2.provider == "none"

    # repeated heartbeat collapses into the same row
    r3 = service.refresh(session, "tst", adapter=ad, provider=ai)
    assert r3.id == r2.id

    # real fundamental change -> LLM update, anchored on the baseline
    ad.ev["financial_health"]["gross_margin"] = 0.30
    r4 = service.refresh(session, "tst", adapter=ad, provider=ai)
    assert r4.kind == "update" and r4.level == "material" and r4.prev_review_id == r1.id

    # the update is now the anchor, so identical inputs mean "nothing changed": a free check, no model call
    r5 = service.refresh(session, "tst", adapter=ad, provider=ai)
    assert r5.kind == "check" and r5.provider == "none" and r5.prev_review_id == r4.id




===== workbench_v2/tests/test_valuation_ctx.py =====

import unittest
from app.core.valuation_context import consensus_period_end, earnings_quality_flags, status_quo, to_date
from app.core.valuation import Assumptions, EbitdaAssumptions, calculate


def data():
    return dict(retrieved_at='2026-10-11T00:00:00+00:00', quote=dict(value=111.35, currency='USD'),
                anchors=[dict(id='ttm', value=2.9, currency='USD', period_end='2026-09-21'),
                         dict(id='annual:2026-01-31', value=2.73, currency='USD', period_end='2026-01-31')],
                estimates=[dict(metric='eps', period='0y', avg=2.89), dict(metric='eps', period='+1y', avg=3.22)],
                historical=[], current_multiples=dict(forward_pe=34.5, trailing_eps=2.9))


def pe(anchor, **kw):
    base = dict(anchor_id=anchor, years=3, required_return=.1, growth=.114, exit_pe=34.5, growth_spread=.03, pe_spread=.1, acknowledge_basis=True)
    return Assumptions(**{**base, **kw})


class Ctx(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_period_end(self):
        self.assertEqual(consensus_period_end({'nextFiscalYearEnd': 1801440000}, '0y'), '2027-02-01')
        self.assertEqual(consensus_period_end({'nextFiscalYearEnd': 1801440000}, '+1y'), '2028-02-01')
        self.assertIsNone(consensus_period_end({}, '0y'))
        self.assertIsNone(to_date('bad'))

    def test_one_off_flag(self):
        rows = [dict(period_end=f'202{y}-12-31', revenue=100, net_income=n) for y, n in ((6, 40), (5, 10), (4, 9), (3, 10))]
        self.assertEqual(len(earnings_quality_flags(rows)), 1)
        rows[0]['net_income'] = 10
        self.assertEqual(earnings_quality_flags(rows), [])

    def test_status_quo(self):
        d = data(); d['historical'] = []
        q = status_quo(d)
        self.assertEqual(q['pe']['growth'], 11.4)
        self.assertEqual(q['pe']['anchor_id'], 'ttm'); self.assertAlmostEqual(q['pe']['exit_pe'], 111.35 / 2.9, 1)   # price ÷ the same starting value

    def test_growth_counted_for_the_holding_period_only(self):
        a = calculate(data(), pe('annual:2026-01-31')); t = calculate(data(), pe('ttm'))
        self.assertEqual(a['growth_years'], 3); self.assertEqual(t['growth_years'], 3)

    def test_same_basis_multiple_adds_nothing(self):
        """Hold today's multiple on the starting value and grow at g: the annual return is g for every starting point."""
        d = data(); d['anchors'].append(dict(id='consensus:+1y', value=3.22, currency='USD', period_end='2028-02-01', category='market_consensus'))
        for anchor, value in (('ttm', 2.9), ('annual:2026-01-31', 2.73), ('consensus:+1y', 3.22)):
            r = calculate(d, pe(anchor, growth=.08, exit_pe=111.35 / value, growth_spread=0.0, pe_spread=0.0))
            base = r['scenarios'][1]
            self.assertAlmostEqual(base['price_cagr'], .08, places=9)
            self.assertAlmostEqual(base['earnings_effect'], .08, places=9); self.assertAlmostEqual(base['multiple_effect'], 0, places=9)

    def test_stale_annual_anchor_warns(self):
        d = data(); d['retrieved_at'] = '2027-10-11T00:00:00+00:00'
        r = calculate(d, pe('annual:2026-01-31', exit_pe=111.35 / 2.73))
        self.assertTrue(any(c['level'] == 'warn' and 'months ago' in c['text'] for c in r['checks']))

    def test_growth_factor_fractional_tail(self):
        from app.core.valuation import growth_factor
        self.assertAlmostEqual(growth_factor(.2, .05, 1.01), 1.2 * 1.05 ** .01)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(growth_factor(.1, .1, 2.5), 1.1 ** 2.5)

    def test_status_quo_prefers_analyst_year(self):
        d = data(); d['anchors'] = [dict(id='ttm', value=3.12, currency='USD'), dict(id='consensus:0y', value=6.72, currency='USD'), dict(id='consensus:+1y', value=7.19, currency='USD')]
        d['quote']['value'] = 86.73
        q = status_quo(d)['pe']
        self.assertEqual(q['anchor_id'], 'consensus:+1y'); self.assertAlmostEqual(q['exit_pe'], 12.1, 1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(q['multiple_by_anchor']['ttm'], 27.8, 1)

    def test_checks_warn(self):
        r = calculate(data(), pe('ttm', exit_pe=60))
        self.assertTrue(any(c['level'] == 'warn' and 'starting point' in c['text'] for c in r['checks']))
        r = calculate(data(), pe('ttm', growth=.4))
        self.assertTrue(any('analyst' in c['text'] for c in r['checks']))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()


===== workbench_v2/tests/test_portfolio.py =====

import unittest
from app import portfolio as p

TH = "方向 代码 名称 订单价格 订单数量 订单金额 交易状态 已成交@均价 下单时间 类型 期限 盘前竞价 时段 触发价 允许开仓 市场 币种 订单来源 成交数量 成交价格 成交金额 成交时间 市场 币种 对手经纪 备注 佣金 平台使用费 交收费 证监会规费 交易活动费 印花税 交易费 证监会征费 财汇局征费 综合审计跟踪监管费 清算费 合计费用".split()


def trade(side, code, status, mkt, cur, qty="", price="", when="", fee=""):
    r = [""] * len(TH)
    r[0], r[1], r[2], r[6], r[15], r[16] = side, code, "n", status, mkt, cur
    r[18], r[19], r[20], r[21], r[22], r[23], r[-1] = qty, price, "1", when, mkt, cur, fee
    return "\t".join(r)


class PortfolioTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_trades_only_filled(self):
        t = "\t".join(TH) + "\n" + "\n".join([
            trade("买入", "TSM", "全部成交", "美股", "USD", "12", "395.00", "2026/07/17 15:12:19 (美东)", "2.03"),
            trade("卖出", "TGT", "等待成交", "美股", "USD"),
            trade("买入", "TSLA", "已撤单", "美股", "USD"),
            trade("买入", "1432", "部成已撤", "港股", "HKD", "6,000", "0.35", "2025/10/03 10:00:00 (香港)", "34.38")])
        r = p.parse_trades(t)
        self.assertEqual([x["code"] for x in r], ["TSM", "1432"])
        self.assertEqual(r[1]["quantity"], 6000.0); self.assertEqual(r[1]["symbol"], "1432.HK"); self.assertEqual(r[1]["currency"], "HKD")
        self.assertTrue(r[0]["executed_at"].startswith("2026-07-17T19:12:19"))       # EDT -> UTC
        self.assertEqual(p.parse_trades(t)[0]["key"], r[0]["key"])                  # stable de-dup key

    def test_trade_report(self):
        t = "\t".join(TH) + "\n" + "\n".join([
            trade("买入", "TLT", "全部成交", "美股", "USD", "10", "90", "2026/07/17 15:12:19 (美东)"),
            trade("买入", "TLT", "全部成交", "美股", "USD", "10", "90", "bad-time"),
            trade("买入", "TSLA", "已撤单", "美股", "USD")])
        r, rep = p.parse_trades_report(t)
        self.assertEqual((rep["rows"], rep["unfilled"], rep["bad_row"], rep["symbols"]), (3, 1, 1, ["TLT"]))

    def test_share_class_symbols(self):
        self.assertEqual(p.norm_symbol("BRK.B"), "BRK-B"); self.assertEqual(p.norm_symbol("bf.b"), "BF-B")
        self.assertEqual(p.norm_symbol("0700.HK"), "0700.HK"); self.assertEqual(p.symbol_of("BRK.B", "USD"), "BRK-B")
        self.assertEqual(p.symbol_of("C09", "SGD"), "C09.SI")

    def test_holdings(self):
        t = "代码\t名称\t现价\t今日盈亏\t摊薄成本价\t持仓盈亏\t盈亏比例\t持有数量\t市值\t持仓占比\nABEV\tAmbev SA\t2.860\t60.00\t1.007\t2,778.98\t184.01%\t1,500\t4,290.00\t0.73%\nTLT\tiShares 20+ Year Treasury Bond ETF\t90\t0\t95\t-50\t-5%\t10\t900\t0.15%"
        r = p.parse_holdings(t)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(r[0]["weight"], 0.0073); self.assertEqual(r[0]["quantity"], 1500.0)
        self.assertEqual([x["kind"] for x in r], ["stock", "etf"])

    def test_csv_and_gbk(self):
        raw = "代码,名称,现价,摊薄成本价,持仓盈亏,盈亏比例,持有数量,市值,持仓占比\n9961,携程集团-S,500,300,1,10%,50,25000,1%".encode("gbk")
        r = p.parse_holdings(p.decode(raw))
        self.assertEqual(r[0]["symbol"], "9961.HK")

    def test_singapore_file(self):
        hdr = "名称\t代码\t市值\t持有数量\t可用数量\t现价\t摊薄成本价\t今日盈亏\t持仓盈亏\t盈亏比例\t持仓占比\t币种\t今日成交额\tDelta"
        row = "栢能集团有限公司\tPCT\t6,760.00\t2,000\t2,000\t3.380\t2.69\t0.00\t1,380.00\t25.65%\t0.90%\tSGD\t0.00\t2000.00"
        r = p.parse_holdings(hdr + "\n" + row)
        self.assertEqual((r[0]["symbol"], r[0]["currency"], r[0]["quantity"]), ("PCT.SI", "SGD", 2000.0))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(r[0]["weight"], 0.009); self.assertAlmostEqual(r[0]["pnl_pct"], 0.2565)

    def test_missing_column(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            p.parse_holdings("代码,名称\nA,b")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
